arXivSTq-fin.CP 2608.29025 ★★★★★

Deep Hedging Under Realistic Market Frictions: A Regime-Conditional Empirical Study of Dynamic Option Hedging on Bitcoin Options

실제 BTC 옵션 데이터에서 딥헤징이 고전 헤지에 전부 졌다.

Deep hedging loses to classical hedges on five years of real BTC options.

Sheryan Kumar

  • 딥헤징 우위는 시뮬레이션과 무마찰 벤치마크에서만 나왔다
  • LSTM·MLP를 CVaR 손실로 학습해 고전 헤지 셋과 붙였다
  • 딥헤징 3종이 모든 지표에서 고전 벤치마크에 밀렸다
  • Whalley-Wilmott는 거래를 8배 줄여 에피소드당 $1.79 절감
방법
블랙-숄즈 델타, Leland 비용조정 헤지, Whalley-Wilmott 무거래 밴드를 LSTM·피드포워드 딥헤징(CVaR 손실, 거래빈도 페널티)과 5bp 동일 거래비용에서 비교했다.
데이터
Deribit의 2020~2024년 BTC 옵션 5년치를 쓰고 2023년 9월~2024년 12월 11,546개 에피소드로 검증했다.
시사점
옵션 델타헤지 비용을 줄이려면 딥헤징 대신 Whalley-Wilmott 무거래 밴드를 시간당 리밸런싱 위에 얹는 게 낫다.
적용도 4/5
Deribit 옵션 데이터는 공개 API와 상용 아카이브로 구할 수 있고 세 벤치마크 모두 규칙이 명시돼 바로 백테스트할 수 있다. 딥헤징 무용론이라는 반증 결과라 가점을 줬다.
  • Prior wins came from simulations and frictionless baselines
  • LSTM and MLP trained on CVaR loss, all at 5bp costs
  • No deep hedging model beat any classical benchmark
  • Whalley-Wilmott saved $1.79 per episode, traded 8x less
Method
Black-Scholes delta, Leland's cost-adjusted hedge and the Whalley-Wilmott no-trade band race an LSTM and a feedforward net trained on CVaR loss with a trading-frequency penalty.
Data
Deribit BTC options, 2020-2024, tested on 11,546 episodes from September 2023 to December 2024.
Use
Bolt a Whalley-Wilmott no-trade band onto hourly delta hedging to cut costs; skip the neural net.
Score 4/5
Deribit option data is available via public API and commercial archives, and all three classical rules are fully specified, so this backtests as written; the deep-hedging refutation earns a bonus.
deep hedgingtransaction costsWhalley-WilmottBitcoin optionsCVaRregime dependence
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTClassical option-hedging methods like Black-Scholes delta assume constant, free rebalancing, which real markets don't allow. Deep hedging trains a neural network to handle these frictions directly, and prior work reports strong results. But those comparisons usually pit deep hedging against a frictionless classical baseline on simulated price data. That's not a fair fight, and it leaves open whether the advantage is real. We test this using five years of actual BTC options data from Deribit (2020-2024), comparing Black-Scholes delta, Leland's cost-adjusted hedge, and the Whalley-Wilmott no-trade band against three deep hedging setups: an LSTM and a feedforward network, each trained with a CVaR loss and, in some runs, a penalty for trading too often. All six strategies face the same 5 basis point transaction cost. On 11,546 test episodes from September 2023 to December 2024, Whalley-Wilmott cuts transaction costs significantly versus hourly rebalancing, saving $1.79 per episode against plain BS delta (95% CI [-2.21, -1.39], p < 0.0001) by trading about eight times less often. Its P&L and tail-risk numbers are better too, though not quite significant at this sample size. None of the three deep hedging models beat any classical benchmark on any metric, and all kept trading almost every hour regardless of penalty weight, a twenty-fold range barely moved the needle. A calmer validation period shows Whalley-Wilmott's P&L edge shrinks or disappears there while its cost edge holds, so the result depends on market regime. We think the likely causes are a small training set and the lack of any built-in mechanism for sitting still in the architectures tested. Not a flashy result, but an honest one: a real-data check on a claim mostly supported by simulations so far.

SSRNAPV26p 7374498 ★★★★★

Calendar Effects of Asset Pricing Factors

롱숏 팩터 프리미엄은 주식이 잘 오르는 달력 구간에서 오히려 줄어든다

Factor premia shrink in exactly the calendar windows where the market rallies.

Jiawei Fan, Sida Li, Wanyu Miao · , Brandeis University and Brandeis University - International Business School

  • 미국 주식의 달력 효과는 팩터에도 같게 적용될까
  • 롱숏 팩터 153개를 복제해 구간별 수익을 갈랐다
  • 야간 -2.01bp 대 주간 5.45bp, 월요일 5.77bp 대 금요일 1.24bp
  • 1월·월말·거시지표 발표 구간에서도 팩터 수익이 낮았다
방법
널리 알려진 롱숏 팩터 153개를 복제한 뒤 야간/주간, 요일, 1월, 월말, 거시지표 발표 창으로 수익을 쪼개 비교한다.
데이터
미국 주식 팩터 수익률을 쓰고 표본 밖 구간에서도 같은 패턴이 남는지 확인했다.
시사점
팩터 포트폴리오를 야간에 줄이고 주간에 실으며, 1월·월말·발표일 노출을 낮추는 타이밍 규칙으로 쓴다.
적용도 4/5
일중·야간 수익 분해와 표준 팩터 정의만 있으면 재현되니 데이터 확보는 쉬운 편이고, 팩터별 가중치와 체결 규칙은 직접 정해야 해 4점이다.
  • Calendar effects are documented for the market, not for factors
  • The authors replicate 153 long-short factors and split returns by window
  • Factors lose 2.01 bps overnight, earn 5.45 bps intraday
  • Monday 5.77 bps vs Friday 1.24 bps; weak in January and at month turn
Method
Replicate 153 long-short factors, then partition returns by overnight/intraday, weekday, January, turn-of-month, and macro announcement windows.
Data
U.S. equity factor returns, with the reversed patterns holding out of sample and strengthening when news sentiment is high.
Use
Time factor exposure: cut it overnight and on Fridays, add intraday and on Mondays, trim around January, month turn, and macro prints.
Score 4/5
Score 4 — intraday/overnight splits and standard factor definitions are readily available data, but factor weights, sizing, and execution rules are left to you.
calendar effectslong-short factorsovernight returnsfactor timingsentimentmacro announcements
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTU.S. equity returns are known to be higher overnight, on Fridays, in January, around the turn of the month, and during macroeconomic announcement windows. We replicate 153 long-short factors and document the mirror image of these patterns: factor premia are systematically lower in exactly the same periods. On average, a factor loses 2.01 bps overnight but earns 5.45 bps intraday; it earns 5.77 bps on Mondays versus 1.24 bps on Fridays; and its returns are significantly lower in January, at the turn of the month, and around macroeconomic announcements. The reversed calendar effects persist out of sample and strengthen when news sentiment is elevated, consistent with sentiment-driven mispricing.

SSRNAPVMEF11p 7376218 ★★★★★

The Cost of Sector Constraints in Cross-Sectional Momentum: Evidence from the Nifty 500

롱온리 모멘텀에 섹터 상한을 걸면 연 8.59%p 손해다.

Sector caps cost a long-only momentum book 8.59pp of annual CAGR.

Harsh Pandhe

  • 섹터 중립은 관행이지만 롱온리에서는 근거가 약하다
  • 인도 Nifty 500에서 섹터 상한 5개 방식을 무제약과 비교
  • 무제약 CAGR 42.56%, 3종목 상한은 33.97%로 하락
  • 최대낙폭은 -26.44%에서 -26.79%로 오히려 나빠졌다
방법
변동성 조정 모멘텀으로 30종목을 뽑고 섹터 상한 C∈{2,3,4,5}와 섹터 균등 선택을 무제약 기준과 비교했다.
데이터
NSE Nifty 500 시점 정합 구성종목, 2018년 1월 1일부터 2026년 2월 26일까지 10개 연속 아웃오브샘플 구간 1260거래일이다.
시사점
롱온리 모멘텀을 돌린다면 섹터 상한을 리스크 관리로 착각하지 말고 상한 강도별 비용을 직접 측정해 보라.
적용도 4/5
규칙이 명확하고 Nifty 500 시세와 섹터 분류는 구하기 쉬워 그대로 재현할 수 있으며 통념을 뒤집는 결과라 +1을 줬다.
  • Sector neutrality is standard practice but untested long-only
  • Five constrained regimes vs unconstrained on Nifty 500
  • Unconstrained CAGR 42.56% and Sharpe 1.728 beat all caps
  • Momentum rank of picks decays from 15.5 to 48.2 under caps
Method
A 30-stock volatility-adjusted momentum screen run under sector caps C∈{2,3,4,5} and a sector-balanced variant, benchmarked against no constraint.
Data
Point-in-time NSE Nifty 500 constituents, Jan 1 2018 to Feb 26 2026, 10 contiguous out-of-sample folds covering 1260 trading days.
Use
Drop or loosen sector caps in long-only momentum, and price the drag explicitly before an institutional mandate imposes one.
Score 4/5
Rules are explicit and Nifty 500 prices plus sector tags are easy to source; +1 for refuting the sector-neutrality default, though caps trade at 42.34 bps round-trip friction that the author already models.
cross-sectional momentumsector constraintslong-only portfolio constructionNifty 500transaction costsfactor dilution
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTInstitutional quantitative equity frameworks routinely mandate sector concentration limits or industry neutrality to eliminate uncompensated macroeconomic sector bets. While developedmarket long-short literature often supports neutralization, recent asset pricing theory [e.g., 6] demonstrates that long-only factor portfolios are systematically impaired by sector constraints because shorting decaying industries is disallowed. We provide an out-of-sample empirical investigation of this hypothesis in the Indian equity market using point-in-time National Stock Exchange (NSE) Nifty 500 constituent data across an 8-year historical sample (January 1, 2018 to February 26, 2026). Evaluating four parametric sector-cap regimes (C ∈ {2, 3, 4, 5}) and one sector-balanced selection regime against an unconstrained benchmark across 10 contiguous out-of-sample evaluation folds (1260 trading days / 5.0 trading years) under realistic transaction costs (42.34 bps round-trip friction including Securities Transaction Tax, stamp duty, exchange levies, and assumed 10 bps execution slippage), we document that all evaluated constrained configurations produce lower realized compound returns and Sharpe ratios than the unconstrained benchmark. An unconstrained 30-stock volatility-adjusted momentum strategy delivers an out-of-sample CAGR of 42.56% and a Sharpe ratio of 1.728. Capping sector allocations to 3 stocks compresses the Herfindahl-Hirschman Index (HHI) from 0.169 to 0.084, but reduces CAGR to 33.97% (a-8.59pp annual drag) and Sharpe to 1.497 (unadjusted bootstrap p = 0.0204; Holm-adjusted bootstrap p = 0.1020; JKM p = 0.0281), with no improvement in maximum drawdown (-26.79% vs.-26.44%). We find evidence consistent with a factor dilution mechanism: sector caps exclude high-conviction momentum leaders in trending macro sectors, replacing them with lower-ranked securities from lagging industries (average selected momentum rank deteriorates from 15.5 to 48.2), which is positively associated with subsequent relative performance drag in Newey-West HAC predictive regressions (t = 2.41, p = 0.018).

arXivTRq-fin.MF 2608.29468 ★★★★★

The Convergence Rate of Stochastic Tracking with Application to Optimal Execution

최적 집행에서 규제화 오차가 O(√ε)로 줄어듦을 증명했다.

Proves regularized optimal execution costs converge at rate O(√ε).

Marcel Nutz, Moritz Voss

  • 체결 스파이크를 없애려면 규제화가 필요한데 그 대가를 몰랐다
  • 목표 추종 문제의 비점근 상한을 베소프 계량으로 유도했다
  • 규제화 비용은 O(√ε), 준최적 전략도 같은 속도다
방법
힐베르트 공간 기법으로 확률적 목표 추종의 이차 문제를 풀고 오비자에바-왕(Obizhaeva-Wang) 모형에 적용한다.
데이터
이론 연구
시사점
VWAP나 재고 목표를 따라가는 집행 알고에서 체결 속도 페널티 ε를 얼마나 키워야 비용이 얼마나 늘어나는지 계산할 때 쓴다.
적용도 3/5
필요한 데이터는 자기 주문과 체결 기록뿐이라 확보가 쉽지만 실제 집행기로 만들려면 충격 파라미터 추정과 스케줄 설계를 따로 해야 해서 3점이다.
  • Smoothing away trading spikes costs money; nobody quantified it
  • Non-asymptotic bounds via a Besov-type modulus of the target
  • Regularization penalty ε costs O(√ε); a closed-form proxy matches it
Method
Hilbert-space characterization of quadratic tracking with absolutely continuous controls, applied to a generalized Obizhaeva-Wang model with random terminal inventory.
Data
theory only
Use
Sizing the trading-rate penalty in a VWAP or inventory-tracking execution algo, with a known cost for the smoothing you buy.
Score 3/5
Scored 3 because the inputs are your own fills and impact estimates — easy to source — but the paper gives a cost bound and a proxy strategy, not a ready execution schedule.
optimal executionObizhaeva-Wangstochastic trackingprice impactconvergence ratetrading-rate penalty
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTWe study the quadratic tracking problem of a general stochastic target process with absolutely continuous controls, with and without terminal constraint. We derive explicit, non-asymptotic upper bounds in terms of a Besov-type modulus of the target. These bounds yield sharp explicit rates that specialize to the square-root order for semimartingale targets. We then apply these results to a generalized Obizhaeva--Wang execution model with random terminal inventory. We first develop a Hilbert-space approach to characterize its optimal strategy, which includes jumps. To avoid such trading spikes, one regularizes the problem by a quadratic trading-rate penalty with coefficient $\varepsilon$. We then show that the regularized optimal execution cost---and therefore the excess price impact cost of the regularized optimal strategy---converges at the sharp rate $O(\sqrt{\varepsilon})$. Since the regularized optimal strategy is not available in closed form, we further construct a nearly optimal strategy which is readily implementable and shares the same approximation rate.

arXivST 2608.29692 ★★★★★

Portfolio Risk Bounds without Cross-Asset Return Covariances: Distributional Fields from Language-Model Representations

뉴스 임베딩만으로 공분산 없이 포트폴리오 분산 상한을 잡는다

Bounds portfolio variance from news embeddings, no cross-asset covariances.

Marcus Gawronsky, Chun-Sung Huang

  • 짧은 고차원 패널에서 자산 간 공분산 추정이 부정확하다
  • 기업별 분포 특성의 Wasserstein-2 거리로 분산 상한을 유도한다
  • 가중 쌍별 완화로 볼록 목적함수를 만들어 배분에 쓴다
  • 52개 종목에서 표본 내 분산 백분위 0.69~1.33위를 기록했다
방법
Qwen3-Embedding-8B가 만든 뉴스 표현을 분포값 특성으로 보고 다기업 Wasserstein-2 산포에서 체계적 분산의 상한을 유도한다
데이터
2018~2022년 52개 기업 패널과 종목별 뉴스 텍스트를 썼고 검증은 표본 내 분산 순위 비교다
시사점
수익률 공분산 추정이 어려운 소형주나 신규 상장 종목 바스켓에서 위험균등(risk parity) 대신 쓸 배분 규칙으로 시험해볼 만하다
적용도 3/5
뉴스 텍스트와 오픈 임베딩 모델은 구하기 어렵지 않지만 진입·청산 규칙이 없고 표본 내 분산 순위만 보고해 배분 기법 수준에 머문다
  • Short high-dimensional panels give unreliable covariance estimates
  • Wasserstein-2 dispersion across firms bounds systematic variance
  • A weighted pairwise relaxation makes the objective convex
  • In 52 firms, the allocation hit the 0.69th-1.33rd variance percentile
Method
Treat firm news embeddings from Qwen3-Embedding-8B as distributions; multi-firm Wasserstein-2 dispersion caps systematic portfolio variance.
Data
52-firm panel, 2018-2022, with firm news text; evaluated by in-sample variance percentile against capped portfolio populations.
Use
Test it as a risk-parity substitute for baskets where return covariances are hard to estimate — small caps, recent IPOs.
Score 3/5
News text and open embedding models are obtainable, but the paper gives no entry/exit rules and reports only in-sample variance ranks.
portfolio risk boundsWasserstein-2 distancelanguage model embeddingscovariance-free allocationnews representations
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTPortfolio risk assessment ordinarily relies on reliable estimates of cross-asset return covariances, which are difficult to obtain in short, high-dimensional panels. We show that firm-level distribution-valued characteristics can instead provide one-sided certificates of portfolio risk. Under maintained links from characteristics to systematic exposures and from exposures to returns, multi-firm Wasserstein-2 dispersion yields a sharp upper bound on systematic portfolio variance and a corresponding bound for standardized returns. A weighted pairwise relaxation produces an objective that is convex under a checkable condition and requires marginal volatility scales but no cross-asset return covariances. With zero firm-specific slack, the common-map scale changes the certified variance reduction but not the normalized allocation, which depends only on observed information geometry. In a 52-firm panel from 2018-2022, an allocation constructed from Qwen3-Embedding-8B news representations lies between the 0.69th and 1.33rd in-sample variance percentiles across four prespecified capped portfolio populations; equal risk weighting lies between the 21.1st and 28.6th percentiles. The lower in-sample variance ranking relative to equal risk also appears across the reported frozen language-model representations. The framework therefore distribution-valued firm information into a coherent risk bound and an implementable allocation rule constructed without cross-asset return covariances.

arXivTRq-fin.MFmath.PRcross-list 2608.30321 ★★★★★

Optimal Block Time for AMM Liquidity Providers under Jump-Diffusion Prices

점프를 넣으면 AMM 유동성 공급 손실에 없어지지 않는 바닥이 생긴다

Jumps put a hard floor under AMM liquidity-provider losses that shorter blocks cannot remove.

Nils Bundi

  • 블록을 짧게 하면 LVR이 사라진다는 통념을 의심한다
  • 기준가를 점프-확산으로 두고 LVR을 두 채널로 분해한다
  • 이더리움 12초에서 471bp/yr 중 125bp는 바닥이다
  • LP 관점 최적 블록타임은 점프 모수와 무관하게 8초 근처다
방법
상수곱 AMM의 LVR을 점프-확산 기준가 아래에서 확산 채널 F(γ/(σ√Δt))와 점프 채널 λV·G(γ;m,δ²)로 분해하고 잔차를 명시적으로 상한 지었다
데이터
이론 연구다. 다만 이더리움 12초 슬롯과 솔라나 400ms 슬롯에 모수를 맞춰 수치를 냈다
시사점
LP 수익 모델에서 수수료 대비 역선택 비용을 잡을 때 블록타임으로 줄일 수 없는 손실 하한을 넣는 용도로 쓴다
적용도 3/5
매매 규칙이 아니라 LP 손익 모델의 부품이라 전략화에 설계가 더 필요하고 온체인 풀·블록 데이터는 공개라 확보 자체는 쉽다
  • Kills the claim that Δt→0 drives LVR to zero
  • Splits constant-product LVR into diffusion and jump channels
  • Ethereum's 12s slot: 471 bp/yr, floor 125 bp/yr
  • LP-optimal block time sits near 8s, invariant to jump params
Method
Models the reference price as a jump-diffusion, then separates the constant-product LVR rate into a Δt-dependent diffusion term F(γ/(σ√Δt)) and a Δt-free jump term λV·G(γ;m,δ²) with a bounded remainder.
Data
Theory only, with numbers calibrated to Ethereum's 12-second slot and Solana's 400 ms slot.
Use
Use the jump-channel floor as the irreducible adverse-selection cost when pricing whether pool fees cover LP risk.
Score 3/5
It is a cost model, not an entry/exit rule, so building a strategy needs real design work; on-chain pool and block data are public, so sourcing is easy.
LVRAMM liquidity provisionjump-diffusionblock timeadverse selectionmarket microstructure
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTLoss-versus-Rebalancing (LVR) is the dominant adverse-selection cost borne by liquidity providers on automated market makers. Under geometric Brownian motion, arbitrage profit scales with the probability of a profitable block, which vanishes as the block time $Δt \to 0$; this is the standing argument for ever-shorter blocks. Modeling the reference price instead as a jump-diffusion, I show that the constant-product LVR rate splits into a diffusion channel carrying the known multiplier $F(γ/(σ\sqrt{Δt}))$ and a jump channel $λV \cdot G(γ;m,δ^2)$ carrying no $Δt$, the two interacting only through an explicitly bounded remainder. The block schedule therefore governs only one channel. For symmetric jump laws the jump channel is moreover an exact lower bound, $\ell(Δt) \ge λV G > 0$, so the rate does not vanish as $Δt \to 0$, and descends slowly, as $\sqrt{Δt}$. At Ethereum's calibrated 12-second slot the rate is 471 bp/yr against a floor of 125, so only three quarters of LP loss is schedule-addressable. At Solana's 400 ms slot the jump channel already dominates. Netting the rate against per-block consensus cost, the LP-side optimal block time is invariant in pool size and in every jump parameter $(λ,m,δ)$: jumps shift the level of LP loss but not the planner's marginal tradeoff. Volatility, the fee tier, and consensus cost set the optimum, near 8 s. However, LVR is only one input to block-time welfare, so this bounds the LP-side contribution rather than settling the design question.
abs PDF
arXivPMSTcs.LG 2608.30446 ★★★★★

End-to-End Neural Shrinkage of Indefinite Pairwise Correlation Matrices for Small-Cap-Inclusive Portfolios

A neural covariance estimator repairs indefinite pairwise correlation matrices for small-cap portfolios.

Christian Bongiorno, Lorenzo Villassero

  • 공통 관측구간을 강제하면 소형주 정보 상당수가 버려진다
  • 쌍별 상관행렬은 부정부호라 마코위츠에 바로 못 넣는다

  • 26년 표본외에서 변동성 -20%, 샤프 +40%

2000~2025년 미국 주식 최대 1,500종목을 확장창 26개 모델로 나눠, 수수료·자금조달·기업행위·시장충격을 넣은 종가 단일가 체결 시뮬레이터에서 검증했다.
시사점
상장 이력이 짧은 종목을 유니버스에서 빼지 않고도 위험모형을 돌리고 싶을 때 공분산 추정 단계를 이걸로 갈아끼우면 된다.
적용도 3/5
데이터는 미국 주식 일별 가격이라 구하기 쉽지만 신호가 아니라 위험모형이라, 매매 규칙을 따로 설계해야 해 3점이다.
  • Common look-backs throw away most small-cap history
  • Pairwise-complete matrices go indefinite, breaking Markowitz
  • Signed-spectrum GRU maps negative eigenvalues to positive ones
  • 26-year OOS: vol down ~20%, Sharpe up ~40%
Method
A rotation-invariant estimator feeds the signed spectrum of a pairwise correlation proxy through a bidirectional GRU conditioned on factor-aligned effective sample lengths, trained end-to-end on five-day realized GMV risk.
Data
26 expanding-window models, 2000-2025, up to 1,500 U.S. equities, in a closing-auction simulator with commissions, financing, corporate actions and market impact.
Use
Drop it in as the covariance step when you want short-history and thinly traded names in the universe instead of screening them out.
Score 3/5
Daily U.S. equity prices are easy to source, but this is a risk model, not a signal, so you still design the strategy around it — hence 3.
covariance shrinkagepairwise-complete correlationrotation-invariant estimatorminimum-variance portfoliosmall-cap universedeep learning
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTSmall-cap-inclusive equity universes contain recently listed and intermittently traded securities, so enforcing a common look-back discards a substantial fraction of the available information. Pairwise-complete estimation preserves the longest overlap for each asset pair, but the resulting correlation matrix can be indefinite because its entries are computed on different samples. This prevents direct use in Markowitz optimization and falls outside the assumptions of standard random-matrix shrinkage. We adapt a rotation-invariant neural covariance estimator to this setting. The model computes mask-aware marginal moments and a pairwise correlation matrix proxy, processes its signed spectrum, and uses a bidirectional gated recurrent unit conditioned on factor-aligned effective sample lengths derived from the overlap matrix and eigenvector loadings. It maps all eigenvalues, including negative ones, to a positive inverse spectrum. The reconstructed covariance is positive definite and is trained end-to-end to minimize five-day realized global-minimum-variance risk. We evaluate 26 expanding-window models from 2000 to 2025 on up to 1,500 U.S. equities in a closing-auction simulator with point-in-time selection, commissions, financing, corporate actions, and market impact. Across the 26-year out-of-sample period, the neural estimator reduces annualized five-day volatility by approximately 20\% and increases the Sharpe ratio by approximately 40\% relative to the next-best covariance estimator. These improvements are consistent across realized risk, risk-adjusted performance, and drawdown control, remain after the modeled execution frictions, and are supported by a 99.9\% Model Confidence Set that retains only the neural estimator.

  • Asks how stock-level manager research aggregates into a market signal
  • Builds a daily spread on active-manager ownership rank
  • Timing strategies on that spread earn Sharpe ratios near one

Method

Use

active manager ownershipmarket timinginformation aggregationS&P 500 futureshedge fundsreturn spread
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTStock-level information produced by many active managers can aggregate into a public signal about the overall market. We show that daily return spreads between stocks with high and low active-manager ownership forecast next-day S&P 500 futures and ETF returns; market-timing strategies based on these spreads earn Sharpe ratios near one. Predictability concentrates among hedge funds and highly active mutual funds, strengthens in recessions and high-uncertainty states, exhibits no reversals, persists longer in tight-funding periods, and extends to global markets. A rational-expectations theory of specialized managers and slow-moving capital reconciles these findings and delivers new mechanisms of information aggregation. Structural estimation implies that market-wide information accounts for about 20% of model-implied stock-level signal variation among stocks heavily owned by active managers.

Style Extremity and Idiosyncratic Risk: An Incremental, Not Categorical, Determinant of Volatility on Valuation Ranks

Tests whether valuation-rank extremity predicts idiosyncratic vol beyond growth/value direction.

Ethan Wuang

  • 스타일 방향과 중앙값 거리를 뭉뚱그린 기존 주장을 분리
  • 부호 순위와 극단성 변환을 세 지표에서 경마식 회귀
  • PSR은 극단성만(t=9.7), PBR은 방향만(t=17.2) 남는다
  • 가격 기반 지표만 변동성 예측과 포트폴리오에서 값어치
방법
부호 붙은 밸류에이션 순위와 그 극단성 변환을 같은 회귀에 넣어 승부를 가리고 무관한 여섯 특성으로 위약 검정을 돌렸다.
데이터
생존편향 없는 시점 정합 패널로 미국 보통주 6,546종목(시총 3억 달러 이상), 2004~2025년 196,438 기업-분기.
시사점
주가 기반 극단성 지표를 HAR-RV·EGARCH 위에 얹어 사전 변동성 추정을 보정하는 리스크 팩터로 쓰되 알파 신호로는 쓰지 않는다.
적용도 3/5
가격·시총·재무 비율만 있으면 되니 데이터는 CRSP·Compustat 수준에서 쉽게 구하지만 매매 규칙이 없어 리스크 모델 재료에 그친다. 수익 프리미엄이 없다고 못 박은 점(t=0.7)은 스타일 극단 베팅을 접게 만드는 실용 정보다.
  • Style literature conflates direction with distance from the median
  • Horse race: signed rank vs. its extremity transform, three measures
  • P/S: extremity wins (t=9.7); B/M: direction wins (t=17.2)
  • Price-based measure beats HAR-RV and EGARCH out of sample
Method
Panel regressions racing the signed valuation rank against its extremity transform, plus six placebo extremity transforms from unrelated characteristics.
Data
Survivorship-free point-in-time panel of 6,546 U.S. common stocks above $300M cap, 2004-2025, 196,438 firm-quarters.
Use
Feed price-based extremity into a pre-trade volatility forecast to size positions, not to pick them.
Score 3/5
Score 3: inputs are standard CRSP/Compustat prices and ratios, cheap to source, but the paper hands you a risk feature with no entry or exit rules; the flat return premium (t=0.7) usefully kills style-extremity as an alpha bet.
idiosyncratic volatilitystyle investingprice-to-salesvolatility forecastingplacebo testsrisk characteristic
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTThis paper separates two claims conflated in the style-investing literature: that a firm's growth/value direction predicts risk, and that its extremity-distance from the ambiguous midpoint-predicts risk incrementally to direction. In a survivorship-free, point-in-time panel of 6,546 U.S. common stocks (market cap ≥ $300 million, 2004-2025, 196,438 firm-quarters), we run a horse race between the signed rank and its own extremity transform on three independent constructions. On price-to-sales, extremity is the whole story: direction is null (t =-0.25) while extremity survives fully intact (t = 9.7)-a genuine, if economically modest (0.050 SD), effect and our cleanest identification. On price, both terms survive jointly (direction t =-4.2, extremity t = 14.2), reported only as an upper bound given disclosed mechanical volatility content. On book-to-market the pattern inverts entirely: extremity collapses to zero (t = 0.43) once direction is included, while direction stays large (t = 17.2)-what looked like extremity is the familiar growth-options finding that low-book-to-market firms are more volatile (Cao, Simin, and Zhao 2008), not a distance-from-median effect. A battery of six placebo extremity transforms from characteristics unrelated to valuation (size, beta, bookto-market, momentum, MAX, idiosyncratic skewness) shows this symmetric pattern is not unique to valuation-all six predict forward idiosyncratic volatility, two more strongly than price-to-sales extremity itself-yet price-to-sales extremity survives every pairwise and joint test against them (t = 8.2 jointly, a 15% attenuation from its solo t = 9.9): a genuine incremental fact, not a newly documented class of risk determinant. Economic value concentrates in the price-based measure, which improves out-of-sample volatility forecasts against HAR-RV and EGARCH benchmarks (Clark-West t ≥ 3.4) and lowers realized portfolio volatility in 18 of 18 sample years, surviving a correlation-matched synthetic placebo. The identifying price-to-sales measure adds far less out-of-sample value and no portfolio benefit, and there is no return premium to either (t = 0.7): style extremity is a risk characteristic, not a return anomaly.

Priced Beta, Unpriced Beta

시장 베타를 가격에 반영되는 부분과 아닌 부분으로 쪼갠다

Splits market beta into a priced component and an unpriced one.

Christian Skov Jensen · Bocconi University

  • CAPM은 시장 베타 하나로 위험 보상을 설명한다
  • 기대수익률은 확률적 할인요인과의 공분산만 보상한다
  • 베타를 priced/unpriced 두 성분으로 분해했다
  • priced 베타만 수익률을 예측하고 격차가 클수록 오차도 커진다
방법

데이터

시사점

적용도 3/5

  • CAPM assumes market beta captures all priced risk
  • Returns pay for SDF covariance, not market covariance
  • Author decomposes beta under a spanning restriction
  • Priced beta predicts returns; unpriced beta earns nothing
Method
An asset-pricing model that imposes a priced-market spanning restriction, giving unpriced beta a zero structural price.
Data
Cross-section of individual stock returns; the abstract names no sample window.
Use
Hedge on priced beta instead of total beta, and trade the gap between the two betas as an alpha signal.
Score 3/5

CAPMmarket betastochastic discount factorpriced riskpricing errorscross-section of returns
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTThe CAPM treats market beta as a sufficient statistic for priced aggregate risk. Yet expected returns compensate covariance with the stochastic discount factor, not covariance with the market itself. This distinction matters when some market fluctuations are orthogonal to the stochastic discount factor: they contribute to market volatility but not to the market risk premium. Market beta measures how much an asset covaries with the market, but not whether that covariance is generated by fluctuations relevant for marginal utility. I show that market beta combines exposure to priced and unpriced market variation. Under a priced-market spanning restriction, expected returns depend on priced beta, while unpriced beta has a zero structural price. Conventional beta therefore becomes an imperfect measure of compensated risk when assets load differently on the two components, generating CAPM pricing errors that increase with the difference in exposures. Empirically, priced and unpriced betas differ substantially across stocks. Consistent with the model, priced beta predicts average returns, unpriced beta carries little independent premium, and CAPM pricing errors increase with the exposure gap. Market beta's pricing performance therefore depends on what moves the market.

Timing the Market: A Skewed Perspective

  • 무작위 진입 1만 명 분산 + 기계적 타이밍 규칙 2종

데이터
MSCI 6개 선진국 국가 지수, 2000~2025년
시사점
하락 매수 규칙을 쓸지 말지를 그 시장의 장기 추세로 먼저 거르는 필터로 쓸 만하다
적용도 3/5
MSCI 국가 지수는 구하기 쉽고 규칙도 단순하지만 진입 타이밍 연구라 매매 전략 자체는 아니라서 3점이다

  • 10,000 randomly-timed investors plus two mechanical timing rules
  • Return vs. timing penalty correlates at −0.82

Method
Dispersion of final wealth across 10,000 random entry dates, plus a dip buyer and a hype chaser benchmarked against dollar-cost averaging.

MSCI country indices for six developed markets, 2000–2025.
Use

market timingdollar-cost averagingdriftdrawdown buyinginternational equity indicesentry timing
ORIGINAL ABSTRACT"Time in the market beats timing the market" is among the most widely repeated maxims in investing, yet the investors who popularised it are almost all American and describe the American market. This paper asks whether the claim travels. Using MSCI country indices for six developed markets over 2000–2025, it measures how much an investor's final wealth depended on when their money entered, through two tests: the dispersion of outcomes across ten thousand randomly-timed passive investors, and the performance of two mechanical timing rules (a dip buyer and a hype chaser) against dollar-cost averaging. Both tests point to the same variable. Entry timing mattered far less than commonly assumed, and where it did matter it tracked a market's drift rather than its volatility: the US and Japan, with near-identical volatility, showed markedly different sensitivity to entry date. For deliberate timing the pattern reverses across markets — waiting for a 20% drawdown beat staying invested in Japan, the UK and Germany, but lost heavily in the high-drift US, where the dip buyer finished below all ten thousand randomly-timed investors. The correlation between a market's return and the penalty for timing it is strongly negative at −0.82. The mechanism is that time spent out of a rising market is a cost that scales with drift; the proverb is therefore best read not as a universal law but as a description of the exceptional US market that produced it.

Theory only — no independent empirical replication, just a self-contained unit-economics illustration.
Use
Plug your own maker fee into a single grid cycle before funding any grid bot.
Score 3/5
No tradeable signal to backtest, but exchange fee schedules and fills are trivially available and it debunks a heavily sold strategy — hence 3.
grid tradingcryptocurrencydelta-neutral market makingmaker feesunit economicsadverse selection
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTGrid trading is one of the most widely marketed algorithmic strategies on retail cryptocurrency exchanges, typically presented through screenshots of positive profitand-loss curves with little disclosure of deployed capital, holding period, or fee tier. This note examines whether grid trading generates an independent trading edge or whether its apparent profitability is better explained by market exposure, execution economics, and market-making infrastructure. We review three mechanically distinct implementations discussed in the recent literature-classical grid trading, Dynamic Grid Trading (DGT), and delta-neutral grid market making-and evaluate the evidentiary basis behind each. For classical grid trading, we rely on an existing analytical result showing zero expected profit under an explicitly stated symmetric random-walk model before costs. For Dynamic Grid Trading, we show that the reported outperformance over buy-and-hold is attributable in part to retained directional exposure over a period of substantial cumulative price appreciation, a point the source paper's own authors acknowledge. For delta-neutral grid market making, we identify an unresolved capital-accounting inconsistency and an incomplete fill model in the primary source, together with citation inconsistencies between its in-text attributions and its bibliography, and we supplement this with an original unit-economics illustration showing that the sign of a representative spot-leg cycle's economics flips from positive to negative when moving from the paper's assumed maker rebate to a standard retail maker fee. Taken together, the evidence indicates that grid trading is not a single strategy with a single source of return: as implementations move from a simple grid toward market-neutral market making, the economic source of any edge shifts away from the grid structure itself and toward fee schedules, execution quality, adverse-selection control, and market microstructure. We do not conduct an independent empirical replication of any of the three implementations; our contribution is a structured, source-verified synthesis of the existing evidence, a citation-consistency check on one of the primary sources, and a self-contained fee-sensitivity illustration.

Theory of European Option Underwriting Portfolios

Recasts unhedged European option writing as an insurance underwriting problem with finite capital.

Pasin Marupanthorn · Heriot-Watt University

  • 무헤지 매도자는 손실 부착점과 자본 배분을 같이 정해야 한다
  • 풋·콜 부채를 선언 자본 단위로 표준화해 잉여와 2차 적률을 계산한다

  • Deribit 데이터에서 총잉여 차이는 평균 양수지만 꼬리는 고르지 않다

다변량 로그정규(multivariate lognormal) 만기 분포에서 풋·콜 청구액의 적률을 닫힌 형태로 풀고 조건부 자본 배분과 국소 행사가 조건을 유도한다.

아카이브된 Deribit 암호화폐 옵션 조합을 시점 고정 계약 스크린과 고정 머니니스 벤치마크로 검증했다. 구간은 밝히지 않았다.
시사점
옵션 매도 북의 자본 소진과 동시 청구 위험을 사전 점검하는 리스크 진단 층으로 붙일 만하다.
적용도 3/5
Deribit 옵션 체인은 공개 데이터라 확보가 어렵지 않지만 진입·청산 규칙이 없고 저자도 실행 가능한 순수익성은 입증하지 못했다고 못 박아 3점이다.
  • Unhedged writers must set loss attachment points and split capital
  • Model states put and call liabilities per unit of declared capital
  • Closed-form claim moments under a multivariate lognormal terminal law
  • Deribit tests show positive mean gross-surplus gaps, uneven tails
Method
A static terminal model separating premium income from claim risk, with analytical moments and conditional capital allocation under multivariate lognormal terminal prices.
Data
Archived Deribit crypto-option formations, screened point-in-time against fixed-moneyness benchmarks; the window is not stated.
Use
Bolt it on as a solvency diagnostic for a short-option book: how much capital each tail can consume and when two tails fire together.
Score 3/5
Deribit chains are public and easy to source, but the paper gives no entry or exit rules and explicitly disclaims executable net profitability, so it caps at 3.
option writingunderwriting surpluscapital allocationmultivariate lognormalcrypto optionstail dependence
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTWriting unhedged options creates a liability-management problem in which the writer must determine both where contractual losses attach and how finite capital is distributed across those exposures. We develop a static terminal framework for European cash-settled written options that jointly represents lower put liabilities, upper call liabilities, and their capital allocations. More specifically, the model separates observed premium income from physical claim risk, expresses each liability per unit of declared capital, and evaluates the portfolio through expected underwriting surplus and the raw second moment of aggregate claims. Under a multivariate lognormal terminal law, we obtain analytical claim moments, characterize conditional capital allocation and local strike conditions, and show that Gaussian dependence affects same-tail and opposite-tail claim accumulation in different directions. We then apply the framework to archived Deribit crypto-option formations using point-in-time contract screens, fixed-moneyness benchmarks, and paired dependence-aware uncertainty. We find positive average gross-surplus differences for the staged policies in the completed samples, although the associated dispersion and tail effects are not uniform and a realized uncovered-call claim exceeds its finite capital proxy. Thus, the empirical evidence supports the use of the framework as a solvency-aware underwriting and diagnostic architecture, but it does not establish calibrated tail probabilities, executable net profitability, or broker-margin survival.

The Third Derivative of Bond Prices: Skewness, Drift, and Expected Returns in Crisis Regimes

채권 가격의 3차 미분항이 위기 국면에서 실제로 돈이 된다는 걸 검증했다

The third derivative of bond price to yield matters in crises, not in calm markets.

Hardik Trehan

  • 3차 항은 그동안 무시해도 되는 상수 취급을 받았다
  • 수익률 변화를 확률변수로 놓고 2단계 존재정리를 세웠다
  • 30년물에서 감마/컨벡시티 비율이 17.5%까지 올라간다
방법
수익률 변화에 정규분포와 정규-역가우시안(Normal-Inverse Gaussian)을 각각 적용해 순수 드리프트, 드리프트-분산, 순수 왜도 세 채널로 3차 항을 분해한다
데이터
Adrian-Crump-Moench 수익률 곡선으로 1973-74 오일쇼크, 2000-02 닷컴 붕괴, 2007-09 금융위기, 2020 코로나 국면을 다뤘고 CME 국채선물 5종 일별 정산가로 왜도를 검정했다
시사점
위기 국면 장기물 포지션의 손익 근사에 3차 항을 넣고 왜도 부호에 따라 듀레이션 헤지 비율을 조정한다
적용도 3/5
ACM 곡선과 CME 선물 정산가 모두 공개 데이터라 재현은 쉽지만 매수·매도 규칙이 아니라 리스크 계산식이라 전략으로 만들려면 설계가 더 필요해 3점이다
  • Prior work dismissed the third-order term as a deterministic curiosity
  • Treats yield change as random, proves a two-stage existence theorem
  • Gamma-to-convexity climbs from 1.9% at 2y to 17.5% at 30y
Method
Decomposes the third-order term into pure-drift and drift-variance channels under a normal yield distribution, plus one pure-skewness channel under a Normal-Inverse Gaussian.
Data
Adrian-Crump-Moench yields across the 1973-74 oil shock, 2000-02 dot-com, 2007-09 GFC and 2020 COVID windows, with skewness tested on daily settlements for five CME Treasury futures (pooled χ²(3) = 73.4).
Use
Add the third-order term to P&L approximations for long-maturity crisis positions and tilt duration hedges by the sign of yield skewness.
Score 3/5
ACM curves and CME settlements are free and public, so replication is cheap, but this is a risk-pricing correction rather than an entry/exit rule, so it needs real strategy design on top.
bond convexitythird-order sensitivityyield skewnesscrisis regimesNormal-Inverse GaussianTreasury futures
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTThe third derivative of bond price with respect to yield, the analogue of gamma for fixed income, has been derived before but treated as a deterministic curiosity of marginal importance. This paper advances and tests a single hypothesis: that this third-order term is economically material during macroeconomic crises, with a contribution to expected price change governed jointly by maturity, the size of the yield move, and the asymmetry of the yield-change distribution. We treat the yield change as a random variable and establish a two-stage existence theorem. Under a normal distribution with non-zero drift the term acts through a puredrift channel and a drift-variance channel; the Normal-Inverse Gaussian extension adds exactly one further term, a pure-skewness channel proportional to the third cumulant that vanishes under any symmetric distribution. Empirically, across the 1973-74 oil shock, the 2000-02 dot-com deflation, the 2007-09 global financial crisis, and the COVID-19 2020 shock measured with Adrian-Crump-Moench data, the third-order sensitivity scales cubically with maturity (a thirty-year to two-year ratio of roughly 525 to 690 across the three historical crises, and about 1380 in the low-yield COVID window), the gamma-to-convexity ratio rises from 1.9 percent at two years to 17.5 percent at thirty years at crisis-scale moves and holds at a stable 0.76 of the Brooks-Livingston convexity-to-duration ratio, and the skewness channel reaches 48 to 86 percent of the drift channels for thirty-year bonds, offsetting most of the normal-distribution gamma contribution in the dot-com episode. The skewness condition itself is confirmed decisively in daily settlement data for five CME Treasury futures contracts, with a pooled statistic of χ²(3) = 73.4 and significant within-window skewness in the dot-com episode, while the global financial crisis window is fattailed rather than skewed; the shorter monthly windows lack the power to reject symmetry on their own. The third-order term is negligible in the small, symmetric setting examined by earlier work and material in the large, directional, asymmetric moves that characterize crises.

BDC stock-to-NAV discounts forecast private credit markdowns.

Reena Aggarwal, Isil Erel, Changyong Song · Georgetown University - Robert Emmett McDonough School of Business , Ohio State University (OSU) - Department of Finance and

ORIGINAL ABSTRACTBusiness Development Companies (BDCs) combine periodic reporting of illiquid private credit with continuously traded equity, providing a unique setting to evaluate private credit performance. During periods of credit stress, performance drops and stock-to-NAV discounts widen sharply as stock prices fall faster than reported NAVs. Market discounts and pre-announcement stock returns predict subsequent NAV markdowns and weaker profitability, although their predictive power is limited for larger BDCs and those with more diversified portfolios. Crucially, market metrics have limited ability to anticipate discrete distress events such as non-accruals and payment-in-kind (PIK) amendments. We find that quarterly NAV announcements contain significant, value-relevant incremental information that results in immediate stock price reactions.

Wasserstein-Barycentric Interaction Fields for Spatial Factor Models: Evidence from Language-Model Representations

Builds a firm interaction matrix from news embedding distributions instead of assuming one.

Marcus Gawronsky, Chun-Sung Huang

  • 공간 수익률 모형은 연결 행렬을 그냥 주어진 값으로 받는다
  • 기사 임베딩 분포를 바리센터로 재구성해 필드를 만든다
  • 2023-2026 페널티 비율 3.46, 95% 구간 [2.89, 4.17]
  • 동일가중·RBF 가중보다 조건부 준우도가 높다
방법
종목별 언어모델 기사 임베딩 분포에 목표 고정 바서슈타인(Wasserstein) 바리센터 재구성을 적용하고, 이차 노출조정 문제로 피드백을 동종업체 정렬 오차 페널티 비율로 바꾼다.
데이터
52개 종목의 뉴스 기사이며 2018-2022년 필드를 고정한 뒤 2023-2026년으로 검증한다.
시사점
동종업체 가중치를 손으로 정하는 대신 뉴스 임베딩에서 뽑아 리스크 모형이나 페어 후보 선정에 넣어볼 만하다.
적용도 2/5
종목 단위 뉴스 본문과 임베딩 파이프라인이 필요해 데이터 확보가 비싸고, 표본도 52개뿐이라 매매 규칙까지 가려면 설계가 많이 남는다.
  • Spatial return models take the interaction matrix as given
  • Wasserstein barycentric reconstruction of article embeddings
  • 2023-2026 penalty ratio 3.46, 95% interval [2.89, 4.17]
  • Beats equal-weighted and RBF peer weights on quasi-likelihood
Method
Target-anchored Wasserstein barycentric reconstruction of language-model article embedding distributions, with a quadratic exposure-adjustment problem converting feedback into a peer-misalignment penalty ratio.
Data
News articles for 52 firms; the field is frozen on 2018-2022 data and evaluated on 2023-2026.
Use
Replace hand-set peer weights in a risk model or pair-selection screen with news-embedding-derived ones.
Score 2/5
Scored 2 because it needs firm-level news text plus an embedding pipeline — costly to source — and 52 firms with no entry or exit rules leaves heavy design work.
spatial factor modelsWasserstein barycenterlanguage-model embeddingspeer networksnews co-mentionquasi-likelihood
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTSpatial return models take the interaction matrix as given and leave feedback uninterpreted. We construct a bandwidth-free field from firms' language-model article embedding distributions using target-anchored Wasserstein barycentric reconstruction. A quadratic exposure-adjustment problem maps feedback into a peer-misalignment penalty ratio. For 52 firms, the field, frozen from 2018-2022 news, yields a 2023-2026 penalty ratio of 3.46 (95% interval [2.89, 4.17]) and higher conditional quasi-likelihood than equal-weighted peer support or RBF weighting of the same distances. Joint penalty ratios for the barycentric and news co-mention fields are 2.33 and 0.86 with boundary calibrated tests which reject both exclusions.

Metaorder modelling and identification from public data

익명 공개 체결 데이터만으로 메타주문을 복원해 LMF 이론과 맞춰본 연구다.

The authors rebuild metaorders from anonymous public trade data and test the LMF splitting theory.

Ezra Goliath, Tim Gebbie

  • LMF 주문분할 검증은 트레이더 ID가 붙은 독점 데이터에 묶여 있었다
  • 익명 공개 체결·호가 데이터로 합성 메타주문을 복원한다
  • 복원 파라미터를 격자 탐색하고 충격 정형사실·LMF 관계로 평가한다
  • 충격 정형사실을 맞추면 LMF가 깨지고 LMF를 맞추면 종목별 적합이 약해진다
방법
체결·호가 데이터에서 합성 메타주문을 복원하는 규칙을 격자 탐색으로 고르고 메타주문 정형사실과 LMF 관계로 채점한다.

시사점
체결 데이터에서 대형 주문의 잔여 물량을 추정하는 복원 규칙은 쓸 만하지만 파라미터 선택 기준에 따라 결론이 갈리니 목적을 먼저 정해야 한다.
적용도 2/5
체결·호가 데이터 자체는 살 수 있어도 JSE 틱 데이터 조달과 복원 파이프라인 구축 부담이 크고 매매 규칙이 아니라 시장구조 검증이라 2점이다.
  • LMF tests have needed proprietary data with trader IDs
  • Grid search over synthetic metaorder reconstruction rules
  • Impact-tuned configs fit stylised facts but break LMF
  • LMF-tuned configs recover the relation, weaker per-stock fits
Method
Synthetic metaorder reconstruction from trades and quotes, with a grid search scored against metaorder stylised facts and the LMF relation.
Data
Trade and quote data for the 239 largest JSE stocks by market cap (ranked 13 March 2026), covering 1 January 2023 to 31 December 2025.
Use
Use the reconstruction rules to estimate how much of a large parent order is still working, but pick the tuning target deliberately — it changes the answer.
Score 2/5
Score 2: trade and quote data is purchasable but JSE tick data plus a reconstruction pipeline is a heavy lift, and the output is market-structure evidence, not a tradeable rule.
metaorder reconstructionorder splittingLillo-Mike-Farmermarket impactorder flow autocorrelationJSE
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTMarket-order flow in financial markets exhibits long-range correlations. This is a widely known stylised fact of financial markets. A popular hypothesis for this stylised fact comes from the Lillo-Mike-Farmer (LMF) order-splitting theory. However, quantitative tests of this theory have historically relied on proprietary datasets with trader identifiers, limiting reproducibility and cross-market validation. We investigate whether it can be recovered from anonymous public data using synthetic metaorder reconstruction. Using transaction and quote data for the largest 239 stocks by market capitalisation on the JSE as of 13 March 2026 with the data range being 1 January 2023 until 31 December 2025, we conduct a grid search over reconstruction parameters and evaluate each configuration against established metaorder stylised facts and the LMF relation. Configurations selected to minimise errors across the metaorder impact stylised facts reproduce the targeted aggregate properties but yield a poor LMF relation. Configurations selected to minimise the LMF discrepancy recover the relation by construction while retaining several broad impact features, although some stock-level execution and decay fits are weaker. These asymmetric results show that recovering aggregate impact stylised facts alone is insufficient to identify LMF-consistent order splitting, while the LMF-targeted result establishes compatibility within the reconstruction class rather than an independent test. The findings support consistency with, rather than direct validation of, the LMF theory using anonymous market data.

Trade Treasury ETF direction in the minutes after the Q&A, sized up when EPU is high — if you build the audio pipeline yourself.
Score 2/5

ORIGINAL ABSTRACTThis paper examines whether vocal delivery during Federal Open Market Committee (FOMC) press-conference Q&A sessions contains market-relevant information beyond the words spoken. Using WavLM, a large-scale speech processing model, we construct a vocal-based measure of monetary-policy stance, defined as hawkish versus dovish rather than generic emotion, applied to 89 press conferences from 2011 to 2025. For each sentence, we compare the original human audio with a text-to-speech (TTS) rendering of the same text. The Human-TTS difference provides a fixed-text measure of Q&A delivery and speaker composition. Larger gaps are associated with higher short-window cumulative abnormal returns on Treasury ETFs across maturities, conditional on textual stance, macroeconomic conditions, monetary-policy surprises, and chair regimes. When economic policy uncertainty is high, larger gaps are also associated with higher S&P 500 ETF returns and declines in VIX and VVIX. The evidence indicates that the vocal delivery and speaker composition of the Q&A contain state-dependent information not captured by text alone.

Large-Load Tariff Decisions and Utility Market Responses

대형 부하 요금제 승인이 유틸리티 주가를 움직이는지 이벤트 스터디로 검증했다

An event study asks whether large-load tariff rulings reprice listed utilities. They mostly don't.

Fuli Yang

  • 데이터센터 전력 요금제 결정이 주가에 반영되나
  • 15건 규제 결정, [-1,+1] 누적초과수익 측정
  • CAR 0.39%p, p=0.353으로 재평가 근거 없음
  • 거래량은 16.2% 늘지만 날짜 집계 시 유의성 소멸
방법
SPY와 XLU를 벤치마크로 삼아 [-120,-21] 구간에서 기대수익을 추정한 뒤 [-1,+1] 누적초과수익(CAR)과 평균조정 로그 거래량, 사후/사전 실현위험 비율을 부호 반전 검정과 부트스트랩으로 따진다.
데이터
2022~2026년 미국 13개 주, 상장 유틸리티 모회사 13곳에 걸친 규제기관 확인 최초 최종 요금제 결정 15건과 해당 종목의 일별 수익률·거래량이다.
시사점
요금제 결정일 전후로 유틸리티 종목의 거래량 급증을 예상할 수는 있으나 방향성 베팅 근거는 없으니 이벤트 회피용 리스크 필터로만 쓴다
적용도 2/5
규제 결정의 정확한 날짜를 직접 손으로 모아야 하고 표본이 15건뿐이라 자동 매매 규칙으로 옮기기 어렵다
  • Do data-center tariff rulings move utility stocks?
  • 15 dated rulings, [-1,+1] cumulative abnormal return
  • CAR 0.39pp, p=0.353 — no average repricing
  • Volume rises 16.2%, but fails event-date aggregation
Method
Estimate expected returns over days [-120,-21] against SPY and XLU, then test [-1,+1] CARs, mean-adjusted log volume, and post/pre realized-risk ratios with sign-flip inference and bootstrap intervals.
Data
15 regulator-verified first final tariff decisions across 13 states and 13 listed utility parents, 2022 through 2026, with daily returns and volume.
Use
Treat tariff decision dates as a volume-spike risk flag on utility names, not as a directional signal.
Score 2/5
The decision dates must be hand-collected from state regulators and the sample is only 15 events, so there is no rule a machine can trade.
event studyutility stockslarge-load tariffsabnormal returnsabnormal volumedata centers
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTU.S. regulators are adopting large-load tariffs to allocate the financial risk created when utilities build long-lived infrastructure for data centers and other concentrated loads. This paper studies 15 regulator-verified, exact-dated first final tariff decisions covering 13 listed utility parents and 13 states from 2022 through 2026. Customer-specific special-contract approvals are excluded from the primary sample. Expected returns are estimated over trading days [-120,-21]; the primary event window is [-1,+1]. Repeated decisions are collapsed to listed-parent means for exact sign-flip inference. The mean SPY-model cumulative abnormal return is 0.39 percentage points (p = 0.353; bootstrap 95 percent interval:-0.40 to 1.14), providing no evidence of average repricing under the primary model. The XLUadjusted [-1,+1] estimate is positive, but alternative timing windows and influence checks weaken it, making the valuation conclusion benchmark-, window-, and influence-sensitive. A transparent mean-adjusted log-volume measure rises by 0.150 log points, or approximately 16.2 percent. The parent-level result is positive (p = 0.028) but does not survive event-date aggregation (p = 0.110). The log post/pre realized-risk ratio is not distinguishable from zero. The evidence is consistent with, but does not establish, a dependence-sensitive attention response; valuation conclusions are not robust across specifications. The design cannot identify project-level incidence or downstream ratepayer effects.

Intraday Response of the Term Structure to US Macroeconomic Shocks: Evidence from the B3 DI Futures Market

미국 고용·물가 발표가 브라질 금리선물 만기별로 어떻게 번지는지 분 단위로 측정했다

Measures how US payroll and CPI releases hit the Brazilian DI curve minute by minute.

Christian Aloys Wafo Nomsi

  • 미국 지표 충격이 브라질 금리 곡선에 만기별로 다르게 닿는다
  • 1분봉 이벤트 스터디에 AR(1)-GARCH(1,1)-X를 얹어 추정했다
  • 발표 충격 계수는 단기 0.3884에서 장기 0.2241로 줄어든다
  • 변동성 지속성은 반대로 0.9257에서 0.9812까지 커진다
방법
AR(1)-GARCH(1,1)-X 결합모형을 HAC Newey-West 표준오차로 추정한 인트라데이 이벤트 스터디다
데이터
B3에 상장된 DI 선물 3종(DI1F29, DI1F32, DI1F37)의 1분 고빈도 데이터로 미국 고용지표와 CPI 발표 시점을 살폈다
시사점
미국 지표 발표 직후 브라질 단기 DI를 방향성으로, 장기 DI를 변동성 매매로 나눠 잡는 근거로 쓴다
적용도 2/5
만기별 계수 차이가 뚜렷하지만 B3 DI 선물 1분봉은 유료 벤더를 거쳐야 하고 발표 직후 체결 인프라도 필요해 2점을 줬다
  • US macro shocks reach each DI maturity differently
  • 1-minute event study plus AR(1)-GARCH(1,1)-X models
  • Surprise beta falls from 0.3884 short to 0.2241 long
  • Volatility persistence rises from 0.9257 to 0.9812
Method
Joint AR(1)-GARCH(1,1)-X estimation with HAC Newey-West robust standard errors around release timestamps.
Data
1-minute B3 DI futures data for DI1F29, DI1F32 and DI1F37 around US Non-Farm Payroll and CPI releases.
Use
Split the post-release trade: direction at the short end, volatility clustering at the long end.
Score 2/5
The maturity gradient is clean, but 1-minute B3 DI tick data needs a paid vendor and fast execution, so it caps at 2.
DI futuresterm structuremacro announcementsGARCHintraday event studyprice discovery
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTThis paper investigates price adjustment speed, price discovery, and conditional volatility dynamics across the Brazilian interest rate term structure following US macroeconomic announcements (Non-Farm Payroll and CPI). Using 1-minute high-frequency data of Brazilian DI futures contracts traded on B3 across three maturities-short (DI1F29), intermediate (DI1F32), and long (DI1F37)-, we conduct an intraday event study and estimate joint AR(1)-GARCH(1,1)-X models with HAC Newey-West robust standard errors. Empirical findings reveal monotonic term-structure heterogeneity: in the conditional mean equation, the directional impact of exogenous surprises peaks at the short end and decreases monotonically toward the long end (β Pay declines from +0.3884 to +0.2241; β CPI decreases from +0.2874 to +0.1362, both statistically significant). In the conditional variance equation, releases trigger immediate volatility spikes (γ ≈ 0.046 to 0.049), while persistence (α + β) increases with contractual maturity (0.9257 for DI1F29, 0.9635 for DI1F32, and 0.9812 for DI1F37). The findings support the semi-strong form of the Efficient Market Hypothesis and show that international shocks primarily alter short-to-medium-term domestic monetary policy expectations, whereas longer maturities experience prolonged volatility clustering.

The Convergence Rate of Stochastic Tracking with Application to Optimal Execution

체결 전략의 규제화 오차가 √ε 속도로 줄어든다는 걸 증명했다.

Proves the regularized optimal execution cost converges at rate O(√ε).

Marcel Nutz, Moritz Voss · Columbia University and University of California Los Angeles, Department of Mathematics

  • 목표 궤적 추종 오차의 명시적 상한이 없었다
  • 베조프(Besov) 계열 모듈러스로 비점근 상한을 유도
  • 반마팅게일 목표에서 제곱근 차수 수렴을 확인
  • 구현 가능한 근사 최적 전략도 같은 속도를 낸다
방법
힐베르트 공간 기법으로 최적 청산 전략을 특징짓고 거래 속도에 이차 페널티 ε을 걸어 규제화한다.
데이터
이론 연구
시사점
점프성 체결 스파이크를 없애려 거래 속도에 페널티를 걸 때 ε을 얼마나 크게 잡아도 되는지 비용 손실로 환산해 정할 수 있다.
적용도 2/5
실행 알고리즘의 파라미터 선택에 바로 쓸 근거지만 신호가 아니라 이론이고, 오비자에바-왕(Obizhaeva-Wang) 모형 보정에는 호가창 복원 속도 같은 주문 흐름 데이터가 필요해 2점이다.
  • No explicit non-asymptotic bounds existed for tracking error
  • Besov-type modulus of the target gives sharp upper bounds
  • Semimartingale targets hit square-root order convergence
  • A closed-form-free nearly optimal strategy matches the rate
Method
A Hilbert-space characterization of the Obizhaeva–Wang optimal strategy, regularized by a quadratic trading-rate penalty ε.
Data
theory only
Use
Size the trading-rate penalty in a liquidation algo by pricing exactly how much excess impact cost each ε buys you.
Score 2/5
Directly useful for tuning an execution engine, but it is theory rather than a signal, and calibrating an Obizhaeva–Wang resilience parameter needs order book or order flow data most desks lack.
optimal executionObizhaeva-Wangprice impactstochastic trackingregularizationconvergence rate
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTWe study the quadratic tracking problem of a general stochastic target process with absolutely continuous controls, with and without terminal constraint. We derive explicit, non-asymptotic upper bounds in terms of a Besov-type modulus of the target. These bounds yield sharp explicit rates that specialize to the square-root order for semimartingale targets. We then apply these results to a generalized Obizhaeva–Wang execution model with random terminal inventory. We first develop a Hilbert-space approach to characterize its optimal strategy, which includes jumps. To avoid such trading spikes, one regularizes the problem by a quadratic trading-rate penalty with coefficient ε. We then show that the regularized optimal execution cost—and therefore the excess price impact cost of the regularized optimal strategy—converges at the sharp rate O(√ ε). Since the regularized optimal strategy is not available in closed form, we further construct a nearly optimal strategy which is readily implementable and shares the same approximation rate.

Regulatory Fragmentation and Firm Risk

Regulatory fragmentation raises option-implied perceived risk of firms.

Harish Kamal, Samit Paul, Sudarshan Kumar · Indian Institute of Management Udaipur, Indian Institute of Management Calcutta and Indian Institute of Management Calcutta

  • 규제 환경이 옵션 가격에 어떻게 스며드는지 몰랐다
  • 규제 파편화·강도·예정 규제를 나눠 위험도와 연결했다
  • 파편화만 위험도를 높였고 강도와 예정 규제는 무관했다
  • 재무제약 기업일수록 효과가 크고 자본비용도 올랐다
방법
규제 파편화 노출도를 옵션 내재 위험도에 회귀하고 연방기관 권한 변화 같은 외생 충격으로 인과를 확인한다
데이터
미국 상장기업의 규제 문서 기반 파편화 지표와 옵션 내재 위험도를 결합했다. 표본 구간은 초록에 없다
시사점
규제 파편화 지표를 옵션 매도 종목의 배제 필터나 내재변동성 리스크 프리미엄 조정 항으로 쓴다
적용도 2/5
옵션 체인은 구하기 쉽지만 규제 파편화 지표는 연방 규제 문서를 텍스트 처리해 직접 만들어야 하고 매매 규칙도 없어 2점이다
  • No one linked regulatory structure to option-implied risk
  • Split regulation into fragmentation, intensity, pipeline
  • Only fragmentation moves perceived riskiness
  • Effect concentrates in constrained, low-flexibility firms
Method
Regress firm-level regulatory fragmentation exposure on option-implied riskiness, with exogenous shocks to fragmentation and to federal agency power as identification.
Data
US firms' regulatory fragmentation exposure matched to option-implied risk measures; sample window not stated in the abstract.
Use
Use fragmentation exposure as a screen for short-vol names or as a tilt in an implied-vol risk-premium model.
Score 2/5
Score 2: option chains are standard, but the fragmentation measure must be built from federal regulatory text yourself, and no trading rule is given.
regulatory fragmentationoption-implied riskimplied cost of equitypolicy uncertaintyfinancial constraintslobbying
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTWe examine the impact of regulatory fragmentation on firms' perceived riskiness. We find that firms' exposure to regulatory fragmentation is positively associated with optionsimplied perceived riskiness, while no such relationship is observed for regulatory intensity or regulatory pipeline. These effects are mainly driven by firms that are financially constrained, exhibit lower financial flexibility, incur higher overhead costs, and are less productive. We show that exogenous shocks that raise (lower) regulatory fragmentation lead to a substantial increase (decrease) in the perceived riskiness of firms that were subject to low (high) pre-shock regulatory fragmentation. We do not find such effects when there is an increase or decrease in the powers of a federal agency. We observe that firms' perceived riskiness increases further when they engage in lobbying and are exposed to greater regulatory fragmentation. With regard to real effect, we observe that firms' regulatory fragmentation positively affects the implied cost of equity. Overall, we identify regulatory fragmentation-related uncertainty as a novel determinant of option-implied risk, highlighting the role of firms' regulatory environment on risk management and option pricing.

Financing Liquidity with Capital or Information?

AMM의 자본 효율은 정보로 자본을 대신한 결과다

Capital-efficient AMMs swap balance-sheet capital for external price information.

Ralf Laschinger, Heiko Leonhard · LMU Munich School of Management and University of Regensburg - International Real Estate Business School (IREBS)

  • 유동성 공급자는 수요보다 먼저 자본을 묶어야 한다
  • 상수곱 AMM과 오라클 기반 AMM을 비교했다
  • 가격 규칙이 평평할수록 자본은 덜 들고 외부 정보 의존은 커진다
  • 재고가 목표에서 벗어나면 자본 효율이 나빠진다
방법
상수곱(constant-product)과 오라클 기반 마켓메이커의 가격 규칙을 비교하고, 재고 민감도와 체결 결과를 실증으로 연결했다.
데이터
토큰화된 실물자산(tokenized RWA)의 온체인 거래 데이터를 썼고 검증 구간은 밝히지 않았다.
시사점
오라클 갱신이 늦어 남아 있는 낡은 호가를 잡아 역선택 차익을 노리거나, 반대로 LP로서 그 구간을 피하는 데 쓴다.
적용도 2/5
온체인 데이터라 원장 자체는 공개지만 오라클 갱신 시점까지 맞춘 밀리초 단위 재구성과 체결 인프라가 필요해 2점을 줬다.
  • Market makers must post capital before demand shows up
  • Compares constant-product and oracle-based AMMs
  • Flatter pricing cuts capital, raises reliance on oracles
  • Efficiency peaks at target inventory, decays with imbalance
Method
Contrasts constant-product and oracle-based pricing rules, then links inventory sensitivity to order flow and markouts.
Data
On-chain trades in tokenized real-world assets; the abstract names no sample window.
Use
Trade stale executable quotes against a fresher reference price, or steer LP inventory away from those windows.
Score 2/5
On-chain data is public, but exploiting stale quotes needs oracle-aligned millisecond reconstruction and fast execution, so it caps at 2.
automated market makercapital efficiencyadverse selectionoracle pricingtokenized RWAmarkouts
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTStandard decentralized market making requires liquidity providers to commit capital before demand arrives. We study what replaces this capital when market design becomes more capital efficient. Comparing constant-product and oracle-based market makers, we show that flatter pricing rules reduce capital requirements by weakening inventory-based price adjustment and increasing reliance on external price information. Using on-chain data from tokenized real-world assets, we find that capital efficiency is strongest near target inventories and deteriorates with inventory imbalances. Reference-price updates redirect order flow, while stale executable prices predict trader selection and adverse markouts, with stronger trading responses when inventory sensitivity is lower. Capital efficiency therefore substitutes information for balance-sheet capital in liquidity provision.

The Inflation-Adjusted Price of Gold and Solar Activity (1968–2025): R2=0.91

태양활동 주기로 실질 금값을 설명한다는 회귀 분석

Regresses CPI-adjusted gold prices on the year number within the solar cycle.

Vladimir Belkin · Independent

  • 제번스와 치제프스키의 태양-경기 가설을 금값에 적용한다
  • 1968~2025년 태양주기 연차별 평균 실질 금값을 비교했다
  • 결정계수 0.9081, 2026년 2,530.91달러를 예측한다
  • 표본 수가 적어 과적합 위험을 따로 다루지 않는다
방법
태양주기 안의 연차 번호를 설명변수로 놓고 CPI 조정 금값에 회귀를 돌렸다.
데이터
1968~2025년 달러 표시 금값과 미국 CPI, 태양물리학 표준 주기 번호를 썼다.
시사점
태양주기 연차를 금 포지션의 보조 필터로 넣어보되 표본 외 검증을 먼저 통과시켜야 한다.
적용도 2/5
금값과 CPI, 태양주기 번호는 모두 공짜로 구할 수 있어 재현은 쉽지만 진입·청산 규칙이 없고 57년 표본에 주기 연차 11개뿐이라 R2 0.91을 신뢰하기 어렵다.
  • Revives the Jevons–Chizhevsky sunspot-economy link for gold
  • Groups 1968–2025 gold prices by solar cycle year number
  • Reports R2 = 0.9081; forecasts $2,530.91 for 2026
  • No out-of-sample test or overfitting control is reported
Method
Averages real gold prices by position within the solar cycle and fits a regression.
Data
US dollar gold prices deflated by CPI, 1968–2025, with standard solar cycle year numbering.
Use
Test solar cycle year as a slow overlay filter on gold exposure, only after a clean out-of-sample check.
Score 2/5
Gold, CPI and sunspot numbers are all free and easy to pull, but the paper gives no entry or exit rules, and 57 years of data spread across roughly 11 cycle-year buckets makes the 0.91 fit look like curve-fitting.
gold pricesolar cycleinflation-adjustedspurious correlationprice forecast
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTThe report uses a methodological approach developed by W. S. Jevons and A. L. Chizhevsky. During the study period, solar cycle years were numbered according to the established order in solar astrophysics, grouped by year numbers, and compared with average real (CPI-adjusted) gold prices in US dollars. The coefficient of determination in the diagram of average solar cycle year numbers versus the inflation-adjusted gold price was found to be 0.9081. The projected gold price for 2026 is $2,530.91 per ounce, and for 2027, $2,570.78 per ounce.

The Integral Origin of Stochastic Processes

확률과정의 본래 형태가 적분미분방정식임을 구성적으로 보인 논문이다.

Occhionero shows the native object of a stochastic process is an integro-differential equation, not a PDE.

Giulio Occhionero

  • 블랙-숄즈식 2차 편미분 방정식은 경로 연속성을 가정한 절단본이다
  • 랜덤워크에서 마스터 방정식을 유도하고 크라머스-무얼 전개로 되짚는다
  • 파울라 정리상 3차 절단은 확률밀도를 음수로 만든다
  • 복합 푸아송 과정에서 음의 밀도를 수치로 확인한다
방법
마스터 방정식과 크라머스-무얼 전개, 파울라 정리를 엮고 디랙 델타 기반 범함수로 합·곱·비율의 밀도를 적분 연산자로 유도한다.
데이터
이론 연구다. 복합 푸아송 과정 수치 예시만 든다.
시사점
점프가 큰 자산이나 조건부 청구권을 다룰 때 확산 근사 대신 점프 적분항을 살린 밀도 모형으로 꼬리 위험과 옵션 가격을 다시 계산해볼 만하다.
적용도 2/5
매매 규칙이 없는 순수 이론이지만 확산 가정을 정면으로 부정해 무엇을 믿지 말아야 할지 알려준다. 데이터는 아예 필요 없어 확보 난이도는 없고, 대신 구현 부담이 전부 모델링 쪽에 있다.
  • Black-Scholes is a truncation that presumes continuous sample paths
  • Derives the master equation, then rebuilds the PDE route from it
  • Pawula: truncating at third order breaks density positivity
  • Numerical compound Poisson case shows the negative density
Method
Master equation plus Kramers-Moyal expansion and Pawula's theorem, with Dirac-delta functionals generating integral operators for sums, products and ratios.
Data
Theory only, with one numerical compound Poisson illustration.
Use
Reprice tail risk and contingent claims on jumpy assets with a jump integral term instead of a diffusion approximation.
Score 2/5
Pure theory with no trading rules, but it attacks the diffusion assumption behind standard pricing, so it tells you what not to trust; data availability is a non-issue since none is needed, and all the cost sits in modelling.
integro-differential equationmaster equationKramers-Moyal expansionPawula theoremjump processesBoltzmann collision integral
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTThis paper argues, and demonstrates constructively, that the natural mathematical object describing a stochastic process is an integro-differential equation rather than a partial differential equation. We begin from the elementary random walk, reconstructed as a sequence of additions of random variables, and show that the integral operator is present in the very first step of the derivation: it is not introduced as a technique, an approximation, or a generalization, but arises directly from the requirement that probability be conserved over all possible antecedents of a state. Passing to continuous time yields the master equation, which is local in time and nonlocal in space. We then reconstruct, step by step, the route by which the familiar partial differential equations of the field are obtained from it: the Kramers-Moyal expansion converts the integral operator into a differential operator of infinite order, and truncation at second order produces the Fokker-Planck equation and, in the financial setting, the Black-Scholes equation. We identify precisely the condition under which this truncation is exact, namely the vanishing of all jump moments above the second, which is equivalent to the continuity of sample paths. We then recall Pawula's theorem, which states that the expansion may terminate after the second term or must contain infinitely many terms, so that no intermediate truncation preserves the positivity of the probability density. This closes the space of possibilities: a stochastic process is described either by a second-order partial differential equation, at the price of assuming continuous paths, or by the full integral operator. We illustrate the theorem numerically for a compound Poisson process, exhibiting explicitly the negative density produced by third-order truncation. A second and independent source of integral operators is then developed: whenever a quantity is a function of random variables, its density is obtained through a generalized functional built on the Dirac delta, as introduced in The Boltzmann Equation in Finance [2]. Sums, products and ratios all generate integral operators, and contingent claims generate probability atoms that no diffusion operator can produce from smooth data. Allowing the population to act as its own environment renders the operator quadratic and yields the Boltzmann collision integral, within which diffusive spreading and concentration coexist, admitting non-trivial steady states unavailable to a translation-invariant Fokker-Planck equation. We close by distinguishing integration over state space, which produces jumps, from integration over time, which produces memory.

Calendar Congestion and Trading Responses: Cornerstone Lock-up Expirations in Hong Kong IPOs

Tests whether same-day clustering of Hong Kong cornerstone lock-up expirations changes the volume response.

Fuli Yang

  • 락업 해제일은 몇 달 전에 확정돼 예측 가능하다
  • 홍콩 IPO 코너스톤 해제 226건의 이벤트 윈도우 분석
  • 혼잡일 계수 0.432, 해제 규모와의 교호항은 -0.683
  • 거래량만 버티고 가격·변동성 효과는 살아남지 못했다

데이터
2022~2025년 홍콩 상장 코너스톤 해제 이벤트 504건 명부에서 뽑은 226건의 완전한 이벤트 윈도우다.
시사점
해제일 캘린더를 미리 만들어두고 혼잡일에 걸린 소형 해제 종목은 거래량 급증 기대를 낮춰 잡는 정도로 쓴다.
적용도 2/5
홍콩 IPO 목논서에서 코너스톤 락업 조건을 일일이 추출해야 해 데이터 확보가 번거롭고 가격 효과도 확인되지 않아 2점이다.
  • Lock-up release dates are fixed months ahead and predictable
  • 226 complete event windows, 2022-2025 HK listing cohorts
  • Crowded-session coefficient 0.432; interaction -0.683

Method
OLS on log(1 + event-session volume over the prior 20-session mean), with a crowded-session dummy interacted with log release strength.
Data
A frozen census of 504 eligible Hong Kong cornerstone release events, 2022-2025 listings, yielding 226 usable windows.
Use

IPO lock-up expirationcornerstone investorsevent clusteringabnormal volumeHong Kong equitiesmultiple-testing correction
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTContractual lock-ups make previously restricted shares eligible to trade on dates fixed months in advance. This paper asks whether the trading response to a Hong Kong IPO cornerstone release depends on other releases reaching the market on the same session. Congestion is formed from a frozen pre-outcome census of 504 eligible release events; the focal sample contains 226 complete event windows for 2022-2025 listing cohorts. The primary equal-event specification models log(1 + event-session volume relative to the preceding-20-session mean), retains 11 zero-volume sessions, and controls for market turnover, issuer size, and pre-event liquidity. The crowded-session coefficient is 0.432 (event-session clustered p = 0.010) and its interaction with log(1 + release strength) is-0.683 (p = 0.018). Event-week clustering, wildcluster bootstrap inference, month-constrained calendar randomization, alternative same-session congestion measures, placebo dates, and joint omission of the most crowded sessions support the negative interaction. The result is scale-sensitive: asinh and positive-only log outcomes agree, but an untransformed volume-ratio model and a Poisson pseudo-maximum-likelihood share-flow model do not. Holm adjustment across volume, return, and volatility interactions gives p = 0.054; neither price nor range volatility is robust. The evidence therefore describes a typical-event transformed-volume pattern, strongest among smaller releases. It does not establish aggregate share-flow pressure, price dislocation, or a causal market-capacity mechanism.

Persistence Without Predictive Advantage: Sponsor Histories and Cornerstone Adoption in Hong Kong IPOs

Sponsor track records persist but add nothing to out-of-time forecasts of cornerstone adoption.

  • 스폰서 이력이 코너스톤 도입을 미리 맞추는지 검증
  • 930건 IPO를 상장일 순서대로 확장창 예측
  • 스폰서 순위는 반기별로 유지되나 AUC는 오히려 하락
방법
선행 거래 5건 이상 스폰서의 팀 도입 점수를 만들고 선형확률모형과 wild-cluster 부트스트랩, 확장창 예측으로 검증한다.
데이터
2018년~2026년 8월 홍콩 메인보드 영업회사 IPO 930건, 스폰서 관계 1,524건, 스폰서 113곳을 쓰고 2020-2023년과 2024-2026년으로 나눠 시점 외 검증을 한다.
시사점
코너스톤 참여 예측 모델을 짤 때 스폰서 이력 변수는 빼도 된다는 근거로 쓴다.
적용도 2/5
스폰서-발행사 관계 1,524건을 투자설명서에서 직접 뽑아야 하고 매매 규칙도 없어 2점이다.
  • Tests whether repeat-sponsor histories forecast cornerstone use
  • Expanding-window prediction on 930 HK Main Board IPOs
  • Pooled AUC falls 0.691 to 0.682 when history is added
Method
Builds a team adoption score from sponsors with 5+ strictly prior deals, then runs linear probability models with clustered and wild-cluster inference plus expanding-window AUC.
Data

Use
Drop sponsor-history features from any cornerstone-participation model; they are redundant once adoption diffuses.
Score 2/5
Score 2: sponsor-issuer relationship data must be hand-built from HK prospectuses, and the paper yields no tradable rule.
IPO sponsorscornerstone investorsHong Kong IPOsout-of-time predictionunderwriter persistenceAUC
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTDo repeated IPO intermediaries exhibit stable contracting histories, and do those histories improve genuine out-of-time prediction? We study 930 Hong Kong Main Board operating-company IPOs from 2018 through August 2026, 1,524 IPO-sponsor relationships, and 113 canonical sponsor entities. Sponsor histories use only strictly earlier listing dates. At the primary five-prior-deal threshold, the team adoption score has a raw AUC of 0.621 (year-stratified 95% bootstrap interval [0.571, 0.668]; within-year permutation p = 0.0004), while sponsor rankings persist across chronological halves (Spearman ρ = 0.610, p < 0.001). The controlled linear-probability coefficient is 0.390. Its p-value is 0.016 with leadsponsor clustered inference but 0.068 under a 4,999-draw wild-cluster bootstrap. Among single-sponsor IPOs the coefficient is 0.298 (clustered p = 0.081; wild-bootstrap p = 0.147), and omitting CICC reduces the full-team coefficient to 0.263 (p = 0.114). Expanding-window forecasts provide no stable incremental advantage: adding sponsor history changes pooled AUC from 0.691 to 0.682 (difference-0.009; bootstrap interval [-0.035, 0.017]). The difference is +0.030 in 2020-2023 but-0.040 in 2024-2026; the formal history-by-post-2024 interaction is imprecise (p = 0.660). Histories also do not predict release intensity or multi-tranche structure. Sponsor histories are persistent descriptors, but their association is dependence-sensitive and their predictive content becomes redundant as cornerstone adoption diffuses. The evidence is consistent with matching, networks, or routines rather than an exogenous sponsor effect or a portable forecasting advantage.

Perturbation Theory for Nonlinear Transient Impact

약한 비선형 충격의 최적 집행 해를 섭동전개로 푼다

Solves optimal execution under weakly nonlinear transient impact via perturbation theory.

Gordon Ritter · New York University (NYU) - Courant Institute of Mathematical Sciences

  • 선형 충격 모델은 실제 체결 비용을 잘 못 맞춘다
  • 선형 해 주변에서 ε 차수로 섭동전개해 해를 구한다
  • 완전단조 전파자에서 선형 최적해의 거래율이 항상 양수다
  • 9차까지 자르면 선형 기준 대비 오차가 약 43배 줄었다
방법
순간 충격 함수를 선형에서 매끄럽게 변형한 뒤 ε의 거듭제곱으로 최적 집행 해를 전개한다
데이터
이론 연구다. 지수합 전파자와 제곱근 충격을 수치 예제로 다룬다
시사점
대량 주문 스케줄러에서 선형 전파자 해를 그대로 쓰지 말고 섭동 보정항을 얹어 앞무겁게(frontloading) 조정한다
적용도 2/5
매매 신호가 아니라 집행 최적화 이론이라 2점이다. 전파자와 충격 지수를 맞추려면 자체 체결 기록이나 틱 단위 주문흐름이 필요해 데이터 문턱이 높다
  • Linear impact models misprice real execution costs
  • Expands the optimizer in powers of a deformation parameter ε
  • Linear optimizer has strictly positive trading rate
  • Ninth-order truncation cuts linear-baseline error ~43x
Method
Deform the instantaneous impact function smoothly away from linear, then expand the optimal schedule in powers of ε.
Data
Theory only; numerical examples use finite-sum-of-exponentials propagators and a square-root impact target.
Use
Bolt perturbative corrections onto a linear propagator schedule to frontload large parent orders more realistically.
Score 2/5
Scored 2: execution theory, not a signal, and calibrating the propagator and impact exponent needs your own fill records or tick-level order flow.
optimal executiontransient impactnonlinear impactperturbation theorypropagator modelfrontloading
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTWe study optimal execution with transient market impact when the instantaneous impact law is a weakly nonlinear deformation of the linear model, using rigorous perturbation theory. For completely monotone propagators with slippage, we show that the linear-impact optimizer has strictly positive trading rate, strengthening the nonnegativity result of Schied and Strehle (2021), and providing a necessary building block for rigorous perturbative expansions. We then consider a smooth deformation f (v; ε) of the instantaneous impact function, which reduces to linear impact at ε = 0, and develop a perturbative expansion in powers of ε. The expansion is asymptotic for smooth deformations and convergent for analytic deformations. The expansion is shown to be useful both for proving theorems, and also as a numerical tool. For propagators given by finite sums of exponentials, we prove in the weakly nonlinear regime the execution asymmetry that Curato, Gatheral, and Lillo (2017) observed numerically, and pointwise frontloading in the single exponential case. We also give a numerical example in which the square-root target is beyond the estimated radius of convergence, but the computed perturbation coefficients are still useful: ninth order truncation reduces the error of the linear baseline by a factor of about 43.
