ORIGINAL ABSTRACTBackground: Cryptocurrency is a digital decentralized currency that enables peer-to-peer transactions without the involvement of intermediaries, using blockchain technology to ensure security and transparency. These emotions can help understand when and what behavioral intentions toward cryptocurrency adoption are necessary. These insights contribute to the growing literature on fintech adoption in emerging economies and provide valuable guidance for policymakers and investors formulating cryptocurrency regulations in Nepal. Objectives: It aims to investigate the influence of perceived usefulness, perceived ease of use, trust, and perceived risk constructs attached to an individual's intention to be involved in cryptocurrency transactions. It finally aims to study the relationships among these variables and their effect on cryptocurrency adoption in the Nepal context. Methods: The study uses a quantitative descriptive design and snowball sampling alone to extract information from cryptocurrency users in Nepal. A structured online questionnaire was used to gather 272 responses and analyzed using correlation and regression to investigate the significance of the proposed hypotheses. Results: Findings show that perceived usefulness, ease, or trust significantly and positively influence behavioral intention toward cryptocurrency adoption, while perceived risk harms adoption because users worry about market volatility, regulatory uncertainty, and security threats. Despite the government's legal restrictions, many Nepalese citizens are still involved in cryptocurrency transactions, seeing it as a source of financial benefit and investment opportunity. Conclusion: The study attests that perceived usefulness, perceived ease of use, and trust motivate behavior toward adopting cryptocurrency, while perceived risk provides a barrier. This enlightens policymakers on balanced regulatory measures that address risks while encouraging innovation in digital finance. These findings would provide valuable implications for policymakers, financial institutions, and technology developers in outlining the future of cryptocurrency regulation and adoption strategies.

ORIGINAL ABSTRACTBitcoin maximalists, individuals who invest exclusively in Bitcoin and reject diversification across other cryptocurrencies, theoretically constitute a significant segment within the broader crypto investor population. This paper investigates whether these investors really differ systematically from other cryptocurrency investors in terms of financial attitudes, and socio-demographic characteristics. Using microdata from the Federal Reserve's Survey of Consumer Payment Choice (SCPC), the analysis shows that maximalists are more likely to be older-each additional year of age increases the probability of being a maximalist by about 0.3-0.4 percentage points, male, and have lower household income-being in a higher income category reduces this likelihood by roughly 1 percentage point, a profile consistent with early adoption and a stronger attachment to Bitcoin. In addition, those motivated by speculation or who have sold cryptocurrency in the past year are 11-16 percentage points less likely to be maximalists, indicating a preference for long-term holding over opportunistic trading.

adaptive liquidityredistribution capabilityrepresentation theoryinformation preorderdynamic sufficiencymarket microstructure
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTRedistribution capability is a structural component of adaptive liquidity systems, but its mathematical representation is not unique. A scalar quantity, reachable set, transition graph, transformation operator, or geometric object may preserve different information about the same underlying capability structure. This paper examines five principal mathematical realizations of redistribution capability: scalar, set-valued, graph-based, operator-based, and geometric. Relations between representations are formalized through an information preorder defined by deterministic recoverability. Mutual recoverability induces an equivalence relation, and the resulting equivalence classes form a partially ordered set under the induced information relation. The analysis distinguishes the underlying redistribution-capability structure Cσ(x) from a particular representation RA(σ, x) = ρA(Cσ(x)). For a specified class of dynamic contexts D, a dynamic response map ΨD is used to identify capability structures that are dynamically indistinguishable for the problem under consideration. The central result gives a necessary and sufficient factorization criterion for dynamic sufficiency. A representation ρ : C → Z is dynamically sufficient relative to D if and only if ΨD = Ψˆ︁ D ◦ ρ. Equivalently, every fiber of ρ must be contained within a single dynamic-equivalence class. This criterion separates generic information loss from dynamically relevant information loss. A representation may be non-injective and compress structural information while remaining dynamically sufficient, provided that it does not identify capability structures with different relevant dynamic responses. The resulting analysis provides a criterion for selecting mathematical realizations of redistribution capability relative to the system class, analytical task, and dynamic contexts under consideration. It does not assume a universal scalar realization or claim universal sufficiency of any single adaptive state representation.

ORIGINAL ABSTRACTUsing takeover announcements for unlisted targets as trigger events, we find that bidders disclose bids early if optimal to do so, and consummate bids promptly when the market reactions are favorable. We also find that bidders learn about deal-specific synergy from the market reactions, and learn more actively when the proprietary cost of early bid disclosure is high. These findings indicate that managers can obtain from market prices specific information about the real decision they face, and do so by voluntarily disclosing their planned action. The results further suggest that utilizing market feedback in timing bid consummation improves bidders’ future valuation, and market information creates value by filling a gap in managers’ private information. Our evidence points to obtaining market information as a potentially important rationale for voluntary disclosure.

ORIGINAL ABSTRACTCloud compute expenditure in enterprise data engineering environments is rarely the result of insufficient capacity-it is the result of insufficient governance. This paper presents a framework for Databricks compute efficiency and cost optimization grounded in policy-driven, workload-aware infrastructure management, and evaluates that framework against a structured review of publications and technical reports spanning 2020-2026, of which 23 met a source-quality screen (Section 2) and were retained. The framework specifies five operating principles-zero idle compute tolerance, workloadspecific compute selection, policy-enforced cluster configuration, serverless-first architecture, and full cost attribution-mapped onto a medallion (Bronze/Silver/Gold) reference architecture with a five-level organizational maturity model. Literature synthesis indicates partial, uneven support: among the retained studies reporting direct cost or resource-utilization reductions, figures cluster in roughly the 18-30% range, alongside improved pipeline reliability and compliance posture. We further report a twoyear, single-organization deployment case study-an anonymized enterprise finance data platform-in which application of the framework reduced steady-state monthly compute billing for the affected workload by approximately 80% (from in excess of $150,000 to approximately $30,000 per month), a larger reduction than the literaturereported range, which we attribute primarily to an unusually unmanaged starting baseline. We discuss the framework's alignment with, and points of departure from, the reviewed literature, and identify independent, multi-organization validation as the primary direction for future work.

ORIGINAL ABSTRACTFinancial regulators promote open data to dismantle monopolies and improve transparency. Yet financial data feeds bundle two distinct objects: information about fundamentals and about liquidity demand. In a strategic-trading model with an upstream data market, competition among data vendors broadens fundamental access and sharpens price discovery, but also induces them to disseminate order-flow signals. These reveal no fundamentals but make liquidity demand predictable, raising market makers' price impact. Hence an open-data paradox: competition can make prices more informative yet markets less liquid. We characterize an information-design remedy, selective openness: broad fundamental access with restricted order-flow data.

미국 상장 투자가능 ETF 3,215종을 2001~2026년 1,289주 구간에서 검증했고 편도 20bp 거래비용을 전 단계에 물렸다.

적용도 5/5
규칙과 리밸런싱 주기가 다 적혀 있고 ETF 가격은 누구나 구하므로 그대로 백테스트할 수 있다. 순수 모멘텀이 무가치하다는 반증도 더했다.
  • Pure ETF momentum loses to passive, so what fixes it?
  • Add technical entries, multi-horizon ranks, 144 exit rules
  • Sharpe rises above 1.3; max drawdown shrinks to -7%
  • Alpha comes from tail-risk cuts, not the signal
Method
Classic cross-sectional momentum plus technical entry filters, an eight-position multi-horizon rank with forced super-category diversification, and 144 dynamic stop-loss/take-profit exits under biweekly buys and weekly exit checks.
Data
3,215 investable U.S. ETFs over 2001-2026, 1,289 weeks, charging 20 bps per side at every stage.
Use
Bolt the exit-rule and diversification layer onto an existing momentum sleeve to cut drawdowns.
Score 5/5
Rules, universe and cadence are fully specified on public ETF prices, and it also refutes plain ETF momentum, whose Sharpe runs -0.17 to +0.22 with 69-82% drawdowns.
ETF momentumcross-sectional momentumstop-losstake-profittail risktransaction costs
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTThe relevant empirical question about momentum in exchange-traded funds (ETFs) is not whether it works, but whether simple operational rules can extract value from it. We show that pure momentum over a broad ETF universe creates no value: replicated across 3,215 investable U.S. ETFs, it delivers Sharpe ratios between −0.17 and +0.22 with drawdowns of 69% to 82%, systematically below passive investing. Yet three layers of rules available to any investor fully reverse that conclusion. We evaluate the 2001–2026 period (1,289 weeks) across the investable universe, charging a 20-basis-point transaction cost per side at every stage of the analysis. On top of replicating classic cross-sectional momentum, we add technical entry signals, a multi-horizon momentum ranking with mandatory diversification across super-categories within an eight-position portfolio, and an expanded family of 144 dynamic stop-loss and take-profit exit rules, all under an asymmetric rebalancing regime of biweekly purchases and weekly exit monitoring. The complete strategy delivers a Sharpe ratio above 1.3 and a maximum drawdown of −7%, beating passive investing by close to a full Sharpe point; the alpha concentrates in tail-risk reduction and is impaired only during the high-implied-volatility regime. The central implication for researchers and practitioners is that the value comes not from the technical signal but from the operational management layer that surrounds it.

What Can and Cannot Be Tuned in a Heuristic Equity Screen: Separable Gate Optimization, a Failed Weight Search, and an Explicit Look-Ahead Audit

휴리스틱 스크린에서 튜닝 가능한 파라미터와 불가능한 파라미터를 갈라낸 연구다.

The authors split a heuristic equity screen into parameters the data can identify and parameters it cannot.

Qiyu Zhang

  • 휴리스틱 가중치를 무작정 튜닝하면 룩어헤드가 섞인다
  • 파라미터를 가중치와 가격 기반 게이트 둘로 분리했다

  • 모멘텀 게이트 +25%→+15%, 롱숏 2.67%→4.73%

나스닥100 5팩터 합성 스크린, 2020년 8월부터 2026년 5월까지 중첩 스코어 날짜 69개(유효 독립 구간 약 23개)를 썼다.

나스닥100 가격 데이터와 표준 펀더멘털만 있으면 게이트 규칙을 그대로 재현할 수 있어 4점이다. 다만 5팩터 합성 점수의 원본 스냅샷은 공개돼 있지 않다.
  • Heuristic weights hide look-ahead from an undated snapshot
  • Split parameters: composite weights vs price-derived gates
  • Weights unidentified; NNLS puts 92% on volatility, OOS R2 -0.314
  • Momentum gate +25%→+15% lifts long-short 2.67%→4.73%
Method
They prove time-constant factors collapse into the intercept, then run NNLS, Newey-West and Hansen-Hodrick errors, Romano-Wolf stepdown, and a walk-forward grid search.
Data
A five-factor composite screen on the Nasdaq-100 across 69 overlapping score dates, 2020-08 to 2026-05, roughly 23 effective independent windows.
Use
Audit which screen parameters are point-in-time clean before tuning anything, then walk-forward only those.
Score 4/5

equity screenparameter identificationwalk-forward optimizationmultiple testing correctionlook-ahead biasNasdaq-100
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTWe report a structured negative result together with the narrow positive result that survives beside it. A fivefactor composite equity screen over the Nasdaq-100, whose weights were set heuristically, is decomposed into two disjoint parameter groups: composite weights, three of whose five factors derive from an undated fundamentals snapshot, and price-derived selection gates, which are point-in-time clean. We prove that the first group is not identified in our data: a factor constant in time contributes a time-invariant column to the design matrix and cannot be separated from the intercept by any pooled estimator. Non-negative least squares on these data place 92% of fitted weight on the volatility term and return unstable zero sets that do not coincide with the contaminated factors; we report these fits as uninformative rather than as confirmation, and out-of-sample R 2 reaches-0.314. We therefore decline to tune the weights, and confirm by direct search that no re-weighting is supportable: across 69 overlapping score dates (2020-08 to 2026-05, ≈ 23 effective independent windows), with Newey-West and Hansen-Hodrick standard errors and a Romano-Wolf stepdown correction replacing the naive paired t, no perturbation to any weight attains significance; the largest effect (removing the earnings-revision factor, t NW =-2.37) has a stepdown-adjusted p = 0.156. The second group is identifiable, and a walk-forward grid search over it produced the one defensible parameter change in this study, tightening the momentum gate from +25% to +15% and raising the full-sample longshort spread from +2.67% (t = 3.20) to +4.73% (t = 4.13), with the gate value selected in 100% of walkforward folds. A second optimization pass against the updated baseline yields no further uplift (-0.09%, t =-0.15), which we interpret as convergence rather than failure. We argue the separability diagnosis-asking which parameters the data can speak to before asking what their values should be-is the transferable contribution.

Identifying and Forecasting US Equity Market Regimes: An Unsupervised and Supervised Machine Learning Analysis of Bull and Bear Market States, 2010-2025 A Research Paper on Quantitative Finance and Market Regime Classification

미국 증시 국면을 비지도학습으로 나눈 뒤 예측·수익 가치를 검증한다.

Clusters US equity regimes, then tests whether they forecast or pay.

Devanga Goswami · National Law University Meghalaya

  • 국면 식별과 국면 예측을 흔히 같은 문제로 착각한다
  • PCA로 16개 특징을 8개 성분(92.1%)으로 줄여 K-Means로 2국면 분류
  • 지속성 벤치마크 93.55%가 랜덤포레스트 91.89%를 앞선다
  • 국면 전술 전략은 MDD만 줄고 CAGR 5.80%로 뒤진다
방법
PCA로 차원을 줄이고 K-Means로 군집한 뒤, 로지스틱·랜덤포레스트·그래디언트 부스팅으로 익일 국면을 예측한다.
데이터
2010년 10월 18일부터 2025년 12월 31일까지 미국 증시 일간 3,823 관측치를 쓰고, 시계열 순서대로 1일 앞 표본외 검증을 한다.
시사점
국면 라벨은 리스크 대시보드나 포지션 사이징 입력으로 쓰되, 익일 국면 예측 모델은 지속성 규칙 대비 우위가 없으니 버린다.
적용도 4/5
가격·VIX·거시 지표 등 공개 데이터만 쓰므로 재현이 쉽고 규칙도 명확하다. 다만 매매 성과가 매수보유에 밀려 그대로 쓸 신호는 아니다.
  • Practitioners conflate regime labeling with regime forecasting
  • PCA cuts 16 features to 8 components (92.1%), K-Means splits 2 regimes
  • Naive persistence hits 93.55% vs Random Forest 91.89%
  • Regime timing cuts MDD to -28.20% but CAGR falls to 5.80%
Method
PCA plus K-Means (checked against a Gaussian Mixture Model), then Logistic Regression, Random Forest, and Gradient Boosting for next-day regime prediction.
Data
3,823 daily US equity observations, 18 Oct 2010 to 31 Dec 2025, with chronological one-step-ahead out-of-sample testing.
Use
Use the regime label as a risk-dashboard or position-sizing input; skip the ML forecasters, since persistence beats them.
Score 4/5
Built entirely on public price, VIX, and macro data, so it replicates cheaply — but the regime strategy loses to buy-and-hold on CAGR (5.80% vs 12.68%) and Sharpe (0.546 vs 0.780).
market regime detectionK-Means clusteringPCAregime forecastingpersistence benchmarktactical asset allocation
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTThis paper investigates whether unsupervised machine learning can identify economically meaningful regimes in the US equity market and whether such regimes provide genuine forecasting or investment value. Using a daily dataset of 3,823 observations spanning 18 October 2010 to 31 December 2025, sixteen standardized market, volatility, and macro-financial features are reduced via Principal Component Analysis (eight components explaining 92.1% of variance) and partitioned using K-Means clustering, validated against a Gaussian Mixture Model. The optimal solution, selected by silhouette score, identifies two statistically and economically distinct regimes, subsequently labelled Bull / Low-Risk (n = 3,201; 83.7% of observations) and Bear / High-Risk (n = 622; 16.3%). The regimes differ significantly across mean return, volatility, VIX level, momentum, and drawdown (Welch t-tests, all p < .001; Cohen's d up to 2.94), and exhibit strong persistence, with Bull-to-Bull and Bear-to-Bear transition probabilities of 96.5% and 81.8% respectively. A chronological, one-step-ahead out-of-sample forecasting exercise compares Logistic Regression, Random Forest, and Gradient Boosting classifiers against a naive persistence benchmark ("tomorrow's regime equals today's regime"). The persistence benchmark achieved the highest out-of-sample accuracy (93.55%), exceeding Random Forest (91.89%), Logistic Regression (89.80%), and Gradient Boosting (89.01%). This finding does not support the hypothesis that supervised machine learning provides superior next-day regime forecasting relative to a simple persistence rule. A regime-conditioned tactical investment strategy (fully invested in Bull, fully divested in Bear, signal lagged by one day, 10 basis point transaction costs) reduced maximum drawdown relative to buy-and-hold (-28.20% versus-33.92%) but underperformed on both compound annual growth rate (5.80% versus 12.68%) and Sharpe ratio (0.546 versus 0.780). Forward-return analysis indicates that returns following Bear/High-Risk observations were, on average, higher than returns following Bull/Low-Risk observations at 1-, 5-, and 20-day horizons, offering a plausible explanation for the strategy's return shortfall despite its lower drawdown. The study concludes that regime identification is statistically robust and economically interpretable, but that regime identification is not equivalent to reliable regime forecasting or to superior risk-adjusted investment performance. The paper's contribution lies in this explicit separation of unsupervised classification, supervised forecasting, and economic usefulness-three distinct questions that are frequently conflated in applied machine learning finance research. A supplementary robustness check (Section XI.J), which widens the feature set to twenty-nine variables by restoring the raw price, volume, and moving-average series the main specification deliberately excludes, produces a weaker four-regime solution and a severe train/test distribution shift that collapses out-of-sample forecasting performance-a negative result that, if anything, reinforces the original feature-exclusion rationale in Section IV.

Volatility and Returns to Leveraged ETFs

레버리지 ETF의 변동성 손실은 자동이 아니라 자기상관이 좌우한다

Volatility does not mechanically hurt leveraged ETFs; serial correlation decides the sign.

Hendrik Bessembinder · W.P. Carey School of Business

  • 변동성이 레버리지 ETF 수익을 무조건 갉아먹는다는 통념을 겨냥한다
  • 상수 레버리지 전략의 기대수익을 분산비율(variance ratio)로 분해한다
  • 자기상관이 양수면 변동성이 평균 수익을 오히려 높이고 음수면 낮춘다
  • 무상관이면 변동성 효과는 0이지만 왜도가 커져 평균과 중앙값 격차는 벌어진다
방법
상수 레버리지 전략의 기대 복리수익을 수익률 시계열 공분산으로 전개해 분산비율과 연결한 해석적 도출이다.
데이터
이론 연구다. 식은 저자의 이전 워킹페이퍼 'Returns to Constant Leverage Strategies'에서 가져왔다.
시사점
레버리지 ETF 보유 여부를 기초자산의 분산비율로 판정하고 롱과 인버스의 리밸런싱 비용 비대칭을 포지션 사이징에 반영한다.
적용도 4/5
가격만 있으면 분산비율을 계산해 규칙으로 만들 수 있어 데이터 확보가 쉽지만 진입·청산 임계값은 직접 정해야 해서 4점이다.
  • Attacks the belief that volatility always drags on constant-leverage returns
  • Decomposes expected LETF returns via the return variance ratio
  • Positive serial correlation makes volatility raise average LETF returns
  • Zero serial correlation means zero effect, but skewness widens mean vs median
Method
Analytical decomposition of expected compound returns for constant-leverage strategies into return serial covariances captured by the variance ratio.
Data
Theory only; equations are lifted from the author's earlier working paper on constant leverage strategies.
Use
Gate leveraged ETF holding periods on the underlying's variance ratio, and size long vs inverse legs differently given asymmetric rebalancing costs.
Score 4/5
Variance ratios come from free daily prices, so data is trivial, but the paper sets no thresholds or holding rules — implementer's job.
leveraged ETFsvariance ratioserial correlationvolatility decayrebalancing costscompound returns
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTBoth practitioner commentary and the academic literature emphasize a negative relation between the volatility of returns and outcomes to constant leverage investment strategies such as leveraged ETFs (LETFs). I demonstrate that there is no hardwired negative relation in expectation. Rather, return serial covariances as captured by the return “Variance Ratio” are crucial; volatility increases average LETF returns in the presence of positive serial correlation and vice versa, and has no effect if returns are serially uncorrelated. In the presence of serial dependence, there are asymmetries in expected rebalancing costs across leveraged long vs. inverse strategies. However, return volatility widens the gap between expected and median compound LETF returns, because of increased skewness. The equations contained herein are extracted from a prior working paper titled "Returns to Constant Leverage Strategies: General Principles and Application to Leveraged Single-Stock ETFs."

What survives honest evaluation? Leakage-safe, search-aware assessment of LLM-driven trading strategy discovery

적용도 3/5
매매 규칙이 아니라 검증 프레임워크라 3점이며, 필요한 데이터는 시점 정합 주가 정도라 확보 난이도는 낮은 편이다.
  • LLM strategy papers report the best candidate, hiding search intensity
  • Registry-validated tools block look-ahead; trial counts deflate results
  • A leaky oracle at Sharpe 35 passes DSR and PBO completely
  • Passive benchmarks certify; all LLM strategies fail across 2 models, 5 runs
Method
An agent acts only through registry-validated tools with a look-ahead-free feature space, and every evaluation is logged to deflate Sharpe ratios by trial count.
Data
453-stock point-in-time US equity universe and a 39-ETF multi-asset universe, with transaction, impact, and borrow costs.
Use

Deflated Sharpe Ratiolook-ahead biasmultiple testingLLM strategy discoverybacktest overfittingpoint-in-time data
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTLarge language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to discover trading strategies, and much of the resulting literature shares a methodological weakness: many candidate strategies are generated, the best is reported, and neither look-ahead bias nor the intensity of the search behind the reported result is corrected for. We present a strategy-discovery system that makes both corrections structural rather than procedural. First, the agent can only act through registry-validated tools whose feature space excludes look-ahead by construction; we show that this guardrail is not redundant with statistical correction: a deliberately leaky oracle posting a Sharpe ratio of 35 survives Deflated Sharpe and probability-of-backtest-overfitting testing completely. Second, the system records every strategy evaluation its search performs and deflates all reported performance by that trial count, tracing how the best in-sample Sharpe ratio climbs with each trial while the deflation threshold, driven by the agent's own search, climbs faster. Across a 453-stock point-in-time US equity universe and a 39-ETF multi-asset universe with realistic transaction, impact, and borrow costs, honest evaluation certifies passive benchmarks (out-of-sample confidence intervals excluding zero), rejects every LLM-discovered strategy (across two frontier models, search budgets up to one hundred candidates, and five repeated runs), catching selection luck, predicted rank degradation, and out-of-sample collapse through complementary instruments, and evaluates a human trader's production rule system under identical instruments. The framework formalizes why pre-registered hypotheses earn lower evidential bars than brute search, and quantifies the sample sizes that credible certification of moderate edges actually requires.

CRSP의 S&P 500 일간 데이터 6,266일(2000년 1월~2024년 5월), 2000~2019년 학습에 2020~2024년 홀드아웃 검증이다.
시사점
알파 신호로 쓰기보다 MACD와 21일 변동성을 리스크 축소 필터로 붙이는 정도가 현실적이다.
적용도 3/5
CRSP 없이도 무료 가격 데이터로 그대로 재현되지만 저자 스스로 수익성을 부정해 매매 규칙으로는 약해 3점이다.
  • Tests whether technical-indicator ML forecasts pay in dollars
  • Blends Random Forest 60% with Gradient Boosting 40%
  • Cuts MAE 3% yet loses to a zero-return benchmark on OOS R2
  • Long-only rule trims drawdown but returns just 2.1% CAGR
Method
Convex ensemble of a 200-tree Random Forest and a 400-stage Gradient Boosting regressor.
Data
CRSP daily S&P 500, 6,266 days from January 2000 to May 2024; trained 2000-2019, held out 2020-2024.
Use
Use MACD and 21-day volatility as a risk-throttle overlay, not as a standalone alpha signal.
Score 3/5
Score 3: data is trivially available from free price feeds, but the authors themselves report no tradable edge.
ensemble learningrandom forestgradient boostingS&P 500 forecastingtechnical indicatorsregime dependence
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTWe test whether ensemble machine-learning models can produce economically meaningful forecasts for daily S&P 500 returns over January 2000-May 2024 (6,266 trading days; CRSP). Eight technical predictors are engineered to capture momentum and volatility structure. A Random Forest (200 trees) and Gradient Boosting regressor (400 stages) are trained on 2000-2019 and combined in a convex ensemble (60% RF, 40% GB). On the 2020-2024 hold-out sample, the ensemble improves MAE by 3% versus its constituents but fails to beat a naïve zero-return benchmark in out-of-sample R 2. A long-only rule that takes positions only when forecasts are positive reduces drawdown and annualised volatility yet delivers only 2.1% CAGR, suggesting limited net value after trading costs. Importance analysis shows MACD and 21-day volatility contribute 39% of the predictive signal, and year-by-year errors confirm strong regime dependence, especially during the COVID-19 crash and the 2022 inflation sell-off. We conclude that tree ensembles aid risk control, but consistent performance likely requires regime-aware or deep sequence approaches with macro-sentiment features.

Quantifying Market Synchronization During Financial Crises: A Rolling Z-Score Analysis of Cross-Sector Equity Correlations in U.S. Markets (1980-2025)

미국 대형주 100종목의 상관관계를 z-점수로 표준화해 위기 지표를 만들었다

Builds a standardized index of cross-sector equity correlation to flag crisis regimes.

Ioannis Karassavidis

  • 위기 때 분산투자 효과가 사라지는 현상을 수치로 잡아야 한다
  • 252일 롤링 쌍별 상관계수를 10년 롤링 z-점수로 표준화
  • 1987년 블랙먼데이, 닷컴, 2008년, 2020년 코로나에서 지수가 크게 튄다
  • 위기를 '통계상 비정상적인 동조화 국면'으로 정의한다
방법
쌍별 상관계수를 252거래일 창으로 굴리고 10년 롤링 z-점수로 표준화해 DPSI를 만든다
데이터
1980~2025년 미국 여러 섹터 대형주 100종목의 일별 수정 종가
시사점
DPSI가 높게 튀는 구간에서 레버리지와 분산 가정을 줄이는 리스크 스위치로 쓴다
적용도 3/5
일별 수정 종가만 있으면 그대로 재현되지만 매매 규칙이 없어 리스크 필터 설계를 따로 해야 한다
  • Diversification collapses in crises; needs a hard number
  • 252-day rolling pairwise correlations, 10-year rolling z-score
  • 1987, Dot-Com, 2008-09 and COVID-2020 all spike sharply
  • Defines a crisis as statistically abnormal synchronization
Method
Rolling 252-day pairwise correlations, standardized by a ten-year rolling z-score into the DPSI.
Data
Daily adjusted closes for 100 large-cap U.S. equities across sectors, 1980-2025.
Use
Use DPSI spikes as a risk switch to cut leverage and stop trusting diversification assumptions.
Score 3/5
Data is free daily adjusted closes, but the paper gives no entry or exit rules, so the risk overlay is yours to design.
cross-sectional correlationmarket synchronizationrolling z-scoresystemic riskdiversification breakdowncrisis indicator
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTFinancial crises are often followed up by a breakdown in diversification, as assets that typically behave independently begin to move in tandem. In this study, a financial crisis is defined as a period of statistically abnormal market synchronization, characterized by strong correlations among a broad cross-section of equities rising significantly above historical norms. To quantify this phenomenon, the Diversified Portfolio Synchronization Index (DPSI) is developed using daily adjusted closing prices for 100 large-cap U.S. equities across multiple economic sectors from 1980 to 2025. Pairwise correlations in a rolling 252-tradingday window are calculated and then standardized using a ten-year rolling z-score approach to determine unusual phases of high synchronization. The resulting index reveals a strong relationship between systemic market stress and rising cross-sectional correlation. Major historical crises, including the 1987 Black Monday crash, the Dot-Com Bust, the Global Financial Crisis of 2008-2009, and the COVID-19 market crash of 2020, feature large positive deviations from the average value of synchronization over a long period of time. These findings indicate that periods of financial distress are consistently accompanied by significant reductions in diversification benefits as market participants tend to respond similarly to common macroeconomic and financial shocks. This study discusses and develops a framework to measure cross-sectional market synchronization around major U.S. equities. Using raw correlation dynamics, the DPSI is built as a standardized index to serve as an efficient tool to monitor financial stability and abnormal market conditions.

Half the Signal: Retail Attention and the Price of Volatility

레딧 개인투자자 관심도가 옵션에 절반만 반영된다는 사실을 보인다.

Reddit attention predicts next-week volatility that option prices only half price in.

Pouya Behmaram

  • 옵션 시장이 개인 관심도를 얼마나 선반영하는지 묻는다
  • 레딧 댓글 8,670만 건으로 종목별 주간 관심도를 만든다
  • 변동성 예측력의 절반이 내재변동성 통제 후에도 남는다
  • 흡수된 절반은 등가격 기간구조로 흐르고 스마일은 아니다
방법
레딧 댓글에서 뽑은 관심도를 옵션 표면 전체와 비선형 모형까지 통제해 회귀한다.
데이터
레딧 댓글 8,670만 건과 미국 주식 옵션 표면을 쓰며, 표본 구간은 밝히지 않는다.
시사점
관심도 잔차를 변동성 예측 모형에 얹어 스트래들·분산 스왑의 매수·매도를 가른다.
적용도 3/5
신호 정의가 명확하고 옵션 데이터도 표준이지만, 레딧 댓글 8,670만 건을 직접 수집·정제해야 해 3점이다.
  • Asks whether options already price retail crowding
  • Builds weekly attention from 86.7M Reddit comments
  • Half the predictive power survives the full IV surface
  • Absorbed half sits in ATM term structure, not the smile
Method
Regress realized volatility on Reddit attention while controlling for the full implied-vol surface, including nonlinear fits.
Data
86.7 million Reddit comments matched to US equity option surfaces; the abstract names no sample window.
Use
Add the unabsorbed attention residual to a vol forecast, then trade straddles or variance swaps against implied.
Score 3/5
Score 3: the signal is clear and option data is standard, but you must scrape and clean 86.7M Reddit comments yourself.
retail attentionimplied volatilityReddit sentimentvolatility forecastingterm structureoptions
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTRetail investors reveal in public which stocks they are crowding into, and those stocks become more volatile the following week. Option prices, the market's forecast of that volatility, absorb only about half the signal. Measured from 86.7 million Reddit comments, attention keeps half its predictive power after the full surface is observed, and nonlinear readings do not close the gap. The absorbed half runs through the at-the-money term structure, not the smile, which attention nonetheless reshapes. The unabsorbed half improves volatility forecasts out of sample. Controlling for implied volatility does not control for what retail attention predicts.

Does machine learning beat the market by trading fast or by waiting? Evidence from Bursa Malaysia

ML 포트폴리오 수익을 보유 기간별로 쪼개 알파의 출처를 찾았다

Splits an ML portfolio's returns by movement duration to locate its alpha.

Ahmad Shauqi bin Haji Mohamad Zubir, Wan Nurul Nisrin binti Wan Mohd Zamani · and affiliation not provided to SSRN

  • 연율 수익률만으로는 알파가 어느 속도에서 나오는지 모른다
  • 일별 수익을 2일~128일 이상 시간 층으로 분해했다
  • 6개월 초과 구간은 그냥 시장 추종이었다
  • 분기~반기 층은 대형주 지수 베타 0.09로 거의 독립이다
방법
저장된 보유 종목에서 일별 수익을 복원한 뒤 지속 기간별 층으로 나누고 층마다 벤치마크 5개와 글로벌 요인 4개에 회귀했다
데이터
2022~2025년 부르사 말레이시아 ML 포트폴리오의 보유 기록과 말레이시아 지수 5개, 글로벌 시장 요인 4개
시사점
기존 전략 수익을 기간별로 쪼개 시장 추종 층과 진짜 독립 층을 갈라내는 성과 진단 도구로 쓴다
적용도 3/5
매매 규칙이 아니라 사후 분해 기법이라 전략화하려면 설계가 더 필요하고, 대상 ML 포트폴리오의 보유 기록은 저자 고유 자산이라 외부에서 못 구한다
  • Annualized return hides which horizon actually earns the money
  • Rebuilds daily returns from holdings, layers them 2 to 128+ days
  • Beyond six months the strategy just rides the Malaysian market
  • Quarter-to-half-year layer: beta 0.09, survives trading costs
Method
Rebuild daily returns from stored holdings, decompose into duration layers, regress each layer on 5 Malaysian indices and 4 global forces.
Data
One Bursa Malaysia ML portfolio's holdings, 2022-2025, plus 5 Malaysian benchmark indices and 4 global market factors.
Use
Use it as a diagnostic: split your own strategy's returns by horizon to separate beta-riding layers from genuinely independent ones.
Score 3/5
A post-hoc attribution method, not a trading rule, and the specific ML portfolio's holdings are proprietary to the authors, though the technique applies to any strategy whose daily returns you own.
wavelet decompositionholding horizonbeta decompositionBursa Malaysiamachine learning portfoliomarket neutrality
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTMost machine learning trading strategies are judged by their annualized return, that is, the average gain or loss over a year. That figure says nothing about the speed of the price movements that produce it. We take a machine learning portfolio built for Bursa Malaysia, rebuild its daily returns exactly from its stored holdings over 2022 to 2025, and split those returns into layers by how long each movement lasts, from two-day fluctuations to trends beyond 128 trading days. We then compare every layer with five Malaysian benchmark indices and four global market forces. The results show two distinct parts. At horizons beyond six months, it simply rides the Malaysian market. At the one-quarter to half-year horizon it moves almost independently of the large-cap index, with a beta of 0.09, and this independent layer is unchanged after trading costs. Its co-movement with the market is confined to crisis episodes, and no global force tracks it.

The authors build a time-varying return-CVaR optimizer on a dynamic R-Vine copula.

Zhan Chen, Dongqun Ma, Na Li, Jing He · Southwest Jiaotong University, affiliation not provided to SSRN , Southwest Petroleum University and affiliation not provided to SSRN

  • 정적 최적화는 위기 국면의 꼬리 전염을 못 담는다
  • 동적 R-Vine 코퓰라로 세 시장의 의존구조를 추정한다

  • 정적 모형·동일가중 대비 위험조정수익과 하방위험이 나아졌다
방법
동적 R-Vine 코퓰라로 추정한 위험 전염 경로를 수익-CVaR 이중목적 최적화에 넣는다.
데이터
에너지·주식·외환 세 시장의 수익률을 쓰지만 표본 구간과 종목은 초록에 없다.

적용도 3/5
가격 데이터만 있으면 재현할 수 있으나 비네 구조 선택과 리밸런싱 규칙을 직접 설계해야 해 3점이다.
  • Static optimizers miss crisis-period tail contagion
  • Dynamic R-Vine copula maps energy-equity-FX dependence
  • Weights rebalance against a return vs CVaR trade-off
  • Beats static and equal-weight on risk-adjusted and downside metrics
Method
A dynamic R-Vine copula feeds estimated contagion channels into a bi-objective return-CVaR optimizer.
Data
Energy, equity and FX market returns; the abstract names no sample period or instruments.
Use
Bolt it on as the risk module of a multi-asset book to cut downside exposure in stress regimes.
Score 3/5
Standard price data makes it reproducible, but vine structure selection and rebalancing cadence are left open, so 3.
R-Vine Copulatail dependenceCVaRdynamic portfolio optimizationrisk spillovermulti-asset allocation
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTFrequent geopolitical shocks and swings in global energy prices have strengthened cross-market connectedness and tail risk spillover among energy, equity and foreign exchange markets, which suggests that conventional static portfolio optimization may fail to adapt to intricate market conditions. This paper establishes a time-varying bi-objective portfolio optimization framework built upon the dynamic R-Vine Copula model. Empirical evidence points to notable lower-tail dependence across the three markets; the magnitude of risk spillovers appears to surge amid crisis episodes such as geopolitical clashes and global monetary tightening cycles. By embedding dynamic risk contagion channels into portfolio decision rules, the proposed model allows for adaptive weight rebalancing that seeks a trade-off between return maximization and CVaR minimization. Based on the evaluation metrics adopted in this research, the dynamic optimization strategy tends to deliver better performance relative to static specifications and equal-weighted benchmark portfolios in respect of risk-adjusted returns and downside risk containment. The present work may offer viable methodological references for constructing cross-market dynamic portfolios, and its findings could provide tentative practical insights for investors conducting multi-asset allocation and managing cross-market systemic risks.

RetailAgent: Structured Adverse Timing in Self-Conditioned Multimodal LLM Trading Agents

LLM 매매 에이전트가 구조적으로 나쁜 타이밍을 반복한다는 사실을 밝힌다.

LLM trading agents systematically pick the wrong intervals to hold stock.

Yupeng Zhang, Liuyuan Jiang, Hongyi Huang, Bingheng Li, Lisha Chen

  • LLM 에이전트의 매매 결정에 예측 가능한 방향성이 있는지 묻는다
  • 익명 처리한 장중 가격을 주고 롱/현금을 반복 선택하게 한다
  • 롱 비중을 맞춰 비교하니 타이밍 점수가 계속 마이너스다
  • 행동 순서를 섞으면 효과가 크게 줄어 정렬이 원인임을 보인다
방법
RetailAgent라는 실험 틀에서 멀티모달 LLM에 장중 가격 이력과 상태를 주고 롱/현금을 반복 선택시킨다.
데이터
익명 처리한 개별 종목의 장중 가격 이력을 쓰지만, 종목·기간·표본 규모는 초록에 없다.
시사점
LLM 에이전트를 신호원으로 쓰기보다, 상대편 에이전트의 예측 가능한 패턴을 역이용하는 연구 출발점으로 삼는다.
적용도 2/5
장중 가격은 구하기 쉽지만 LLM 추론 비용과 미공개 프롬프트·종목 구성 탓에 재현이 어렵고, 매매 규칙 자체가 없어 2점이다.
  • Asks whether LLM agent policies carry exploitable directional structure
  • LLM sees anonymized intraday prices, picks long or flat each interval
  • Exposure-matched timing stays negative across models, horizons, states
  • Shuffling the action sequence kills the effect, so alignment drives it
Method
RetailAgent feeds anonymized intraday price histories plus allowed state to multimodal LLMs that repeatedly choose long or flat.
Data
Anonymized intraday equity price paths; the abstract names no tickers, sample size, or date range.
Use
Treat it as a starting point for fading predictable LLM-agent flow, not as a signal to trade directly.
Score 2/5

LLM trading agentsmarket timingintraday equitymultimodalbehavioral financeagent predictability
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTIn financial markets, a sequential policy that reacts systematically to price movements may become predictable to other market participants. This paper studies whether large language model (LLM) agents exhibit such directional structure through RetailAgent, an experimental framework in which an LLM observes anonymized intraday equity price histories and permitted state, then repeatedly chooses long (hold the stock) or flat (stay out) before the subsequent interval return is revealed. We compare returns during long and flat intervals along the same stock's intraday path after removing the overall fraction of long decisions. This exposure-matched measure reveals persistent negative timing across modality, horizon, state, and model family. Shuffling saved action sequences substantially attenuates the effect, showing that alignment between actions and subsequent returns drives the negative score. Feeding self-authored memories into decisions further increases policy persistence, while timing becomes more negative among stock-days on which the agent uses both actions. These results reveal stable, recoverable directional structure in sequential LLM financial decisions and a behavioral signal for studying how another participant could respond to a predictable policy.

ORIGINAL ABSTRACTHow markets perceive risk in announced M&A is difficult to measure, yet these perceptions are central to bidder valuation and deal outcomes. We address this problem using the Q&A sections of 3,465 M&A announcement conference calls. Analysts' questions are promising because they reveal the transaction-specific concerns that remain after management presents the deal. Using generative AI, we interpret these questions in context and construct a deal-level measure of analyst risk perception (ARP). ARP is higher in more uncertain and complex deals, predicts poorer completion outcomes and lower bidder valuation, and identifies when top-tier advisors add value: bidder gains are higher with top-tier advisors when ARP is high, but not when perceived risk is low. ARP also adds information beyond dictionary-based risk indices and pretrained financial-sentiment measures, showing that contextual analysis of analyst questions captures deal-specific risk signals missed by conventional text methods.

Balance-Sheet Drivers of Bank Resilience Across Five Systemic Shocks: A Cross-Country, Two-Window Event Study

Tests whether pre-shock bank fundamentals predicted stock resilience in five crises.

Seyedali Shams

  • 은행 펀더멘털이 위기 때 주가를 지켜주는지 불분명하다
  • 5개 충격, 19개국 172개 은행의 누적초과수익률을 회귀
  • 충격 구간은 조달구조, 회복 구간은 유동성과 자본이 유리
  • 순이자마진 부호는 국면마다 뒤집힌다

국가별 시장모형으로 누적초과수익률을 뽑고 후보 53개 변수를 교차검증 LASSO로 추린 뒤 국가 고정효과 OLS를 돌린다

시사점

적용도 2/5
은행 재무제표와 주가는 구하기 쉽지만 충격 사건을 사전에 알아야 성립하는 사후 분석이라 규칙화가 어렵다
  • Unclear which balance-sheet traits protect bank equity in stress
  • 172 banks, 19 countries, five shocks, CAR regressions
  • Impact windows favor funding usability; recovery favors liquidity, equity

Method
Country-market-model CARs regressed on fundamentals picked from 53 candidates by cross-validated LASSO, then post-selection OLS with country fixed effects and clustered errors.
Data
172 listed banks in 19 economies across COVID-19, the Russia-Ukraine invasion, the June 2022 tightening repricing, SVB, and the April 2025 tariff repricing, split into impact and recovery windows.
Use
Tilt a bank long-short book toward funding stability and direct equity measures when a systemic shock hits.
Score 2/5
Bank financials and prices are easy to source, but the analysis is event-conditional and associative, so it yields no standalone entry rule.
event studycumulative abnormal returnsbank fundamentalsLASSOsystemic shockscross-sectional regression
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTThis paper examines whether pre-shock bank balance-sheet characteristics explain differences in marketassessed resilience across five systemic shocks: COVID-19, the Russia-Ukraine invasion, the June 2022 inflation and monetary-tightening repricing, the Silicon Valley Bank (SVB) turmoil, and the April 2025 U.S. tariff repricing. The sample contains 172 listed banks from 19 major economies. Resilience is measured by cumulative abnormal returns from a country-market model, with separate impact and recovery windows. Cross-sectional regressions use pre-event bank fundamentals selected from 53 candidate variables through cross-validated LASSO and multicollinearity screening, followed by post-selection OLS with country fixed effects and country-clustered standard errors. The evidence is heterogeneous but economically interpretable. Impact windows place greater weight on funding usability, liability structure, and income mix; recovery windows more often reward conventional liquidity, direct equity, recognised loss absorption, and earnings rebuilding. Net interest margin is regime dependent, turning positive during the June 2022 repricing but negative during the COVID-19 impact window and the war recovery window. Broad risk-weighted capital is less stable than higher-quality or more direct equity measures in acute stress. The results are associative, not causal, and conventional significance levels should be read cautiously because the models are selected from the same data and inference relies on 19 country clusters.

ORIGINAL ABSTRACTA fund can look greener without its manager shifting the portfolio toward greener firms. Independent measures agree that U.S. mutual fund carbon intensity fell by more than a third, yet portfolio reweighting explains only 1.2 percent. Most of the decline instead comes from changing issuer emissions, sales denominators, estimates, and coverage. These inputs reverse the institutional sector trend and reshuffle manager rankings. Attribution replaces two in five managers selected for a green mandate. The replacements subsequently trade greener than those they displace. Carbon scores measure portfolio exposure; attribution reveals who deserves credit for changing it.

Passive Investing and the Supply of New Shares

Passive inflows redirect new share supply toward firms with real growth opportunities.

Pouya Behmaram

  • 패시브 유입이 신주 공급을 늘리는지 자체가 불분명했다
  • 고객사 시장의 연방정부 구매 증가로 실물 기회를 식별한다

  • 기회가 오면 현금 조달용 신주로 공급이 이동한다
방법

  • Unclear whether passive inflows expand corporate share supply
  • Instruments opportunity with federal purchases in customers' markets
  • Inflows raise no offerings on average across firms

passive investingindex fundsequity issuanceshare supplygovernment purchases instrumentcorporate financing
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTPassive inflows do not make firms issue equity indiscriminately. They redirect corporate share supply toward firms with real opportunities. I isolate those opportunities using federal purchasing growth in markets served by a firm's customers, which raises the firm's own sales. Index funds mostly get their shares from compensation and other transactions that bring the firm no money, and inflows raise no offerings on average. When a real opportunity arrives, supply shifts toward primary shares that raise cash. Firms already indexed issue far less against the same opportunity. Indexed ownership decides which opportunities get financed with new equity.

ORIGINAL ABSTRACTWe develop a simple and replicable five-variable model that identifies index-based investors with 98% accuracy. Our analysis shows that index ownership grew from 3.0% of global market capitalization in 2004 to 16.9% by 2023, driven by growth in explicit indexing. By the end of 2023, index-based investors own 10 percent or more of 5,300 firms globally. In the U.S., mid-caps have more index ownership than large-caps. In Asia and Europe, index ownership increases with firm size, and only the largest firms experience significant index ownership. Differences in market capitalization and free float help explain regional variations in index ownership.

institutional site visitspre-announcement driftprofit distributioninsider sellingChina A-sharesinformation leakage
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTInstitutional site visits enable investors to obtain incremental information beyond routine public disclosures and engage directly with corporate managers. This study posits that site visits may facilitate the early transmission of event-related information to the market, thereby contributing to price reactions before major corporate announcements. Using mandatory disclosures of site visits in China, this study finds that firms receiving site visits experience higher cumulative abnormal returns before profit distribution announcements, consistent with the view that site visits transmit information to the market before formal disclosure. The incremental pricing effect of site visits, however, weakens when event signals become stronger. Investor attention further facilitates the pricing of site-visit-related information, whereas different sources of information complexity either strengthen this pricing role in high-signal payout events or weaken the general transmission of site-visit information. Further trading evidence shows that major shareholders and executives are more likely to reduce their holdings when their trading operations are preceded by high-signal payout events with prior site visits, and that mutual funds affiliated with the corresponding site-visit institutions exhibit staged holding adjustments around these events. Overall, the evidence suggests that site visits provide an observable channel through which incremental information enters prices before formal disclosure, although this pricing role varies with event signal strength and information-processing conditions.

ORIGINAL ABSTRACTThis article examines three structural crises that the nation-state confronts simultaneously in the twenty-first century: demographic ageing and the unsustainability of social security systems; labour-market transformation driven by automation and artificial intelligence; and climate-induced food insecurity and migration. Drawing on recent institutional and academic literature (2018-2026), the study argues that these crises are not merely concurrent but mutually reinforcing through feedback loops that generate a spiral, placing the classical nation-state / welfare-state model under a performance-legitimacy crisis. The article proposes a four-layer model of "regulatory stakeholder capitalism" that moves beyond voluntary stakeholder capitalism by incorporating a state-capacity filter, SME proportionality, regulatory-arbitrage safeguards, and an inter-stakeholder prioritisation mechanism. Prepared as a descriptive-critical literature review, the paper advances five testable hypotheses and outlines a three-stage research programme intended to ground future quantitative inquiry. The contribution lies in integrating demographic-fiscal, technological and climatic pressures into a single institutional-response framework that treats state capacity as a central filter rather than a residual variable.

ORIGINAL ABSTRACTUnpacking Perpetual Decentralized Exchanges examines how decentralized finance is reshaping market structures beyond access, focusing on the design of systems that balance openness, efficiency, and risk in the absence of intermediaries. It positions Perp DEXs as a critical evolution, enabling leveraged derivatives trading through non-custodial, on-chain infrastructure, and outlines their core mechanics—including liquidity models, oracle systems, funding mechanisms, liquidation frameworks, order matching, and smart contracts—across leading platforms such as dYdX, GMX, Hyperliquid, and Synthetix. The analysis identifies structural challenges such as fragmented liquidity, oracle dependency, governance centralization, and user-level risk exposure, while evaluating emerging pathways including cross-chain liquidity aggregation, diversified oracle systems, DAO-based governance, and AI-driven optimization. The report ultimately frames Perp DEXs as a foundational layer of decentralized financial markets, where sustained progress will depend on aligning technical innovation with robust system design, resilient infrastructure, and stable market dynamics.

The asymmetrical acquisition of information about the range of asset value in market

Models how asymmetric access to an asset's payoff bounds reshapes liquidity and price sensitivity.

jianhao su, yanliang zhang, dongguang zhang · Shandong Normal University, Shandong University of Finance and Economics and Shandong University of Finance and Economics

  • 기존 정보 비대칭 모형은 신호의 정확도만 다뤘다
  • 가치의 상한·하한이라는 '범위 정보'를 새로 정의했다
  • 합리적 기대(rational expectation) 균형 모형으로 분석했다
  • 거친 범위 정보만 가진 투자자는 아예 거래를 접는다
방법
max-min 모호성 회피(ambiguity aversion) 기준을 넣은 합리적 기대 균형 모형을 세워 균형 가격과 거래량을 푼다
데이터
이론 연구
시사점
정보 비대칭이 큰 종목에서 유동성 공급자의 호가 스프레드가 왜 벌어지는지 해석하는 틀 정도로만 쓸 만하다
적용도 1/5
거래 규칙이 하나도 없는 순수 이론이고 모형 속 '범위 정보'는 시장 데이터로 관측할 방법조차 없어 1점을 줬다
  • Prior asymmetry models focus on signal precision, not bounds
  • Defines range information: the payoff's upper and lower limits
  • Solves a rational expectations equilibrium with ambiguity aversion
  • Traders holding only coarse bounds stop trading entirely
Method
A rational expectations equilibrium where uninformed traders optimize under a max-min ambiguity aversion criterion.
Data
Theory only
Use
A lens for reading why spreads widen in names with severe information asymmetry — no signal to trade.
Score 1/5
Pure theory with zero trading rules, and the model's range information has no observable counterpart in market data.
range informationrational expectations equilibriumambiguity aversionmarket liquidityasymmetric informationprice informativeness
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTThe information investors acquire in financial markets has various forms. We refer to range information as information about the upper and lower bound which the payoff of an asset may reach in the future. This study develops rational expectation models to explore the market impacts of investors' asymmetrical acquisition of range information. Uninformed traders are inherently unable to directly obtain the private signal held by informed traders. Our model shows that when range information is released to investors asymmetrically, uninformed traders who can only obtain rougher range information will not trade assets under the max-min ambiguity aversion criterion. Investors' asymmetrical acquisition of range information can cause that market liquidity and the sensitivity of market price to private signal vary continuously with the signal and noise trading volume. Our model also reveals that investors' asymmetrical acquisition of range information can decrease market liquidity and increase the sensitivity of price under some conditions and we also specify these conditions.

ORIGINAL ABSTRACTThe United States has increasingly leveraged the Entity List as a key instrument within its export control regime. While existing studies primarily examine the political motivations and macroeconomic consequences of these measures, we extend the literature by empirically investigating the link between U.S. Entity List targeting Chinese entities and stock price crash risk of affected Chinese firms. Utilizing a panel dataset of Chinese listed companies spanning 2012–2023, we find a positive association between U.S. Entity List sanctions and stock price crash risk. The identified channels include increased overseas supply vulnerability, weakened inventory turnover efficiency, and increased managerial risk narrative suppression. U.S. Entity List sanctions particularly increase stock price crash risk in firms with greater internal control deficiencies, lower analyst coverage, more negative investor sentiment, and longer financial disclosure delays. These findings identify targeted export control policy shocks as a novel driver of stock price crash risk, underscoring the need for policymakers to anchor market expectations and for listed firms to develop sanction-resilient governance and risk management that foster the sustainable development of financial markets.

The Tail Is the Only Signal: Flush Reversion in Equity Indices and Crypto Perpetual Futures

폭락 다음날 반등 효과는 대부분 집계 오류이고, 지수 -7% 이하만 살아남는다.

Almost all buy-the-dip reversion is a counting artifact; only index drops of 7%+ survive.

Alexandr Dashyan

  • 폭락 반등 백테스트가 같은 날짜를 중복 계산한다
  • 날짜 단위 집계, 중첩 임계값, 다중검정, 부트스트랩 보정
  • 지수 -7% 이하 50건만 다음날 평균 3.08% 수익
  • 암호화폐 무기한선물은 6년 내내 베타 차감 후 음수
방법
이벤트 스터디에 날짜 단위 집계와 familywise 보정, 짧은 블록 부트스트랩을 함께 적용한다.
데이터
미국 주가지수 4종의 76년치와 단일종목 28개 패널, 암호화폐 무기한선물 20종의 6년치를 쓴다.
시사점
지수 -7% 이하 폭락일 종가에 사서 다음날 청산하는 규칙만 남기되, 자본 배분은 극히 낮게 잡는다.
적용도 5/5
진입·청산 규칙과 임계값이 명확하고 지수 일봉만 있으면 되므로 데이터 확보가 사실상 무료다.
  • Event studies double-count one shock across instruments
  • Fix: date-level counting, nesting, familywise, bootstrap
  • Index drops ≤-7%: next day averages +3.08%, p=0.006
  • Crypto perps: net-of-beta edge negative every year
Method
One event definition plus four corrections: date clustering, nested thresholds, familywise p-values, short-block bootstrap.
Data
Four US equity indices over 76 years, a 28-name panel, and 20 crypto perpetual futures over six years.
Use
Buy the index at the close after a 7% or worse drop, exit next session, and size it as a rare overlay.
Score 5/5
Rules and thresholds are explicit and need only free daily index prices, so data availability is a non-issue.
mean reversionevent clusteringmultiple testingblock bootstrapcrypto perpetualstail risk
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTBuying an asset immediately after a large single day decline is one of the oldest ideas in short horizon trading, and it is easy to make it look profitable. This paper applies one event definition and one testing protocol to two asset classes, four US equity indices over seventy six years and, in results carried over from an earlier stage of the same research program, twenty cryptocurrency perpetual futures, and finds that almost all of the apparent effect is an artifact of how the events are counted. Large declines arrive on the same few calendar dates across instruments, so an event study that treats each instrument day as an independent observation is counting one market wide shock many times. In a panel of twenty eight single names, 1,361 declines at or below seven percent are only 791 distinct dates, and the pooled t statistic of 6.76 falls to 1.68 when each date is counted once. Three further corrections matter and are usually skipped: the depth thresholds that define such events are nested rather than independent, so a strong tail leaks into every shallower cut; testing several depths requires a familywise correction; and a stationary block bootstrap with a block length that is a large fraction of the sample is badly anti conservative, which in our own first pass manufactured a p value two orders of magnitude too small. After all four corrections, exactly one result survives in equities. Index declines at or below seven percent, 50 events on 25 dates clustering into 10 episodes since 1950, are followed by a mean next session return of 3.08 percent, with a familywise p value of 0.006 and a bootstrap interval from 1.34 to 4.84 percent. It is not crash window drift: the next session returns 3.08 percent while each of the following nine averages 0.01 percent, below the unconditional daily mean. It is implementable, since 83 percent of the move occurs after the opening print. Everything shallower is indistinguishable from zero, and the corresponding single name effect is absent. In crypto perpetuals the same mechanism fails completely, with an edge net of market beta that is negative in every regime and every year of a six year sample. The economics are the final constraint: an account trading the surviving tail signal deploys 0.05 percent of its capital time and beats Treasury bills by 0.44 points a year, which is a real effect that cannot carry a portfolio.

Adds five price-based network features that sharpen systemic tail-loss attribution.

Alberto Acedo

  • 하방 베타나 MES는 자산별 노출만 보고 전염 구조를 놓친다
  • 일별 가격만으로 삼각 구조 2개와 FOMC 반응 3개를 뽑는다
  • 최대 31개 변수 기준선 대비 out-of-fold R²를 1.3~3.4p 올린다
  • 같은 폭의 노이즈 위약 변수는 모든 설정에서 성능을 떨어뜨린다

자산별 5개 네트워크 특성(Omega Block)을 만들어 유연한 학습기에 변수로 넣는다.
데이터
두 개 유니버스와 거의 겹치지 않는 두 구간(2012-2017, 2016-2025) 일별 가격으로 검증한다.
시사점

적용도 3/5
일별 가격과 FOMC 일정만 있으면 재현할 수 있어 데이터 확보는 쉽지만, 매매 규칙이 없어 별도 설계가 필요하다.
  • Downside beta and MES miss where shocks recirculate among peers
  • Two triadic features vs a null, three FOMC response features
  • Beats up to 31-column baselines by 1.3-3.4 points of out-of-fold R²
  • A same-width noise placebo hurts the model in every configuration
Method
Per-asset triadic and event-response features fed to a flexible learner, not a hand-built index.
Data
Daily prices on two universes over two nearly disjoint windows, 2012-2017 and 2016-2025.
Use
Bolt onto a risk model to rank next-year tail-loss exposure and trim crowded, tightly co-moving names.
Score 3/5

correlation networkssystemic tail riskdownside betaconditional MEScross-sectional attributionFOMC event response
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTAttributing future systemic tail losses across assets remains dominated by exposures estimated asset by asset, market beta, downside beta and volatility, although drawdowns are precisely the episodes in which losses become collective and concentrate where shocks recirculate among tightly co-moving names. The quantities proposed here originate in our prior work on network fragility: the FSRI, a structural index for microbial co-occurrence networks in agricultural soils, and its financial descendant, the TSI, whose standalone version we falsified and reported. From that starting point we hypothesized that the same structural information could carry marginal value if localized to individual assets, extended with a response dimension, and handed to a flexible learner as features rather than composed by hand. The result is the Omega Block: five per-asset features computed from daily prices alone, two triadic quantities measured against a closed-form configuration null and three response quantities around scheduled FOMC announcements, among them Omega-R, a perturbation response normalized by each asset's own baseline drift, developed for this block. In pre-registered paired ablations with a same-width noise placebo, the block adds 1.3 to 3.4 points of out-of-fold R 2 to next-year tail-loss attribution over baselines that instantiate the public state of the art, up to 31 columns including sector, downside beta, co-moments, a conditional MES proxy, size, liquidity and balance-sheet fundamentals, replicated on two universes and two nearly disjoint periods (2012-2017, 2016-2025), while the placebo worsens the same model in every configuration. Because the baselines that decide adoption in practice are proprietary, the paper's verification is designed to travel to the data: an open acceptance harness reruns the entire protocol on any universe, baseline and event calendar, with a values-only mode and a cryptographic time-lock. The scope established by the evidence is cross-sectional attribution, not market timing.

ORIGINAL ABSTRACTWe examine whether NSE index derivatives exhibit a liquidity discontinuity at 15:00 IST, tied to mandatory broker RMS auto-square-off and, historically, the 15:00-15:30 IST closing-price VWAP window. Pre-Expiration Liquidity and Price Reactions to IPO Lockup Expirations

  • IPO 공시에서 락업 계약을 직접 수집해 사건연구를 돌린다
  • 회전율이 높을수록 누적초과수익(CAR)이 덜 하락한다
  • 유동성 효과는 해제 이후 구간에만 나타난다

  • Lockup expiration returns vary widely; what explains the spread?
  • Hand-collected lockup contracts plus a standard event study
  • Higher pre-expiration turnover means higher CAR; median CAR is negative
  • The liquidity effect shows up only after expiration, not before
Method

Use

IPO lockup expirationliquidityturnovercumulative abnormal returnevent studyTokyo Stock Exchange Mothers
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTThis study examines whether pre-expiration stock liquidity is associated with cross-sectional variation in price reactions to a potential increase in the supply of tradable shares, using IPO lockup expirations as pre-scheduled events. We construct a hand-collected dataset of lockup contracts from IPO disclosure documents for 522 IPO firms listed on the Mothers market of the Tokyo Stock Exchange between 2012 and 2021. Because lockup expiration dates are determined at the time of the IPO, this setting mitigates concerns that event timing is chosen in response to contemporaneous market conditions. We find a negative median cumulative abnormal return (CAR) around lockup expirations, and the magnitude of the reaction varies systematically with pre-expiration liquidity. Firms with higher pre-expiration turnover experience higher CAR, and this association remains significant in the joint specification. Higher Amihud illiquidity is associated with lower CAR in separate specifications, whereas wider bid-ask spreads show a marginally significant negative association; neither association remains statistically significant in the joint specification. The liquidity–CAR association is concentrated in the post-expiration window and is not statistically significant in the pre-expiration window, consistent with variation in post-expiration price reactions rather than pre-existing return trends. Overall, pre-expiration liquidity is associated with cross-sectional variation in post-expiration price reactions, with the most robust evidence coming from turnover.

ORIGINAL ABSTRACTWhen an AI system appears to generate equity alpha, five explanations compete with genuine skill: factor exposure, a weak benchmark, data leakage, model complexity, and an advantage a conventional system could replicate. We test all five on the same signal. Using the peer-reviewed replication of 212 published anomalies, language-model and agentic arms forecast which anomalies persist after their original samples end. The arms earn significant factor alpha-and none of it survives comparison with a passive portfolio of all published anomalies, which requires no model at all. Leakage does not explain the result: contamination made performance worse. The models' verbal mechanism judgements outperform their own numeric scores, but in-sample persistence absorbs them. A composite forecast scoring 0.400 owes 98.5% of that to a statistic we supplied. Apparent AI alpha here is a benchmarking artefact, not information.

ORIGINAL ABSTRACTWe survey 28 leading Swiss pension fund consultants and asset managers to understand the factors and their ranking driving institutional asset allocation decisions. The risk tolerance of pension fund boards, target returns, and risk-free rates emerge as the most influential factors, while traditional portfolio theory predictors such as expected returns, volatility or individual risk aversion show limited practical relevance. Finally, we find that individual risk aversion of beneficiaries plays a limited role. Our findings suggest that while institutional investors may overcome individual suboptimal asset allocation choices, they face distinct institutional frictions that also influence asset allocation decisions.

ORIGINAL ABSTRACTInvestment banking, shadow banking, and high-frequency trading are among those manifestations of modern finance that evoke fascination and mistrust in equal measure. Drawing on the academic literature and on current data from international institutions (Financial Stability Board, ESMA, SEC, LSEG), this paper examines what these lines of business actually are, how they function, who conducts them, and whose interests they serve. The analysis shows that all of the activities examined constitute legal, economically well-founded, and in large part regulated businesses whose questionable reputation stems chiefly from opacity, conflicts of interest, and isolated excesses. Investment banking is a highly concentrated business of a few large banks, with worldwide fee income of $60.5 billion in the first half of 2025 alone. Shadow banking, as recorded by the Financial Stability Board, comprises assets of $256.8 trillion and thus already 51 per cent of the global financial system. In high-frequency trading, roughly 55 per cent of US and up to 43 per cent of European equity trading volume is attributable to strategies whose reaction times are measured in millionths of a second; the costs associated with latency arbitrage amount to roughly five billion dollars per year worldwide (Aquilina, Budish, and O'Neill, 2022). For investors, a differentiated assessment follows: none of the businesses examined is disreputable per se, and none is free of risk. Those who understand how they work, who the participants are, and where the profits originate can weigh opportunities and dangers realistically — and may, in selected cases, participate in these markets themselves, whether through listed trading firms or through regulated credit funds.

Satellite-to-Ground Quantum Key Distribution: Simulated and Hardware-Validated Effects of Atmospheric Loss on Secure Fleet and Aircraft-to-Base Communications

위성-지상 양자키분배에서 대기 손실이 키 생성률과 오류율에 미치는 영향을 시뮬레이션과 실제 양자 하드웨어로 검증한다.

The author simulates and hardware-tests how atmospheric loss degrades satellite-to-ground quantum key distribution.

Dr. Zuhair Ahmed · Centre of Excellence for Technology Quantum and AI Canada

  • 이동 자산은 광섬유를 못 쓰니 위성 양자통신이 필요하다
  • 광자 손실과 검출기 암계수를 분리해 밀도행렬로 모의한다
  • 투과율 0.50→0.005에서 QBER이 2.6%→33.3%로 오른다
  • 손실형 열화는 도청의 급격한 오류 급등과 다르게 나타난다

적용도 1/5
물리 실험 논문이라 매매 규칙이 전혀 없고, 필요한 데이터도 위성 링크 실측과 양자 프로세서 접근이라 금융 데이터로 구할 수 없다.

  • Density-matrix sim separates photon loss from dark counts
  • Transmittance 0.50 to 0.005 drives QBER from 2.6% to 33.3%
  • Loss-driven QBER rises gradually, unlike an eavesdropper spike
Method
PKTron density-matrix simulation of BB84 satellite links, with the measurement step run on IBM's ibm_marrakesh processor.
Data

Use
Nothing tradable here; at most background on quantum-comms technology readiness if you track that equipment theme.
Score 1/5
Score 1: pure physics with no trading rules, and the inputs are satellite link telemetry and quantum processor time, not obtainable financial data.
quantum key distributionatmospheric lossQBERdark countssatellite communicationsIBM Quantum
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTSatellite-to-ground quantum key distribution (QKD) has been proposed and experimentally demonstrated (e.g., China's Micius satellite) as a means of extending quantum-secured communication beyond fiber-optic range, with direct relevance to naval fleet communications and aircraft-to-base communications where mobile assets cannot rely on fixed fiber infrastructure. Unlike this research group's companion terrestrial BB84 study, which evaluated eavesdropper detection under near-ideal channel conditions, satellite links are dominated by a different physical effect: atmospheric photon loss, driven by slant-path absorption, scattering, beam divergence, and elevation-angle-dependent atmospheric thickness. This study models that effect using PKTron's density-matrix simulator, explicitly separating two physically distinct phenomena that are frequently conflated in simplified models: heralded photon loss (which reduces raw key rate without directly causing bit errors) and detector dark counts (a roughly constant background click rate that becomes proportionally significant, and increasingly drives quantum bit error rate, QBER, as the real signal weakens). Across four simulated transmittance scenarios representing satellite pass geometries from zenith (η=0.50) to near-horizon (η=0.005), sifted key count fell from 547 to 15 bits per 2,000 transmitted qubits, while QBER rose from 2.6% to 33.3%, driven by dark counts constituting an increasing fraction (1.3% to 60.0%) of all detector clicks. Hardware validation was performed on IBM Quantum's ibm_marrakesh processor for the surviving-photon measurement step, using the same classical channel model to determine which photons survived transmission. At η=0.50, hardware measurement yielded a 0.550 click rate (matching theoretical prediction) with 12 sifted bits and 0.000 QBER; at η=0.03, a larger sample (400 transmitted qubits) yielded a 0.037 click rate (again matching prediction) with 8 sifted bits and a QBER of 0.125, consistent with the simulated prediction of elevated error rates at low transmittance. These results confirm, in both simulation and on real quantum hardware, that atmospheric loss degrades satellite QKD primarily through reduced key rate and a distinct, gradual, dark-count-driven QBER increase-qualitatively different from the sharp, immediate QBER signature of eavesdropping demonstrated in this group's companion terrestrial QKD study-a distinction with direct relevance to link budget engineering for naval and airborne quantum-secured communication systems.
abs PDF

sovereign defaultequity returnsquantitative macro modelcapital accumulationevent study
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTThis paper evaluates the extent to which standard quantitative sovereign default models exhibit equity return responses around default risk spikes comparable to the data. It finds that they largely do not. Hébert and Schreger (2017) employ a high-frequency event study around legal rulings and report a 10% increase in default risk causes a 6% decline in firms’ market value. A workhorse sovereign default model augmented with capital accumulation delivers only a 0.2% equity value drop. Three features account for the disconnect: the absence of private capital outflows and trade surpluses during default, the model’s counterfactual prediction of rising dividends, and the nominal depreciation embedded in the dollar-denominated index used in the data.

SSRNGIS27p 7363041 ★★★★★

Gravity and the Arch: Implementation Tests and Findings

손실 후 안전자산을 푸는 배분 규칙의 장기 성장률을 증명한다.

Proves the long-run growth law for a rule that spends stored safety after losses.

Tom Anderson

  • 기준 배분을 지키며 켈리로 전환하는 규칙의 성장률이 쟁점이다
  • 시뮬레이션으로 구조를 찾고 정상분포와 라플라스 변환으로 증명한다
  • 켈리 체류 비율은 p*=y0/(2-y0)로, 로그성장률은 아치 값으로 수렴한다
  • 정적 포트폴리오 대비 우위는 30/70에서 67년, 시장에서 122년만 유지된다
방법
뱅뱅(bang-bang) 부족분 규칙을 몬테카를로로 돌리고 정상분포를 닫힌 형태로 유도한다.
데이터
이론 연구다. 실데이터 없이 20년과 50년 구간을 시뮬레이션한다.
시사점
목표 수익선 유지 확률을 중시하는 계좌의 위험자산 비중 스위칭 규칙으로 쓴다.
적용도 3/5
데이터는 가격만 있으면 되지만 규칙을 실전 신호로 만들려면 설계가 더 필요해 3점이다.
  • Tests the Gravity paper's claimed recovered growth rate, the arch
  • Simulates the bang-bang shortfall rule, then proves the structure found
  • Time at Kelly converges to p* = y0/(2-y0); log-growth hits the arch
  • Edge over static portfolios lasts 67 years at 30/70, 122 at the market
Method
Monte Carlo on a bang-bang shortfall rule, plus a closed-form stationary law and Laplace transform.
Data
Theory only; simulated 20- and 50-year horizons, no market dataset.
Use
Use it as a switching rule for accounts that optimize probability of holding a target line, not growth.
Score 3/5
Only price data is needed, but the rule still needs real design work to become a signal, so 3.
Kelly criterionbang-bang allocationlong-run growthshortfall rulestationary distributiondynamic asset allocation
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTThe Gravity paper claims a recovered growth rate—the arch—for an investor who holds a baseline allocation and exercises stored safety after damage. This paper tests that claim in simulation, discovers the structure underneath it, and proves what the simulation found. The arch is an exact long-run law. Under the bang-bang shortfall rule studied here, for baselines below the market the gap to the arch line is positive recurrent with a closed-form stationary law. The fraction of time spent at Kelly converges to (p^* = y_0/(2-y_0)), and mean log-growth converges to the arch. At the market seat, the stationary law disappears and the convergence becomes degenerate. At the 20- and 50-year horizons tested, the arch is kept by both the median and the dollar mean, but not yet by mean log-wealth: the remaining shortfall is a transient whose clock is computable. Against the natural skeptic—a static portfolio at twice the baseline, or at the best static seat for the same goal—the moving portfolio loses on growth and dispersion and wins on the one thing it was built for: the probability of keeping the line. It does so with less average exposure, and for a computable number of years: 67 at 30/70, 78 at 50/50, 91 at 70/30, and 122 at the market. The crossover boundary (T^*(y_0)) is exactly characterized—the static side in closed form and the moving side by an explicit Laplace transform—and evaluated numerically as a curve in years. What remains outside the theorem—which paths fall deep, and how deep, before the law asserts itself—is the price. The price is itself a finding: exact, at a price, over time, within limits.
abs PDF

GPU 담보 대출의 구조적 위험을 개념화한 논문이다.

Defines two structural risks in GPU-collateralised lending.

Zohaan · affiliation not provided to SSRN

  • 5000억 달러 GPU 담보 금융을 기존 ABS 이론이 못 다룬다
  • 2008년 MBS와 공유하는 세 가지 조건을 지적한다

Builds a risk taxonomy and maps it onto the pre-2008 MBS analogy.

ORIGINAL ABSTRACTOn August 10, 2026, Nvidia announced memoranda of understanding with Apollo, BlackRock, Blackstone, Brookfield, Goldman Sachs, and KKR to mobilize over five hundred billion dollars of third-party capital through GPU-backed financing platforms. This paper argues that the structure introduces a category of risk that existing asset-backed securitisation theory does not formally address. I identify three conditions shared with the mortgage-backed securities market before 2008: correlated collateral values, unproven cash flow assumptions, and asymmetric incentives between originators and downstream debt holders. I then define two risk categories specific to technology asset-backed lending: Vendor-Circular Financing, which describes the structural conflict created when a manufacturer backstops credit facilities enabling purchases of its own products, and Innovation-Cycle Depreciation, which describes the systematic destruction of collateral value triggered by the vendor's own product roadmap. Secondary market data shows that H100 GPUs have lost approximately seventy-three percent of their 2023 value by mid-2026, providing preliminary empirical grounding for both risk categories.

Blockchain Adoption and Transaction Transparency in Banking: Empirical Evidence from the Industry 4.0 Paradigm

은행권 설문으로 블록체인 도입과 거래 투명성의 관계를 검증했다.

A survey of 120 bankers links blockchain adoption to transaction transparency.

Cephas Abban · University of Aberdeen

  • 블록체인이 거래 투명성을 높인다는 실증 근거가 부족하다
  • 은행 종사자 120명 설문을 상관·회귀로 분석했다
  • 상관계수 0.713, 설명력 50.8%로 양의 관계를 확인했다
  • 감사 추적과 사기 감소가 이득, 예산과 레거시가 걸림돌이다
방법

시사점
트레이딩 규칙으로 쓸 건 없고, 은행 핀테크 테마의 도입 장벽 정도만 참고한다.
적용도 1/5
가격 데이터가 전혀 없는 자체 설문 연구라 재현조차 불가능해 1점이다.

Method
Descriptive survey with Pearson correlation and simple linear regression in SPSS.
Data
