blockchain adoptionbanking transparencyIndustry 4.0survey researchregression analysis
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTBlockchain technology has emerged as a transformative innovation with the potential to enhance transparency, security, and efficiency in banking operations. However, empirical evidence on its effect on transaction transparency within the context of Industry 4.0 remains limited. This study investigates the relationship between blockchain adoption and transaction transparency in the banking sector, with particular attention to implementation practices, transparency outcomes, and the challenges influencing adoption. A quantitative descriptive research design was employed using a structured questionnaire administered to 120 banking professionals drawn from commercial, microfinance, and central banks. Data were analysed using descriptive statistics, Pearson correlation, and simple linear regression in SPSS, with the measurement instrument demonstrating good internal reliability (Cronbach's α = 0.858). The findings reveal a strong and statistically significant positive relationship between blockchain adoption and transaction transparency (r = 0.713, p < 0.001). Regression analysis further indicates that blockchain adoption explains 50.8% of the variance in transaction transparency (R² = 0.508), highlighting its substantial contribution to improving transparency within banking operations. Respondents identified enhanced auditability, fraud reduction, operational efficiency, and regulatory confidence as key benefits, while budget constraints, legacy system integration, regulatory uncertainty, and limited technical expertise were identified as major barriers to implementation. The study contributes to the growing literature on blockchain-enabled digital transformation by providing empirical evidence on the role of blockchain in enhancing transaction transparency within the Industry 4.0 paradigm. The findings offer practical insights for banking executives, policymakers, and regulators seeking to strengthen transparency, trust, and operational performance through blockchain adoption.

Option-Implied Signals and Crash Risk: Predictability and Machine-Learning Evidence from U.S. Equity Options

옵션 시장 예측력이 시기마다 달라진다는 사실을 대규모 패널로 확인했다.

Re-tests six canonical option-implied signals across three market regimes, 2015-2026.

Baichuan Li, Mengxiao Wang

  • 코로나 이전에 세운 정설이 지금도 통하는지 다시 검증한다
  • 2015~2026년을 세 국면으로 쪼개 회귀와 부스팅 트리를 돌린다
  • 스머크는 힘을 잃고 IV 스프레드와 위험중립 왜도는 살아남는다
  • AI·초대형주 국면에서만 트리 모델이 선형 모델을 이긴다
방법
여섯 개 표준 옵션 신호를 회귀로 비교하고 XGBoost로 수익률과 급락을 예측한다.
데이터
미국 주식 옵션 10,026개 종목, 1,236만 기업-일 관측치를 2015~2026년 세 국면으로 나눠 검증한다.
시사점
스머크 대신 IV 스프레드와 위험중립 왜도를 알파 신호로 쓰고, 국면 판별 장치를 반드시 붙인다.
적용도 4/5
신호 정의가 명확해 바로 구현할 수 있지만, 미국 옵션 체인 이력 데이터를 유료로 사야 해 4점이다.
  • Canonical smirk results were established pre-COVID; do they still hold?
  • Splits 12.36M firm-days into three regimes, runs OLS plus boosted trees
  • Smirk decays to -0.006 (t=-1.5) and flips positive at 3 months
  • IV spread and risk-neutral skewness stay significant everywhere
Method
Univariate and joint regressions on six option signals, plus XGBoost on IV-surface, Greeks, activity and liquidity features.
Data
12.36M U.S. equity firm-day observations, 10,026 underlyings, 2015-2026, split into 2015-19, 2020-22 and 2023-26.
Use
Drop the smirk, keep Cremers-Weinbaum IV spread and risk-neutral skew, and gate any ML overlay on a regime detector.
Score 4/5
Signals are precisely defined and reproducible, but historical U.S. option chains are a paid vendor feed, so 4 not 5.
option-implied volatility smirkIV spreadrisk-neutral skewnessregime dependencegradient boostingcrash prediction
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTWe re-estimate canonical option-implied predictability evidence using a unified 2015--2026 panel of 12.36 million U.S. equity firm-day observations across 10,026 underlyings. We split the sample into three regimes: late-post-crisis low volatility (2015--2019), high-volatility transition (2020--2022), and AI/mega-cap concentration (2023--2026). The Xing et al. (2010) smirk--return relationship weakens steadily: the next-month univariate smirk coefficient falls from $-0.023$ $(t=-5.5)$ in 2015--2019 to an insignificant $-0.006$ $(t=-1.5)$ in 2023--2026, and turns positive at the three-month horizon $(+0.016,\ t=+2.1)$. In joint specifications with all six canonical signals, the smirk is insignificant throughout and again changes sign in the latest regime. By contrast, the Cremers--Weinbaum IV spread and a Bakshi et al. (2003)-style risk-neutral skewness measure remain significant across regimes and specifications. A boosted-tree benchmark using IV-surface, trading-activity, Greeks, and liquidity features outperforms linear models for next-month return prediction only in the AI/mega-cap regime, with $R^2_{\mathrm{OOS}}=+1.29%$ versus $+0.07%$. Permutation importance identifies different leading predictors by regime, and no canonical hand-engineered signal enters the top five. For firm-level five-day crash classification, AUC is highest in calm markets $(0.706,\ \text{XGBoost})$ and lowest during the high-volatility transition $(0.561)$. Overall, option-implied predictability is real but regime-dependent, and the post-2023 AI/mega-cap period differs sharply from the pre-COVID setting in which the canonical results were established.

From electricity prices to profits: multidimensional probabilistic forecasting for BESS trading

전력 가격의 다차원 확률예측으로 배터리 저장장치 매매 수익을 직접 예측한다.

Weron and Maciejowska forecast the joint distribution of hourly power prices and turn it into a distribution of daily BESS profits.

Tomasz Weron, Katarzyna Maciejowska

  • 일중 24시간 가격의 결합분포를 제대로 잡는 방법이 부족하다
  • Multiple Split(MS)에 추정창 길이별 예측 평균을 더한다
  • MS-ave가 PICP·CRPS·에너지 스코어 모두에서 앞선다
  • 운영비용이 0이 아닐 때 매매 전략 지원 효과가 특히 크다
방법
다중 분할(Multiple Split) 기법에 서로 다른 추정창 길이의 예측을 평균해 앙상블 가격분포를 만든다.
데이터
독일·스페인 하루전(day-ahead) 전력시장의 2021~2024년 가격 자료로 검증한다.
시사점
앙상블 시나리오를 그대로 넣어 일일 수익 분포를 뽑고, 충·방전 시각 선택과 리스크 한도 설정에 쓴다.
적용도 4/5
충·방전 규칙을 수익 분포에서 바로 도출하지만 배터리 사양과 비용 가정은 직접 정해야 하며, 유럽 하루전 가격은 ENTSO-E에서 무료로 받는다.
  • Point forecasts miss the 24-hour joint price structure
  • Multiple Split method plus averaging over estimation windows
  • MS-ave wins on PICP, CRPS and Energy Score
  • Edge is largest once operational costs are non-zero
Method
Multiple Split (MS) ensemble with forecast averaging across estimation windows of different lengths.
Data
German and Spanish day-ahead electricity markets, 2021-2024.
Use
Feed the price ensemble into a storage optimizer to pick charge and discharge hours and size the risk of each day's trade.
Score 4/5
Charge/discharge rules fall straight out of the profit distribution, but battery specs and cost assumptions are yours to set; European day-ahead prices are free from ENTSO-E.
probabilistic forecastingelectricity pricesBESS arbitrageensemble forecast averagingenergy scoreday-ahead market
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTThis article examines various methods of constructingmultidimensional probabilistic forecasts of electricity prices. Building on the Multiple Split (MS) method, it incorporates forecast averaging across estimation windows of different lengths and compares its performance with that of other, well-established methods. The research demonstrates that the ensemble representation of the price distribution is particularly useful in battery energy storage system (BESS) management. It enables the direct construction of probabilistic forecasts of daily profits. These forecasts can be used to determine optimal charging and discharging hours, as well as to support risk management decisions. The methods are evaluated using data from the German and Spanish day-ahead electricity markets from 2021-2024. The results indicate that theMS method with averaging (MS-ave) generally outperforms the other considered approaches in terms of Prediction Interval Coverage Probability (PICP), the Continuous Ranked Probability Score (CRPS) and the Energy Score (ES). Moreover, it is superior in supporting BESS trading strategies, particularly in case of non-zero operational costs.

Tabular Deep Learning for Algorithmic Trading: Cross-Regime Bayesian Optimisation for Equity Signal Generation

국면별 베이지안 최적화로 주식 신호를 뽑고 XGBoost·TabNet을 앙상블한다.

Tunes equity models for cross-regime trading performance, then ensembles XGBoost with TabNet.

Joshua Le Grice

  • 기존 평가는 하이퍼파라미터 단계에서 국면 강건성을 안 본다
  • 통계적으로 다른 3개 국면의 매매 성과를 목표로 튜닝한다
  • 하이브리드 앙상블이 연 51.26%, 샤프 2.44, 알파 0.423
  • 베타가 0에 가까워 초과수익은 종목 선택에서 나온다
방법
5개 모델을 국면별 매매 성과로 튜닝하고 XGBoost와 TabNet을 순위 합산으로 묶는다.
데이터
미국 대형주 약 300종목의 11년치 일간 데이터를 쓰고 테스트 구간 4개 분기로 검증한다.
시사점
단일 딥러닝 모델 대신 부스팅 트리와 순위 앙상블을 쓰고, 튜닝 목적함수를 국면별 성과로 바꿔 쓴다.
적용도 4/5
가격·재무 데이터는 구하기 쉽고 방법도 재현 가능하지만, 대체 데이터와 세부 진입·청산 규칙은 직접 설계해야 한다.
  • Standard tuning ignores regime robustness in model selection
  • Bayesian optimisation targets returns across three regimes
  • Hybrid ensemble: 51.26% annual, 2.44 Sharpe, 0.423 alpha
  • Near-zero beta: gains come from selection, not exposure
Method
Five model classes tuned by Bayesian optimisation on regime-split trading metrics; XGBoost and TabNet merged by rank aggregation.
Data
Daily bars for ~300 US large-cap stocks over eleven years, tested across four out-of-sample quarters.
Use
Swap your tuning objective to regime-split trading performance, and rank-blend a boosted tree with a tabular net.
Score 4/5
Prices and fundamentals are cheap and the recipe is reproducible, but alternative-data features and exact entry/exit rules are left to you.
tabular deep learningBayesian optimisationregime robustnessXGBoostTabNetrank aggregation
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTAlgorithmic trading now represents a market exceeding $20 billion, where even marginal gains in signal robustness can translate into economically significant returns. Existing evaluations of equity prediction models do not explicitly target regime robustness during hyperparameter selection. Five model classes are trained on daily observations from approximately 300 large-cap US equities over eleven years, with Bayesian optimisation configured to target trading performance across three statistically different market regimes. Regime-robust hyperparameter selection is associated with out-of-sample generalisation, as signal precision remains above the random baseline across all four quarters of the test period, and portfolio performance slowly degrades under simulated input noise before collapsing beyond a defined threshold. No individual tabular deep learning architecture outperforms gradient-boosted trees, but combining XGBoost and TabNet using rank aggregation produces a Hybrid ensemble with an annualised return of 51.26%, a Sharpe ratio of 2.44, and a statistically significant CAPM alpha of 0.423 (p = 0.011). A near-zero beta indicates this outperformance is driven by stock selection, not market exposure. Alternative data plays a secondary role once technical and fundamental features are accounted for, as well as contributing more strongly on the short side than the long, and varies by model class. An interactive application makes these results explorable in real time, with live data integration the remaining step toward practical deployment.

QuantpediaQPCAPEindex valuation boundaries-of-time-series-momentum ★★★★★

Boundaries of Time Series Momentum

밸류에이션 극단 구간에서 시계열 모멘텀이 무너진다고 밝힌다.

Suominen and Hjalmarsson show time-series momentum fails at valuation extremes.

Cyril Dujava

  • 추세추종을 늘 통하는 알파로 믿는 관행을 문제 삼는다
  • 정상 경기순환 구간과 밸류에이션 극단 구간을 나눠 본다
  • 극단 구간에서 주식 추세는 무너지고 강하게 반전한다
  • 추세 매매의 거시 경계선을 실무 규칙으로 제시한다
방법

ORIGINAL ABSTRACTTime-series momentum stands as one of the most reliable and heavily backtested anomalies in quantitative finance, serving as a foundational alpha source for modern managed futures and trend-following strategies. However, a recent academic paper by Matti Suominen and Erik Hjalmarsson, titled "Boundaries of Time Series Momentum," uncovers a structural vulnerability that every practitioner must account for. The authors demonstrate that while equity market trends persist reliably during normal business cycles, they systematically break down and aggressively reverse when market valuations reach historical extremes. This phenomenon establishes clear macro "boundaries" where chasing the trend shifts from a highly profitable strategy to a severe drawdown risk.

ORIGINAL ABSTRACTWe document a large and robust pre-announcement drift in Japanese equity indices ahead of scheduled Bank of Japan (BOJ) monetary policy decisions: prices drift up steadily in the days before a meeting, peak immediately ahead of the decision, and do not continue afterward, earning an economically large and statistically significant excess return without elevated volatility. The drift is concentrated in meetings preceded by high ex-ante implied volatility. It is the Bank-of-Japan analog of the pre-FOMC drift of Lucca and Moench (2015), with a distinctive Japanese feature-variable announcement timing-that aids identification.

적용도 3/5

  • Asks if open-only information forecasts the same-day close direction
  • Expanding-window walk-forward pits five models against naive baselines
  • Over the last 800 days: Logistic 71.09%, RF 61.20%, XGBoost 58.45%

ORIGINAL ABSTRACTWe study statistical predictability in daily U.S. equity prices using only information available at the market open. Using SPY from February 1, 1993 through March 15, 2024, we benchmark XGBoost against Random Forest, LightGBM, Logistic Regression, and naive baselines under expanding-window walk-forward validation. After observing the current day's opening price and two lagged target-specific prices, the task is to predict whether the same day's close will be above or below the previous day's close. On the last 800 trading days, Logistic Regression attains the highest close-direction accuracy (71.09%), Random Forest reaches 61.20%, and XGBoost reaches 58.45% with a 95% bootstrap confidence interval of [54.94%, 62.08]. For XGBoost, close-direction accuracy rises to 72.7% when the predicted move exceeds 1%, but the usable sample falls to 154 observations. We also report Diebold-Mariano and McNemar tests, regime-specific results, SHAP feature importance, and an auxiliary 541-equity screen. The evidence supports a narrow statistical-finance conclusion: simple daily equity features contain detectable same-day directional information, but the result should be interpreted with careful sample-size accounting and limited economic claims.

Graph-Based Modeling of Financial Volatility Dynamics

내재변동성 표면을 그래프로 바꿔 실현변동성을 예측한다

The authors forecast realized volatility by modeling the IV surface as an evolving graph.

Chuanzhen Wang, Alice Zhang, Wei Chen, Michael Brown

  • 기존 방식은 IV 표면을 정지 이미지로만 다룬다
  • 격자점을 노드로 삼아 시공간 그래프를 만든다
  • 옵션 그릭스와 잡음 제거 모듈을 함께 넣는다
  • R² 0.473으로 최고 성능을 찍었다
방법
FA-GSTN은 IV 표면의 격자점을 노드로 잇고, 일중 공간 연결과 일간 시간 연결을 학습한다
데이터
대규모 주식 옵션 데이터로 검증했고, 2020~2021년 시장 충격 구간에서도 성능을 유지한다
시사점
옵션 포지션의 변동성 리스크 헤지나 델타 헤지 빈도 조절에 예측치를 넣어 쓴다
적용도 3/5
IV 표면과 그릭스는 유료 옵션 데이터가 필요하고, 예측치를 매매 규칙으로 바꾸려면 별도 설계가 더 든다
  • Prior methods freeze the IV surface into a static image
  • FA-GSTN turns grid points into spatio-temporal graph nodes
  • Greeks as features, smoothing gate for high-frequency noise
  • R² up to 0.473; 0.372 vs 0.315 on one year of data
Method
A spatio-temporal graph net with adaptive intra-day edges, explicit inter-day edges, and a robust loss.
Data
Large-scale equity options dataset, including the 2020-2021 stress period.
Use
Feed the RV forecast into option vol hedging or delta-hedge rebalancing frequency.
Score 3/5
Scored 3 because IV surfaces and Greeks need paid options data, and the forecast still needs a trading rule built around it.
realized volatility forecastingimplied volatility surfacespatio-temporal graph neural networkoption Greeksequity options
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTAccurate forecasting of realized volatility ($RV$) is crucial for risk management and derivatives pricing. Although the implied volatility ($IV$) surface offers rich informational content, prevailing methods that treat it as a static image fail to capture its inherent dynamics. To overcome this limitation, we propose the Finance-Aware Graph Spatio-Temporal Network (FA-GSTN), a novel architecture that reframes $RV$ forecasting as modeling the evolution of a structured financial object. FA-GSTN builds a spatio-temporal graph sequence from the $IV$ surface, where nodes correspond to grid points and edges encode adaptive spatial (intra-day) and explicit temporal (inter-day) dependencies. The model incorporates domain knowledge through finance-aware node features (e.g., option Greeks) and tackles high-frequency noise via a multi-scale temporal smoothing gate coupled with an adaptive robust loss function. Comprehensive evaluations on a large-scale equity options dataset show that FA-GSTN sets a new state of the art, delivering superior predictive accuracy ($R^2$ up to 0.473). It also demonstrates remarkable data efficiency, substantially outperforming strong Vision Transformer baselines when trained on only one year of data ($R^2$: 0.372 vs. 0.315). Furthermore, the model exhibits enhanced robustness during periods of market stress, such as 2020--2021. Ablation studies confirm the vital roles of the spatio-temporal graph structure, finance-aware components, and integrated noise-handling modules. Our work underscores the substantial benefits of explicitly modeling temporal dynamics and infusing financial inductive biases for accurate and robust volatility forecasting.

Analysis of the Principal Components of Correlation Matrices of S&P 500 Financial Data from an Econophysics Perspective

Clusters S&P 500 correlation spectra into market states.

Javier Gómez Morales

  • 시장을 복잡계로 보고 집단 동학을 파악하려 한다
  • 이동창 상관행렬의 고유값·고유벡터를 클러스터링한다
  • 코로나 국면은 주성분 2~3개를 써야 재현된다
  • 위기 때는 참여도가 더 많은 종목으로 퍼진다
방법
이동창 피어슨 상관행렬에서 최대 고유값과 주고유벡터, 절단 재구성 행렬 C^l을 뽑아 k-평균(k-Means)으로 시장 국면을 나눈다.
데이터
S&P 500 편입 430개 종목의 일간 로그수익률을 쓰고, 코로나 구간과 2017~2018년 구간을 살핀다.
시사점
국면 전이행렬과 역참여비율(IPR)을 리스크 스위치로 삼아 위기 국면에서 분산 비중을 조정한다.
적용도 3/5
데이터는 일간 종가만 있으면 되어 확보가 쉽지만, 논문 스스로 예측이 아닌 서술적 분석이라 밝혀 매매 규칙까지는 추가 설계가 필요하다.
  • Treats the market as a complex system, not single names
  • k-Means on eigenvalues and dominant-eigenvector weights
  • COVID needs 2-3 components; C^2, C^3 reproduce it
  • Crises spread participation across more stocks
Method
Builds sliding-window Pearson correlation matrices, then k-Means clusters the top eigenvalue, squared dominant-eigenvector entries, and truncated reconstructions C^l.
Data
Daily log returns of 430 S&P 500 companies, with the COVID-19 episode and the 2017-2018 interval as focus periods.
Use
Use the state transition matrix and inverse participation ratio as a regime switch for sizing and diversification.
Score 3/5
Data is trivial to source (daily closes), but the author calls the work descriptive, not predictive, so entry and exit rules need building from scratch.
correlation matrixprincipal componentsmarket statesk-means clusteringeconophysicsinverse participation ratio
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTThis thesis analyzes the collective dynamics of the S&P 500 from an econophysics perspective, treating the financial market as a complex system. Using daily logarithmic returns of 430 companies, time-dependent Pearson correlation matrices are constructed over sliding windows and their spectral structure is studied through the leading eigenvalues and eigenvectors. Market States (MS) are identified by applying k-Means clustering to several spectral quantities: the largest eigenvalue (interpreted as the State of the Market), the squared entries of the dominant eigenvector (read as a relative participation of each stock), and truncated matrix reconstructions C^l built from the l largest eigenpairs and normalized to retain a correlation-matrix interpretation. The stability of the resulting clusters is assessed, transition matrices and their stationary vectors are computed, and the inverse participation ratio is used to quantify how participation spreads across assets. The COVID-19 episode emerges as an atypical state in the full correlation matrices; reconstructing it requires more than one principal component, and the C^2 and C^3 constructions reproduce it, particularly for window length q=40 and k=5. During crisis periods the relative participation distributes over a larger number of assets, while the 2017-2018 interval contributes to the dynamics without appearing as an isolated regime. The analysis is descriptive rather than predictive, and provides conceptual and methodological tools relevant to portfolio design and trading strategies, with natural extensions to other international exchanges.

  • Nobody had isolated data resolution as the only variable in bar construction

ORIGINAL ABSTRACTThis paper provides a controlled comparison of six information bar types (dollar, volume, volatility, range, Renko, and hybrid bars) constructed from both raw Binance aggTrade tick data and one-minute OHLCV bars for the BTCUSDT USDT-margined perpetual futures market over a six-year period spanning January 2020 to December 2025, and evaluated against fixed-interval time-bar baselines. Both pipelines share a common adaptive EMA calibration framework; the tick pipeline additionally uses strictly tick-native activity signals, isolating data resolution as the sole experimental variable. Results across eight statistical quality criteria reveal that the tick advantage is bar-type-specific and most pronounced in bar types whose activity signals are most sensitive to intra-minute price dynamics: tick Renko bars achieve the smallest random-walk deviation recorded ($|\mathrm{VR}(4){-}1| = 0.020$, lag-1 autocorrelation $= 0.002$), and tick volatility bars reduce serial dependence by 69\% relative to the minute baseline ($|\mathrm{VR}(4){-}1|: 0.028$ versus $0.089$). In the multi-regime six-year sample, normality improvements are regime-dependent and secondary: the extreme market events of 2020--2022 inflate fat tails across all bar types, and Ljung-Box independence is rejected for all series at the sample sizes studied. A matched-frequency robustness analysis shows that the apparent tick underperformance on distributional criteria is largely a sampling-frequency artefact: when tick series are coarsened to the minute pipeline's bar count, frequency-matched tick dollar bars lead on all six criteria and matched tick volatility bars attain LB $p = 0.51$, recovering serial independence that the raw oversampled series rejects.

Forecasting Economically Significant Bitcoin Moves: A Multi-Scale TCN with Profit-Optimized Thresholds

The authors predict whether Bitcoin rises over 5% within 7 days.

  • 가격 예측 대신 경제적으로 유의미한 급등만 겨냥한다
  • 다중 스케일 TCN에 채널 어텐션과 랭킹 손실을 결합한다
  • AUC 0.6316, 수익 1.703으로 baseline 5종을 모두 앞선다
  • 불균형 데이터라 정확도 대신 AUC로 평가한다

InceptionTCN 블록, CNN 채널 어텐션, 적응형 평균 풀링, 쌍별 랭킹 손실을 쓴 다중 스케일 시간 합성곱망이다.
데이터
2018년 2월부터 2025년 12월까지 비트코인 온체인·시장·심리 데이터를 쓴다.
시사점
수익 기준으로 임계값을 고르는 방식은 다른 분류형 신호에도 그대로 옮겨 쓸 만하다.
적용도 3/5
온체인·가격 데이터는 구하기 쉽지만 심리 지표 출처와 진입·청산 규칙이 빠져 있어 재현에 추가 설계가 필요하다.
  • Targets economically meaningful jumps, not raw price levels
  • Multi-scale TCN with channel attention and ranking loss
  • AUC 0.6316 and profit 1.703 beat all five baselines
  • Uses AUC over accuracy because classes are imbalanced
Method
A multi-scale temporal convolutional network with InceptionTCN blocks, CNN channel attention, adaptive average pooling, and pairwise ranking loss.
Data
On-chain, market, and sentiment data for Bitcoin from February 2018 to December 2025.
Use
Steal the profit-optimized threshold step: pick cutoffs by PnL, not by F1, on any classifier signal.
Score 3/5
On-chain and price data are easy to source, but the sentiment inputs are unspecified and no entry/exit rules are given, so a tradable version needs real design work.
Bitcointemporal convolutional networkon-chain dataprofit-optimized thresholdranking lossclass imbalance
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTBitcoin's future fluctuations are a substantial concern for investments and risk management. Investors and financial institutions require accurate forecasts of these price movements to hedge and optimize portfolios. This study aims to answer the question of whether Bitcoin's price will rise beyond 5% within the next 7 days by utilizing on-chain, market, and sentiment data from February 2018 to December 2025. The proposed model consists of a multi-scale temporal convolutional network with InceptionTCN blocks, CNN channel attention, adaptive average pooling, and a pairwise ranking loss. Dilated convolutions with bottleneck and fusion layers are employed to efficiently capture features over horizons from 1 to 4 days. Given the class imbalance in the dataset, AUC is used instead of accuracy and other classification metrics to reflect the model's performance better. Subsequently, a profit-optimized decision threshold is also applied to align model selection with financial objectives. The proposed model is compared with 5 other baselines: ImprovedTCN_GRU, LSTM, TCN, XGBoost, and Random Forest. Results indicate that the proposed model achieved an AUC of 0.6316 and a profit of 1.703, outperforming all baseline models. Using a novel deep learning model would assist investors in making better financial decisions.

Climate Policy Uncertainty and Ambiguity-Aware Strategic Asset Allocation

Jinsuk Choi, Seongeun Kim · Korea Investment Corporation and

  • 장기 기관투자자는 정책 반전 위험을 배분에 못 담는다

  • 다만 샤프 개선은 통계적으로 유의하지 않다
방법
최대 샤프와 최소분산을 섞는 ARMVO, 기대수익을 균형수익으로 축소하는 ALBL 두 규칙을 쓴다.
데이터
Gavriilidis 외(2026)의 기후정책 불확실성(CPU) 지수와 글로벌 ETF 11종, 2012~2024년 구간을 쓴다.
시사점
거시 불확실성 지표의 롤링 백분위로 축소 강도를 조절하는 배분 오버레이에 쓴다.
적용도 3/5
CPU 지수와 ETF 가격은 공개 데이터라 구현은 쉽지만, 신호가 아닌 배분 기법이고 개선폭도 유의하지 않아 3점이다.
  • Long-horizon investors lack rules for policy-reversal risk
  • CPU index acts as an observable ambiguity state variable
  • ALBL lifts Sharpe from 0.119 to 0.286 vs equal weight
  • Sharpe gain is not statistically significant
Method
Two rules: ARMVO blends max-Sharpe and min-variance; ALBL shrinks expected returns toward equilibrium.
Data
Gavriilidis et al. (2026) CPU index plus eleven global ETFs, 2012-2024.
Use
Use it as an allocation overlay where a macro uncertainty percentile dials shrinkage up or down.
Score 3/5
CPU index and ETF prices are public and easy to source, but this is a portfolio-construction technique, not a signal, and the Sharpe gain lacks significance.
climate policy uncertaintyambiguityBlack-Littermanrobust mean-variancestrategic asset allocationETF
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTClimate policy uncertainty has become an important consideration for strategic asset allocation, particularly for long-horizon institutional investors whose portfolios are exposed to regulatory shifts and policy reversals. This paper examines how institutional investors can translate climate policy uncertainty into implementable strategic asset-allocation rules. Motivated by the distinction between risk and Knightian ambiguity, we use climate policy uncertainty as an observable state variable that governs the strength of portfolio shrinkage. Building on this idea, we propose two allocation rules. The first is an ambiguity-penalized robust mean -variance optimizer (ARMVO), which blends the maximum-Sharpe and minimum-variance portfolios with a blending intensity controlled by the uncertainty state. The second is an ambiguity-linked Black-Litterman model (ALBL), which shrinks the expected-return input toward the reverse-optimization equilibrium return using the same state variable. We implement the two rules using a rolling percentile of the newly developed climate policy uncertainty (CPU) index of f Gavriilidis et al. (2026) and test them on eleven global ETFs from 2012 to 2024. ALBL improves the Sharpe ratio from 0.119 for equal weighting to 0.286 and generates a certainty equivalent gain of 208 basis points per year for a risk-aversion parameter of γ=5, although the Sharpe-ratio improvement is not statistically significant at conventional levels. Asset-based attribution suggests that the improvement is not fully explained by a static allocation to the same ETFs. The paper contributes a transparent and implementable framework for ambiguity-aware strategic asset allocation.

commercial real estatecap ratesatellite imagerystreet viewcomputer visionhedonic pricing
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTInvestors price commercial property partly on how it looks, yet visual attributes rarely appear in the data used to study transaction pricing. Using satellite and street view imagery for 6,533 U.S. single-tenant net lease transactions from 2005 to 2020, we read each property's images two ways: as scores against attributes we name in advance, and as unlabeled statistical summaries. Together they close about 6.5 percent of the cap rate variation left unexplained by conventional property, market, lease, and time controls, and the named attributes account for most of it. The gain survives county fixed effects, public controls for walkability, demographics, urban form, and flood risk, and it improves out-of-sample forecasts. Because most of what imagery contributes can be stated in words, appraisal and underwriting can use it without sacrificing transparency.

Multi-Agent Deep Reinforcement Learning for Maximal Extractable Value Allocation under Proposer-Builder Separation

PBS 블록 생산에서 MEV가 누구에게 흘러가는지 다중 에이전트 강화학습으로 추적한다.

Simulates who captures MEV across searchers, builders, and proposers under PBS.

Shengyu Chen, Hui Zhang, Junhuan Zhang · , Beihang University (BUAA) and School of Economics and Management, Beihang University

  • MEV 배분 구조를 실증 데이터로 관측하기 어렵다
  • 서처·빌더·프로포저를 에이전트로 세워 정책을 학습한다
  • 샌드위치 허용 시 일반 트레이더 보상이 줄어든다
  • 빌더 경쟁이 가치 일부를 프로포저에게 넘긴다
방법
다중 에이전트 심층 강화학습(MARL)으로 각 참여자 정책을 최적화하고, 평균 근사 최적대응(ABR) 후회로 일방적 이탈 이득을 잰다.
데이터
실거래 데이터 없이 2개 풀·단일 토큰쌍 시뮬레이션으로 백러닝 서처 2인 구성과 샌드위치+백러닝 혼합 구성을 비교한다.
시사점
온체인 집행 비용을 모델링할 때 샌드위치 노출도를 슬리피지 가정에 반영하는 정도로 쓴다.
적용도 2/5
시뮬레이션 전용 연구라 매매 신호가 없고, 실제로 재현하려면 멤풀과 블록 입찰 데이터에 저지연 인프라까지 필요해 데이터 확보 장벽이 높다.
  • MEV allocation is hard to observe from on-chain data alone
  • MARL agents learn searcher, builder, and proposer policies
  • Allowing sandwiches cuts uninformed traders' rewards
  • Builder competition passes value up to the proposer
Method
Multi-agent deep RL over the full PBS pipeline, with average approximate best-response regret measuring residual unilateral gains.
Data
No market data: a two-pool, single-token-pair simulation comparing two back-running searchers against one sandwich plus one back-running searcher.
Use
Use it to size sandwich exposure in slippage and execution-cost assumptions for DEX order routing.
Score 2/5
Simulation only, no tradable signal, and replicating it needs mempool plus builder-bid data and low-latency infrastructure most desks cannot source.
MEVProposer-Builder Separationmulti-agent reinforcement learningDEXsandwich attackback-running
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTWe develop a multi-agent deep reinforcement learning framework for analyzing Maximal Extractable Value (MEV) allocation in decentralized exchange (DEX) markets under Proposer-Builder Separation (PBS). The model follows the PBS block-production pipeline from uninformed DEX-user transactions to searcher bundle construction, builder block assembly, proposer block selection, and on-chain execution. Multi-agent deep reinforcement learning (MARL) optimizes participant policies in this shared execution environment, and average approximate best-response (ABR) regret quantifies the residual unilateral gains found by a finite evaluation procedure that holds all other agents' policies fixed and retrains one agent at a time. In a two-pool, single-token-pair experimental instantiation, we compare a configuration with two back-running searchers against a mixed configuration with one sandwich searcher and one back-running searcher. This distinction matters because a back-running transaction is appended after observed order flow and does not change the amount of output tokens received by the original uninformed trader, whereas a sandwich attack places searcher transactions around a targeted victim swap and can reduce the amount of output tokens received by that trader. The results show that MEV-related cumulative rewards are allocated differently across these configurations. Searchers convert visible order flow into bundle profits, builders aggregate these bundles through block construction, and builder competition transfers part of the resulting value to the proposer through block bids. Permitting sandwich attacks lowers uninformed traders' cumulative rewards and increases reward asymmetry among searchers.

investor attentioncatering disclosuretextual analysiscross-border e-commercereturn reversalChina A-shares
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTWe provide novel evidence on how investor attention interacts with corporate disclosure during the “full-consignment” shift in cross-border e-commerce, a reversal of the decade-long platform migration toward agency selling. Using 41,236 question-and-answer dialogues from China’s regulated investor interaction platforms for 118 listed supply-chain firms over 2022–2024, we document three counterintuitive findings. Consignment-related Q&A spikes generate overpricing that is larger and reverses faster than that associated with unregulated attention proxies: a five-day abnormal return of +2.9% fully reverses within six weeks, consistent with a credibility illusion created by exchange supervision. Attention also runs backward—investor questioning induces consignment narratives, concentrated in firms that never report consignment revenue. Finally, the market rewards narrative adopters while underreacting to genuine converters. The price-formation benefits of regulated interaction platforms are thus topic-contingent, and investor questioning constitutes a novel demand-side trigger of catering disclosure.​

ORIGINAL ABSTRACTThe Markets in Crypto-Assets Regulation (MiCA) is usually discussed as a matter of supervisory detail: the rules for stablecoins, the requirements for service providers, and the perimeter of decentralised finance. This paper argues that MiCA's most consequential problems are structural, and that they lie at the intersection of regulatory law and private international law. It develops a single method: control as the criterion of responsibility and entitlement, coordinated across borders through conflict-of-laws connecting factors. From that method it derives a reform agenda. It proposes an objective directed-activity test for reverse solicitation, transposed from Pammer and Hotel Alpenhof; a general antiabuse rule for MiCA; a controller-of-last-resort attribution rule for decentralised finance; a functional property rule for tokens grounded in Article 114 TFEU; a crypto-specific presumption of the centre of main interests anchored in Eurofood and Interedil; and a free-standing conflict-of-laws Regulation for the proprietary aspects of tokens, with the effective locus of control over key-management infrastructure as the connecting factor for custody. It closes with a ten-principle framework for a transnational private law of digital-asset custody. The paper's contribution is to show that the perimeter, stablecoin, DeFi and private-law questions form one problem, and that control and private international law supply their common solution.

Where does Title Live? Tokenised Fund Units, Control and the Conflict of Laws in the DIFC 1

DIFC 토큰화 펀드 지분의 소유권 귀속 공백을 짚고 입법 해법을 낸다.

Maps the legal gap that blocks tokenised fund units in the DIFC and proposes a fix.

Cayetana Santaolalla · Public University of Navarra (UPNA)

  • 규제 장벽은 기술이 아니라 법이라는 문제의식
  • 집합투자규칙 8.7조와 2024년 디지털자산법을 대조 분석

  • UNIDROIT 원칙 5를 본떠 지정에 의한 준거법 고정을 제안
방법
DIFC 규정과 ADGM 체제를 비교하는 법리 분석이다.
데이터
이론 연구
시사점
중동 토큰화 펀드에 투자하거나 담보로 잡을 때 법적 리스크 점검표로 쓴다.
적용도 1/5
매매 규칙이 전혀 없는 순수 법률 논문이고, 백테스트할 데이터 자체가 존재하지 않아 1점이다.
  • Barrier to tokenised units is legal, not technological
  • CIR 8.7 register carries no legal effect on title
  • 2024 Digital Assets Law presumes control equals title
  • Proposes anchoring by designation, per UNIDROIT Principle 5
Method
Doctrinal analysis of DIFC rules, benchmarked against the Abu Dhabi Global Market.
Data
Theory only
Use
Use as a due-diligence checklist before holding or pledging Gulf tokenised fund units.
Score 1/5
Pure law and policy with no signal, no universe, and no dataset to obtain, so it scores 1.
tokenised fund unitsDIFCconflict of lawsdigital assetscontrolcollateral
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTIn July 2026 the Dubai Financial Services Authority asked whether any regulatory barrier stands in the way of issuing tokenised fund units in the Dubai International Financial Centre. This paper argues that the barrier is legal rather than technological, and that the Authority has already described it without naming it. Section 8.7 of the Collective Investment Rules requires a fund manager to keep a register of unitholders. It attributes no legal effect to that register. The register is not conclusive evidence of title, or evidence of title, and no power of rectification accompanies it. The DIFC Digital Assets Law of 2024 provides that the person in control of a digital asset is presumed to hold the superior title. One legal order therefore supplies an answer and a silence, and the answer was drafted for native crypto assets rather than for fund units. The paper meets the objection that this tension is illusory. The DIFC statute admits only assets that exist independently of any person and of any legal system, and a fund unit is a right constituted by a legal system. The objection is well founded, and it does not rescue the position. It produces what this paper calls the constitutive paradox. Either the tokenised unit is a digital asset, and control governs by default; or it is not, and the token carries no proprietary protection at all under the law that issued it. The paper then identifies a second gap, which it calls the anchoring problem. Article 129 of the DIFC Law of Security refers the applicable law for security rights in digital assets, and in assets embodied in digital assets, to the laws of England and Wales. That provision reaches tokenised fund units used as collateral. It refers the question to a system that has not yet settled it. Outside the security context nothing governs title at all. The paper states the case for that referral before answering it and compares the DIFC with the Abu Dhabi Global Market. It proposes anchoring by designation, modelled on Principle 5 of the UNIDROIT Principles on Digital Assets and Private Law, and applies the same reasoning to collateral, custody and insolvency.

EcoTechnoPolitical Citizenship

AI 시대 시민권을 생태·기술·정치로 묶는 개념 틀을 제안한다

Calzada proposes EcoTechnoPolitical Citizenship, a framework for governing AI-dependent societies.

Igor Calzada · Ikerbasque Foundation UPV/EHU

  • 기존 디지털 시민권론은 생태 비용과 인프라 소유권을 놓친다
  • AI는 에너지·물·광물·노동·토지를 쓰는 물리적 시스템이다
  • 물적 기반, 인프라 민주적 통제, 시민 행위성, 세대 간 책임 네 축을 세운다
  • 영향평가·공공 인프라·데이터 공유재 등 정책 함의를 도출한다
방법
규범적 개념 분석으로 시민권 프레임워크를 구성한 이론 논문이다
데이터
이론 연구
시사점
AI·데이터센터 관련 규제 리스크를 ESG 스크리닝 항목으로 참고할 정도다
적용도 1/5
정책 개념 논문이라 자동화할 규칙이 없고, 애초에 쓸 데이터가 제시되지 않아 1점이다
  • Digital citizenship theory ignores ecological cost and infrastructure ownership
  • AI runs on energy, water, minerals, labour and land
  • Four axes: material basis, democratic control, agency, cross-generational duty

digital citizenshipAI governanceecological limitsdigital-green transitiondata commons
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTArtificial intelligence is changing how citizenship is exercised. Decisions about welfare, mobility, work, knowledge and political communication increasingly depend on data and automated systems operating across territorial and legal boundaries. These systems also require energy, water, minerals, labour and land, revealing the physical limits of seemingly immaterial digitalisation. Established accounts of digital citizenship explain rights, participation, inclusion and people's capacity to act through data. They give less attention, however, to ecological costs, ownership of essential infrastructure and consequences that extend across places and generations. This chapter develops EcoTechnoPolitical Citizenship as a framework connecting these concerns. It defines citizenship as the practical capacity to claim digital rights, participate in collective decisions, shape technological infrastructure and accept ecological responsibilities according to each actor's power and contribution. The framework has four dimensions: the physical and ecological basis of digital systems; democratic control over infrastructure; meaningful citizen agency; and responsibility across territories, supply chains and generations. The chapter traces the development of digital citizenship towards automated public life and explains AI economics through data, computing capacity, finance, labour and public dependence. It then examines the digital-green transition, which seeks to align digitalisation with sustainability but often leaves technological growth outside enforceable ecological limits. Practical implications are developed for impact assessment, public digital infrastructure, data commons, territorial oversight, citizen participation and education. Particular attention is paid to unequal power among citizens, governments, firms and communities affected by infrastructure. EcoTechnoPolitical Citizenship ultimately provides an accessible conceptual map for governing AI-dependent societies democratically while respecting planetary limits.

arXivPMmath.OC 2608.24449 ★★★★★

Generalizing Markowitz Portfolio Optimization by a Quadratic Risk Measure

마코위츠 최적화를 일반 이차 위험척도로 확장해 닫힌 해를 구한다.

The authors extend Markowitz optimization to any strictly convex quadratic risk measure, in closed form.

Ignas Gasparavičius, Andrius Grigutis

  • 분산만 위험으로 쓰는 마코위츠 틀은 거래비용·벤치마크를 못 담는다
  • 공분산행렬을 임의의 대칭 양정부호 행렬로 바꾸고 선형·상수항을 더한다
  • 효율적 경계, 최소위험·최대샤프·접점 포트폴리오를 모두 닫힌 식으로 얻는다
  • 접점 포트폴리오가 최대 샤프 포트폴리오와 더는 일치하지 않는다
방법
위험을 강볼록 이차형식으로 두고 라그랑주 방식으로 해를 유도한다.
데이터
이론 연구이며 수치 예제 하나로 공식을 확인한다.
시사점
거래비용이나 벤치마크 추적오차를 위험함수에 직접 넣고 해석해로 리밸런싱한다.
적용도 3/5
데이터는 가격만 있으면 되지만 신호가 아닌 최적화 틀이라 전략화에 설계가 더 필요하다.
  • Variance-only risk excludes transaction costs and benchmark-relative goals
  • Swap covariance for any symmetric positive definite matrix, add linear and constant terms
  • Closed forms for efficient frontier, min-risk, max-Sharpe, tangency, max-utility portfolios
  • Tangency portfolio no longer equals the max-Sharpe portfolio
Method
Strictly convex quadratic objective solved analytically under budget and return constraints.
Data
Theory only; one numerical example checks the formulas.
Use
Fold turnover costs or tracking error straight into the risk term and rebalance with an analytic solution.
Score 3/5
Only standard price data is needed, but this is a portfolio-construction framework, not a signal, so a strategy still needs substantial design.
portfolio optimizationquadratic risk measureefficient frontiertangency portfoliotransaction costsclosed-form solution
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTWe show that the key optimization results of the classical Markowitz portfolio selection theory, originally formulated for variance as the risk measure, remain available in explicit closed form under a broader class of strictly convex quadratic risk measures. The proposed framework replaces the covariance matrix with an arbitrary symmetric positive definite matrix and allows additional linear and constant terms, thereby containing various models arising in transaction cost optimization, benchmark relative optimization, covariance regularization, and factor models. Closed-form formulas are obtained for the efficient frontier, the global minimum risk portfolio, the maximum Sharpe ratio portfolio, the Capital Market Curve, the tangency portfolio, and the maximum utility portfolio. In contrast to the classical Markowitz model, the tangency portfolio does not coincide with the maximum Sharpe ratio portfolio, revealing a new geometric phenomenon. A numerical example confirms the derived formulas.
abs PDF

ORIGINAL ABSTRACTA single backtest can show that strategy was profitable, but it does not always show whether the strategy was competitive. This is especially true for systematic futures strategies. A trend-following strategy can have a positive Sharpe ratio, a long live-like performance history, and a reasonable drawdown, but those numbers are difficult to interpret without a relevant comparison group. A broad equity index is often not the right benchmark for this type of strategy. A monthly rebalanced multi-asset futures strategy has a different objective, different risk profile, and different return drivers than a long-only stock index. A more useful question is whether the strategy performs well compared with other systematic trend-following futures strategies. This case study shows how the Quantpedia API can be used to build a custom peer group benchmark for strategy evaluation. The workflow has two steps. First, Quantpedia strategy metadata is used to screen and define a comparable peer group. Second, the historical equity curves of the selected strategies are downloaded, converted into daily returns, and aggregated into an equal-weighted peer group benchmark.

arXivPMphysics.soc-phecon.GNcross-list 2608.23576 ★★★★★

Minimum-Distortion Wealth Taxation, I: Information-Theoretic versus Transport-Geometric Optimality on the Proportional Class

부유세 최적 설계를 두 가지 최적성 기준으로 비교한 이론 논문이다.

Derives closed-form optimal wealth-tax schedules under two rival distortion criteria.

Anders G Frøseth

  • 부유세 왜곡을 재는 규범적 기준이 서로 갈린다
  • 로그 자산에 포커-플랑크 방정식을 세워 푼다
  • JKO는 3구간, W2는 유량세 코너로 붕괴한다
  • 노르웨이 보정값은 포트폴리오 변동성에 따라 갈린다
방법
로그 자산의 포커-플랑크 확산 위에서 JKO 자유에너지 격차와 2-바서슈타인(2-Wasserstein) 거리를 각각 최소화한다.
데이터
이론 연구다. 다만 노르웨이 세제를 본뜬 보정값을 예시로 쓴다.
시사점
거래 신호로 쓸 건 없고, 세후 복리 수익 모형이나 노르웨이 자산가 자금 흐름 시나리오 정도에 참고한다.
적용도 1/5
조세 정책 이론이라 자동화할 규칙이 없고, 필요한 데이터도 시장 데이터가 아닌 세제 파라미터라 1점이다.
  • Two normative traditions disagree on measuring tax distortion
  • Fokker-Planck diffusion on log-wealth, two optimality metrics
  • JKO splits into 3 phases; W2 collapses to flow-tax corner
  • Bluntness index B: JKO weights it linearly, W2 quadratically
Method
Minimises the JKO free-energy gap and the squared 2-Wasserstein distance over a two-parameter tax design plane.
Data
Theory only, with illustrative Norwegian-style tax calibrations.
Use
At most a reference for after-tax compounding models or Norwegian household capital-flow scenarios.
Score 1/5
Pure tax-policy theory with no tradable rule; inputs are statutory tax parameters, not market data, so it scores 1.
wealth taxationFokker-PlanckWasserstein distanceJKO schemeoptimal taxationlog-wealth dynamics
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTWe characterise minimum-distortion wealth taxation under two contrasting normative criteria within a Fokker-Planck framework on log-wealth: the JKO free-energy gap, an information-theoretic measure aligned with the Mirrleesian decision-distortion tradition, and the squared 2-Wasserstein distance from the no-tax distribution at horizon $T$, a transport-geometric measure aligned with the Saez-Zucman distributional-compression tradition. Restricting to the neutrality-preserving (C1)-(C3) schedule class of a companion paper (Froseth 2026), both optima admit closed forms in the two-dimensional design plane parametrised by the corporate-dividend retention $k = (1-τ_c)(1-τ_d)$ and the proportional wealth-tax rate $τ_w$. The JKO optimum partitions the regime axis into three phases as a function of the dimensionless ratio $ρ= Σ_0 m_0/σ^2$, with $m_0 = μ- σ^2/2$ the geometric mean log-return: a pure wealth-tax phase at low $ρ$, a mixed-instrument phase at intermediate $ρ$, and a pure flow-tax phase at high $ρ$. The $W_2$ optimum, by contrast, is degenerate in this calibration: it pins to the pure flow-tax corner across the whole regime axis. The criterion contrast admits an economically meaningful reading via a bluntness index $B(m_0) = b/(a m_0)$ that measures the wealth-tax channel's mean-displacement-per-revenue overshoot relative to the flow-tax channel; JKO weights $B$ linearly, $W_2$ weights it quadratically, and the two normative traditions correspond to this difference in weighting. Norwegian-flavoured calibrations sit inside the JKO mixed-instrument phase under stock-heavy portfolio volatility but move into the pure flow-tax phase under the realised effective volatility of typical real-estate-heavy households.

KellyBoost: Growth-Optimal Portfolio Construction with Gradient-Boosted Trees

XGBoost 손실함수를 켈리 성장률로 바꿔 비중을 직접 뽑는다.

Swaps XGBoost's loss for Kelly log-growth so the model outputs weights directly.

Jiayu Li

  • 수익률 예측과 비중 결정을 나누면 최적성이 깨진다
  • 소프트맥스 출력을 비중으로 두고 -log(1+wy)를 최소화한다
  • 그래디언트와 헤시안을 닫힌 형태로 유도해 유한차분으로 검증한다
  • 의존성 없는 참조 엔진을 함께 공개한다
방법
다중 출력 XGBoost 한 개에 켈리 로그성장률 손실을 직접 넣어 학습한다.
데이터
이론과 구현 중심이며 초록에 데이터셋이나 검증 구간은 없다.
시사점
예측 모델의 출력을 비중으로 바꾸는 단계를 없애고 싶을 때 쓴다.
적용도 3/5
데이터는 자산별 수익률이면 충분해 확보가 쉽지만, 종목 유니버스와 리밸런싱 주기, 레버리지 제약을 직접 설계해야 한다.
  • Splitting return forecasts from sizing loses optimality
  • Softmax output is the portfolio; loss is -log(1 + w y)
  • Closed-form gradient and Hessian, checked by finite differences
  • Ships a dependency-free reference engine
Method
One multi-output XGBoost trained on the exact negative log-growth objective.
Data
Theory and implementation only; the abstract names no dataset or test window.
Use
Use it to collapse the predict-then-optimize pipeline into a single fitted allocator.
Score 3/5
Data is just per-asset holding-period returns, so sourcing is trivial, but you still design the universe, rebalance cadence and leverage caps yourself.
Kelly criteriongrowth-optimal portfolioXGBoostcustom lossportfolio constructiongradient boosting
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTKellyBoost is a single multi-output XGBoost model whose softmax output is the portfolio: with y the vector of per-asset holding-period returns, the training loss is - log(1 + w y), the negative log growth rate, so the fitted model is the growth-optimal (Kelly) allocation conditioned on the features. The objective is exact rather than a surrogate: we derive the gradient, the analytic diagonal Hessian and the full Hessian in closed form, verify them by finite differences, and ship a dependency-free reference engine.

Li axiomatizes systematic trading into five constants a researcher must declare.

  • 과거 규칙성이 지속된다는 믿음을 방치해 온 문제

latent statetime-invariancesignal capacityregime dependencesystem design axioms
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTSystematic trading rests on one article of faith: that regularities found in the past persist. We state it as a time-invariant mechanism driven by an unobserved latent state, and show that it leaves a researcher five constants to declare --- the recurrence bound $Lambda$ at a block length $b$, the invariance defect $epsilon_0$ of the representation it is declared of, the coherence times $ell_i$ of the state's coordinates, the signal ceiling $rho$ and the fraction $kappa$ of it contingent on the regime --- after which the architecture of a correct quantitative investment system is nearly forced.

Wealth management with macro factors

QuantpediaQPcalendar anomalycalendar effects sectoral-intramonth-momentum-cycle ★★★★★

Sectoral Intramonth Momentum Cycle: Exploiting Turn-of-the-Month Patterns in Sector ETF Strategies

미국 섹터 ETF에서 월중 모멘텀 주기를 찾아 매매 일정으로 바꾼 연구다.

Quantpedia turns an intramonth momentum cycle in sector ETFs into a dated trade schedule.

Cyril Dujava

  • 달력 효과 연구가 개별 주식에만 머물렀다
  • 252일 모멘텀을 월중 특정 일자에만 태운다
  • 롱숏 연 5.99%, 샤프 0.55를 기록한다
  • 월 거래일 절반도 안 되게 투자한다
방법
252일 추세 순위로 섹터를 줄 세우고 월중 세 구간에서만 방향을 바꿔 잡는다.
데이터
SPDR 섹터 ETF 9종과 SPY를 1998년 12월부터 2026년 6월까지 검증한다.
시사점
월초 1일 추세, 2~3일 반전, 월말 10~5일 전 구간을 각각 독립 신호로 붙여 쓴다.
적용도 5/5
ETF 종가만 있으면 그대로 재현되고 진입·청산 일자까지 명시해 5점을 준다.
  • Calendar-anomaly work stopped at single stocks
  • 252-day momentum, traded only on set month days
  • Long-short returns 5.99% a year at 0.55 Sharpe
  • Market-neutral leg: 3.77% at 0.54 Sharpe
Method
Rank sectors on trailing 252-day momentum, then flip direction across three intramonth windows.
Data
Nine Select Sector SPDR ETFs plus SPY, December 1998 through June 2026.
Use
Run momentum long day 1, reverse days 2-3, add the T-10 to T-5 month-end leg as a separate sleeve.
Score 5/5
Scores 5: daily ETF closes are free and public, and the entry and exit days are spelled out.
intramonth momentum cycleturn-of-the-monthsector ETFscalendar anomalylong-shortmarket-neutral
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTWe document a persistent intramonth momentum cycle in U.S. sector ETFs that yields meaningful risk-adjusted returns when properly sequenced. Using the nine original Select Sector SPDR ETFs and SPY as the market benchmark from December 1998 through June 2026, we show that trailing 252-day sector momentum generates a positive spread on the first trading day of the month—and then sharply reverses on days two and three. A third, independent leg of the cycle emerges in the window from ten to five trading days before month-end, consistent with the intramonth momentum cycle recently documented at the single-stock level by Nathan, Suominen and Tasa (2026). Stitching the three legs together into a single composite strategy delivers 5.99% annualized return at a 0.55 Sharpe ratio for the long-short variant, and 3.77% at 0.54 for the market-neutral variant—all while being invested fewer than half the trading days each month. Our contribution is twofold: we extend the calendar-anomaly literature from individual equities to sector-level portfolios, and we provide practitioners with a transparent, low-turnover framework that translates these academic patterns into actionable trade schedules.
source 📄 한국어

Normalize five macro factors, average them equally, and map the composite to duration exposure.

Is Trend Still Your Friend?: A Microstructural Account of the Demise of Short-Term Trend-Following

추세추종 수익 붕괴를 틱 사이즈로 설명한다.

Volatility-normalized tick size explains where trend following died.

Cyril Dujava

  • 2008년 이후 추세추종 수익이 급격히 나빠졌다
  • 선물 약 100종을 1995~2025년 구간에서 검증한다
  • 변동성 대비 틱이 작은 계약에서만 수익이 사라진다
  • 자산군이 아니라 미시구조가 성과를 가른다
방법
변동성으로 정규화한 틱 사이즈로 계약을 나눠 신호 구간별 수익을 비교한다.
데이터
유동성 높은 선물 약 100종의 1995~2025년 일별 데이터를 쓴다.
시사점
추세 포트폴리오를 틱 사이즈 기준으로 쪼개고, 작은 틱 계약은 비중을 줄이거나 뺀다.
적용도 3/5
선물 가격과 틱 사이즈는 구하기 쉽지만, 매매 규칙이 아니라 유니버스 필터만 준다.
  • Trend following decayed sharply after the 2008 crisis
  • Tests ~100 liquid futures contracts, 1995-2025
  • Profits collapsed only on small-tick contracts
  • Microstructure, not asset class, splits the book
Method
Sort futures by tick size scaled by volatility, then compare trend profits across signal horizons.
Data
About 100 liquid futures contracts from 1995 to 2025.
Use
Split a trend book by volatility-normalized tick size and cut or downweight small-tick contracts.
Score 3/5
Futures prices and tick sizes are easy to source, but the paper gives a universe filter, not entry and exit rules.
trend followingmarket microstructuretick sizefuturesmomentum decay
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTTrend following was one of the most persistent anomalies in finance for nearly two centuries, yet its performance deteriorated sharply after the 2008 financial crisis. An analysis of approximately 100 liquid futures contracts from 1995 to 2025 shows that this decline is highly selective. The decisive factor is not asset class, liquidity, market electronification, or strategy crowding, but volatility-normalized tick size. After 2008, trend-following profits collapsed almost entirely on small-tick contracts across all signal horizons, while remaining largely intact on large-tick contracts. This finding suggests that modern trend-following portfolios are fundamentally split into two distinct regimes governed by market microstructure rather than traditional asset classifications.
source

From Backtest to Benchmark: Validating New Strategies with Quantpedia API

신규 전략이 기존 전략의 변형인지 판별하는 검증 절차를 제시한다.

Quantpedia benchmarks a new strategy against documented ones to test whether it is actually new.

david.mesicek@quantpedia.com

  • 좋은 백테스트만으로는 전략의 독창성을 증명하지 못한다
  • Quantpedia 전략 DB와 비교해 최근접 이웃을 찾는다
  • 팩터 군집을 배정하고 유니크니스 점수를 산출한다
  • 연구 견고성 리포트로 중복 여부를 판정한다
방법
Quantpedia API 전략 집합을 기준으로 최근접 이웃 탐색과 군집화를 돌린다.
데이터
Quantpedia에 수록된 계량 전략 데이터베이스를 쓰며, 검증 구간은 밝히지 않았다.
시사점
새 전략을 실전에 넣기 전 기존 팩터와 겹치는지 걸러내는 사전 점검에 쓴다.
적용도 3/5
검증 방법론 개선이라 가점을 주지만, Quantpedia API가 유료 구독이라 데이터 확보가 걸림돌이다.
  • A clean equity curve does not prove the idea is original
  • Compare the backtest to the Quantpedia strategy universe
  • Find nearest neighbours, assign a factor cluster, score uniqueness
  • Output a robustness report flagging redundancy
Method
Nearest-neighbour matching plus factor clustering over the Quantpedia strategy database.
Data
Quantpedia's universe of documented quant strategies; no evaluation window given.
Use
Run it as a gate before allocating: kill strategies that duplicate a known factor or timing rule.
Score 3/5
Scored 3 — it improves validation methodology, but the Quantpedia API is a paid subscription, so the benchmark data is not freely available.
strategy validationfactor clusteringuniqueness scorebenchmarkingnearest neighboursresearch robustness
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTA profitable backtest is rarely the end of a research process. In professional quantitative research, the more important question often comes after the first positive result: is the strategy genuinely new, or is it simply another version of an already known factor, timing rule, or anomaly? This is especially relevant when a researcher develops a new systematic strategy with a clean historical equity curve. The strategy may have acceptable risk-adjusted performance, stable drawdowns, and a logical trading rule, but those statistics alone do not prove that the idea is unique. A silver strategy, for example, may look different on the surface while still behaving like a known commodity timing model, a trend-following strategy, a volatility filter, or a broader macro factor exposure. This article shows how Quantpedia API can be used as a benchmark dataset for validating new research. Instead of evaluating a new backtest in isolation, the strategy is compared against the Quantpedia universe of documented quantitative strategies. The workflow identifies nearest neighbours, assigns the strategy to a factor cluster, calculates a uniqueness score, and produces a research robustness report that can be used for deeper validation. The goal is not to replace human research judgment. The goal is to create a structured robustness checker that helps researchers understand whether a new strategy is truly differentiated, redundant with known effects, or simply a variation of an existing Quantpedia strategy profile.
source

The learning process relies on neural networks, as they learn portfolio weights directly from a full historical panel of macroeconomic divergence factors and return data. They do not require stock-by-stock theoretical priors for model construction. […] The post Portfolio optimization with macro factors and neural networks appeared first on Macrosynergy .

Getting the Target Right in Return Prediction

수익률 대신 순위를 예측하면 성과가 크게 좋아진다

Training models on stock ranks beats training on raw returns.

Cyril Dujava

  • 머신러닝 연구는 모델 구조에만 매달려 왔다
  • 학습 목표를 원시 수익률에서 순위로 바꿨다
  • 대형주에서 수익률과 샤프지수가 약 두 배로 늘었다
방법
동일한 예측 모델을 두고 학습 목표만 원시 수익률과 횡단면 순위로 나눠 비교한다.
데이터
Cakici와 Zaremba가 주식 시장 자료로 검증했지만, 초록은 표본 기간과 시장을 밝히지 않는다.
시사점
기존 수익률 예측 모형의 라벨을 횡단면 순위로 바꿔 재학습하고 성과 차이를 직접 확인한다.
적용도 3/5
가격 자료만 있으면 순위 라벨을 바로 만들 수 있어 데이터 확보는 쉽지만, 모델과 매매 규칙은 직접 설계해야 해 3점이다.
  • ML research chases architectures, not the prediction target
  • Cakici and Zaremba swap raw returns for cross-sectional ranks
  • Large-cap returns and Sharpe ratios roughly double
Method
Hold the model fixed and vary only the training label: raw return level versus cross-sectional rank.
Data
Equity universes including large caps; the abstract names no sample period or market.
Use
Relabel an existing return-prediction model with cross-sectional ranks and measure the portfolio gap.
Score 3/5
Rank labels come free from price data, but you still build the model, universe and rebalancing rules yourself — hence 3.
return predictionmachine learningcross-sectional rankstarget transformationequity long-shortSharpe ratio
ORIGINAL ABSTRACTRecent interesting research from Cakici and Zaremba, highlights an often-overlooked aspect of machine learning for equity return prediction: the choice of prediction target. Rather than focusing on increasingly sophisticated model architectures or feature engineering, the authors show that how returns are represented during training has a much larger impact on predictive performance. In particular, models trained to predict stock ranks instead of raw return levels generate substantially stronger portfolio performance—roughly doubling both returns and Sharpe ratios in large-cap universes.

  • 단일 구간 샤프비율은 우연과 실력을 못 가른다
  • 겹치는 lookback 구간마다 샤프비율을 계산한다

  • 팩터 선별과 전략의 상업성 판단에 쓴다

  • A single-window Sharpe cannot separate luck from skill

  • Divide the mean Sharpe by its standard error
  • Use the score to pick factors and judge viability
Method
Mean Sharpe ratio across overlapping lookback windows, divided by its estimated standard error.
Data
Theory and code post; ships with a Jupyter notebook.
Use
Use it as a second-stage filter on factor candidates before trusting any backtest Sharpe.
Score 3/5
Scored 3: the input data is your own strategy PnL, so it is trivially available, but this is a validation metric, not a tradable signal.

  • Macro divergence factors become stock-level relative return signals.

Data
Abstract names no dataset or test window; it only links two notebooks.
